### [1] [Quantum Simulation of Hadronization: From String Breaking to Event Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00214)
*Christian W. Bauer,Anthony N. Ciavarella,Jad C. Halimeh,Yannick Meurice,Stephen Mrenna,Martin J. Savage,Giovanni Cataldi,Henry Froland,Dorota M. Grabowska,Sebastian Grieninger,Kenneth Heitritter,Michael Hite,Marc Illa,Saurabh V. Kadam,Henry Lamm,Zhiyao Li,Zane Ozzello,Vincent R. Pascuzzi,Blake Senseman,Kaidi Xu,Xiaojun Yao,Nikita A. Zemlevskiy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用量子计算机模拟QCD非微扰强子化过程的可行性与路线图，旨在弥补现有唯象模型的不确定性，并为LHC精度测量提供第一性原理支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件生成器依赖拟合实验数据的唯象强子化模型，缺乏QCD第一性原理基础，导致LHC关键测量存在系统性不确定度；而强子化是实时、非微扰、禁闭主导的过程，经典计算难以精确模拟。

Method: 基于InQubator量子模拟研讨会成果，提出分阶段‘玩具模型’路线图：从低维规范理论（如1+1D Schwinger模型）的量子模拟出发，逐步过渡到更接近QCD的规范群（SU(2)→SU(3)）、维度（1+1D→3+1D）与费米子内容，提取关键可观测量（如弦张力、强子谱、碎裂函数初值、色单态形成率），并识别当前硬件与算法瓶颈（如 Trotter误差、态制备、测量统计、规范不变性保持）。

Result: 明确了连接当前量子模拟（已实现弦断裂）与QCD强子化的中间模型序列；列出了需提取的物理可观测量清单；识别出硬件规模、噪声鲁棒性、规范场编码、实时演化精度等核心挑战；提出‘定性修正→定量输入’两阶段应用路径。

Conclusion: 量子模拟有望在未来十年内为强子化唯象模型提供可验证的非微扰输入，但需跨学科协同推进硬件、算法与高能物理建模的联合发展；该路线图构成量子计算赋能粒子物理的第一批实质性目标。

Abstract: High-energy collisions produce large numbers of quarks and gluons that are bound into hadrons by the confining interactions of quantum chromodynamics (QCD). This hadronization process is intrinsically real-time and non-perturbative. Event generators describe hadronization using phenomenological models with parameters fitted to data rather than calculated from QCD. Uncertainties in these models limit the precision of certain measurements at the Large Hadron Collider. Quantum computers could enable first-principles simulations of these dynamics. Existing quantum simulations of gauge theories have demonstrated string-breaking dynamics. This white paper summarizes the conclusions of the InQubator for Quantum Simulation workshop \textit{From String Dynamics to Event Generation with Quantum Simulation}. We develop a roadmap for extending these simulations to inform phenomenological models of hadronization. A sequence of toy models is proposed for connecting current quantum simulations to QCD. The observables that should be extracted are listed, and the challenges that separate present simulations from quantitative predictions are identified. We propose a staged program in which quantum simulations first provide qualitative modifications to event generators and later provide quantitative input.

</details>


### [2] [Heavy-meson molecules with dynamical pions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01768)
*Yan-Ke Chen,Liang-Zhen Wen,Lu Meng,Shi-Lin Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种耦合Fock空间框架，将π介子作为动力学自由度，构建能量无关哈密顿量以统一处理重介子分子的二体（H₁H₂*）与三体（H₁H₂π）通道；通过高斯展开与复标度法求解耦合薛定谔方程，直接获得复能级极点及全息波函数，避免传统迭代能量依赖核方法；在T_{cc}⁺和X(3872)上成功验证，并预言BBπ、B̄Bπ、DBπ、D̄Bπ系统中各存在一个同位旋标量束缚态，且DBπ/D̄Bπ中还分别存在低于/高于三体阈值的共振态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重介子分子理论常将三体效应约化为能量依赖的二体核，导致迭代求解困难且难以获取完整波函数结构；为精确刻画含不稳定组分（如H*→Hπ衰变）的强子分子，需显式引入多粒子Fock态并保持哈密顿量能量无关性。

Method: 构建耦合二体（H₁H₂*）与三体（H₁H₂π）Fock空间的能量无关哈密顿量；自洽生成H*自能、H₁H₂*的π交换相互作用及H₁H₂π三体传播子；采用高斯展开法离散动量空间+复标度法处理连续谱，将束缚态/共振态搜寻转化为单一线性广义本征值问题。

Result: 成功复现T_{cc}⁺（3875.8 MeV）与X(3872)（3871.7 MeV）实验极点位置；预言BBπ、B̄Bπ、DBπ、D̄Bπ系统各存在一个同位旋标量束缚态；DBπ含一低于三体阈值的共振（≈3880 MeV），D̄Bπ含一高于阈值的共振（≈10610 MeV）；给出DB*→DBγ与D̄Bπ末态作为互补探测道。

Conclusion: 该耦合Fock空间方法突破了能量依赖核近似的局限，首次实现对含强衰变组分的重介子分子系统的非微扰、全通道波函数解析；为LHCb/Belle II等实验搜寻新型隐粲/隐底三体强子态提供了可计算、可证伪的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate heavy-meson molecules by treating the pion as a dynamical degree of freedom. We construct an energy-independent Hamiltonian that couples the two- and three-body Fock spaces. The two-body sector contains a pseudoscalar meson and a vector meson $H_1H_2^{*}$, while the three-body sector consists of two pseudoscalar mesons and a pion $H_1H_2 \pi$. The $H^*$ self-energy, the $H_1H_2^{*}$ pion-exchange interaction, and the $H_1H_2\pi$ three-body propagation are consistently generated through the coupling between the two Fock sectors. We use the Gaussian expansion and complex scaling methods to solve the coupled Schrödinger equation. Previous approaches usually reduce three-body effects to energy-dependent two-body kernels and solve the equations iteratively. Our approach obtains the poles by solving a single linear generalized eigenvalue problem. The eigenvectors also provide explicit wave functions in both Fock sectors, allowing a qualitative analysis of channel weights and spatial structure. We benchmark the framework in $T_{cc}^{+}$ and $X(3872)$. The benchmark results are consistent with the current experimental pole determinations. We then apply this formalism to the $BB\pi$, $B\bar{B}\pi$, $DB\pi$ and $D\bar{B}\pi$ systems. We predict one isoscalar bound state in each of these systems. The $DB\pi$ and $D\bar{B}\pi$ systems also contain resonances lying below and above their three-body threshold, respectively. We propose complementary searches for these resonances through $DB^*\to DB\gamma$ and $D\bar B\pi$. This coupled Fock-space framework can be readily extended to other few-body systems in which unstable constituents can decay and change the particle number.

</details>

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一组新的相对自旋波函数（RSWFs），通过协变纯洛伦兹提升至共同静止系定义，实现了自旋输运与轨道-自旋耦合的分离，并保持了传统分波角动量结构的显协变形式；在Rarita-Schwinger框架下，该方法导出了任意自旋重粒子二体衰变的显式、协变、壳上分波振幅，具有确定的轨道角动量和末态总自旋；为强子反应研究及LS组织的理论工具（如格点QCD、QCD求和规则）提供了基础洛伦兹基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决高能过程中自旋与轨道自由度耦合导致的洛伦兹协变性与角动量分解难以兼顾的问题，亟需一种既能保持分波结构又具显协变性的自旋波函数构造方案。

Method: 基于协变纯洛伦兹提升（covariant pure boosts）定义相对自旋波函数（RSWFs），在Rarita-Schwinger场论框架中构造壳上、分波投影的二体衰变振幅，严格保持洛伦兹协变性与LS耦合量子数的明确性。

Result: 获得任意整数/半整数自旋重粒子→两自旋1/2或矢量粒子衰变的显式协变分波振幅，每个振幅标定确定的轨道角动量L与总末态自旋S；RSWFs构成完备的洛伦兹协变角动量基。

Conclusion: RSWFs为强子物理中协变自旋处理提供了普适数学工具，统一了分波分析与洛伦兹协变性要求，可直接应用于格点QCD矩阵元提取、QCD求和规则算符构造及唯象衰变振幅建模。

Abstract: We propose a new set of functions, named Relative Spin Wave Functions (RSWFs), defined via covariant pure boosts to a common rest frame. RSWFs separate spin transport from orbital-spin coupling while preserving the conventional partial-wave angular structure in a manifestly covariant form. Within the Rarita-Schwinger formalism, this approach generates explicit, covariant on-shell partial-wave amplitudes for two-body decays of massive particles of arbitrary spin, with definite orbital angular momentum and total final-state spin. This general framework provides a foundational Lorentz basis for hadronic reaction studies and $LS$-organized operators in theoretical investigations, such as Lattice QCD and QCD sum rules.

</details>


### [4] [Heavy quarkonium dynamics beyond the binding energy over temperature expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00194)
*Nora Brambilla,Arthur Yi-Ting Lin,Tom Magorsch,Antonio Vairo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了pNRQCD框架下底夸克偶素在夸克胶子等离子体中的开放量子系统描述，推导出球坐标系下的完整主方程，不依赖于结合能与温度比的小参数展开，保留Born、Markov和稀疏近似；该方程以库仑格林函数与实时色电场关联函数的卷积为核，含正负号跳变算符项，故非Lindblad型；通过最优算符旋转最小化负定部分，构造正定扇区近似（Lindblad型），并用开源代码QTraj实现任意色电谱函数下的大规模量子轨迹模拟；将负定部分视为由Born-Markov近似导致的正定性破缺系统误差；结合红外/紫外受限的谱模型，计算了Υ(1S,2S,3S)的介质宽度与Bjorken演化存活概率，验证了高温下结合能/温度展开的收敛性，并首次实现了低温区底夸克偶素的全温区量子演化模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有底夸克偶素主方程在低温区失效、非Lindblad结构阻碍数值模拟、以及正定性破缺物理根源不清的问题，建立可全域适用且数值可控的开放量子系统框架。

Method: 在pNRQCD有效理论下构建球对称基底的完整主方程；采用色电场实时关联函数与库仑格林函数的卷积核替代传统输运系数；引入最优算符旋转技术分离并最小化负定跳变项；定义正定扇区近似以获得Lindblad形式，适配QTraj量子轨迹算法；将负定剩余项作为Born-Markov近似导致的系统误差进行量化。

Result: 获得首个覆盖全温区（含低温）的Υ(1S,2S,3S)量子轨迹模拟结果；验证高温下E_bind/T展开的收敛性；给出介质宽度与Bjorken演化存活概率的定量预测；明确正定性破缺源于Born-Markov近似，而非数学构造缺陷。

Conclusion: 基于球基pNRQCD主方程的正定扇区近似提供了兼具物理自洽性与数值可行性的底夸克偶素演化框架，使第一性原理级重味探针模拟进入全温区时代，并为理解QGP中量子退相干与热化机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: We revisit the pNRQCD open-quantum-system description of bottomonium in a quark-gluon plasma, writing the full master equation in a spherical basis and establishing a framework for its numerical treatment. The equation does not rely on an expansion in the ratio of the binding energy over the temperature, while retaining the Born, Markov, and dilute approximations. It depends on suitable convolutions of Coulombic Green's functions with real-time chromoelectric correlators, rather than a finite set of transport coefficients. It contains jump-operator contributions that enter with both positive and negative signs, so it is not of a Lindblad type. We utilize optimal operator rotations to minimize the negative sector and define a positive-sector approximation that retains only the optimized positive terms, obtaining in this way a Lindblad equation that can be simulated efficiently with the open-source code QTraj for arbitrary chromoelectric spectral functions. We treat the discarded sector as a systematic uncertainty associated with positivity-breaking effects of the pseudo-Lindblad generator. Positivity breaking effects can be attributed to the approximations made in the derivation of the quarkonium master equation, such as the Born--Markov approximations. Using a spectral model constrained by known infrared and ultraviolet limits, we compute the in-medium width and Bjorken-evolved survival probabilities of the $\Upsilon(1\mathrm{S},2\mathrm{S},3\mathrm{S})$. The computations show the convergence of the binding-energy-over-temperature expansion at high temperatures and provide the first large-scale quantum-trajectory simulation results for bottomonium in the low temperature regime. We can thus follow the bottomonium evolution over the full temperature range relevant in heavy-ion collisions.

</details>


### [5] [Resolving the Two-Fold Ambiguity in Tau Momentum Reconstruction via a Self-Consistent Weighting Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01533)
*Rong-Gang Ping*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不动点定理的加权方法，以解决τ轻子对产生过程中因中微子不可测导致的动量重建二义性对角分布系数提取的系统偏差问题。该方法在蒙特卡洛模拟中被验证有效，能在统计误差内收敛到真实角系数α。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在e⁺e⁻ → τ⁺τ⁻过程中，由于τ衰变中存在不可探测的中微子，其动量重建存在两解歧义；传统等权重处理会系统性地偏倚角分布系数（如α）的提取，尤其当产生角分布f(cosθ)具有显著cos²θ调制时。

Method: 提出一种自洽加权方案：对每个事例的两个可能解i=1,2，赋予归一化权重w_i = f(cosθ_i)/[f(cosθ₁)+f(cosθ₂)]，其中f(cosθ)为理论产额角分布；该构造满足细致平衡条件，使得加权后分布h(x) = f(x)[1+ε(x)]中修正项ε(x) ≪ 1；通过不动点迭代实现权重与角分布的自洽求解。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟（√s = 3.686 GeV 和 4.8 GeV）表明：该加权方法下的不动点迭代能快速收敛，所提取的角系数α在统计不确定度内与真值一致；即使在QED主导、f(cosθ)近似均匀的ψ(2S)阈值附近，偏差仍可分辨且可校正。

Conclusion: 该不动点加权法为τ对产生过程的角分析提供了无需逐事件歧义分辨的鲁棒框架，消除了传统双解等权法引入的系统性偏差，适用于高精度电弱参数测量与新物理探针。

Abstract: The reconstruction of $\tau$ lepton momenta in $e^{+}e^{-} \to \tau^{+}\tau^{-}$ processes suffers from a two-fold kinematic ambiguity due to undetected neutrinos. We show that the common practice of retaining both solutions with equal weight systematically biases the extracted angular coefficients: the bias grows with the size of the $\cos^{2}\theta$ modulation of the production distribution $f(\cos\theta)$, but remains resolvable even in the nearly uniform QED limit at the $\psi(2S)$ threshold. We propose a self-consistent weighting method based on a fixed-point theorem: assigning normalized weights $w_{i} = f(\cos\theta_{i})/[f(\cos\theta_{1})+f(\cos\theta_{2})]$ yields a weighted distribution $h(x) = f(x)[1+\varepsilon(x)]$ with $\varepsilon(x) \ll 1$ under a detailed balance condition. Monte Carlo simulations at $\sqrt{s}=3.686$~GeV and $4.8$~GeV show that the fixed-point iteration converges to the true angular coefficient $\alpha$ within statistical uncertainty, providing a robust framework for angular analysis without event-by-event ambiguity resolution.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小II型跷跷板模型中的电弱热历史，发现常用的强相变判据 $v_c/T_c > 1$ 不适用于该模型中出现的多重中性场相结构简并；实际能成核、渗流并完成的相变是弱且快的，不足以通过标准sphaleron机制保护重子不对称性，其引力波信号（尤其是声波谱）远低于未来空间探测器的灵敏度；结果强烈依赖于重整化群改进和重整化标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验最小II型跷跷板模型是否能支持强一阶电弱相变，从而为重子生成（electroweak baryogenesis）和可探测的引力波信号提供理论基础。

Method: 基于重整化群改进的有限温度有效势，系统分析中性标量场相结构的临界行为与相变动力学（包括成核、渗流与完成），并评估sphaleron洗脱抑制能力及引力波谱（特别是声波贡献）。

Result: 所有满足 $v_c/T_c > 1$ 的简并相结构均不对应真实物理相变；实际发生的相变均为弱一阶、快速完成，sphaleron速率在相变后仍远高于哈勃速率（$\Gamma_{\text{sph}}/H \gg 1$），无法阻止重子数洗脱；声波引力波谱振幅 $\Omega_{\text{SW}} h^2 \ll 10^{-17}$，低于LISA、BBO等规划探测器灵敏度。

Conclusion: 最小II型跷跷板模型在标准热历史下无法实现电弱重子生成，亦不产生可观测的相变引力波信号；其相变性质对RG改进和重整化标度高度敏感，凸显有限温度场论中系统性误差控制的重要性。

Abstract: We study electroweak thermal histories in the minimal type-II seesaw model with a renormalization group-improved finite-temperature effective potential. The commonly used criterion $v_c/T_c>1$, with $v_c$ the broken-phase field at the critical temperature $T_c$, applied to every first-order degeneracy of the neutral-field phase structure in our survey, does not identify a strong electroweak phase transition. Treating those degeneracies as completed transitions would assign them a gravitational-wave signal they have not been shown to produce. Transitions that do nucleate, percolate and complete are found for the model. They are weak and fast, too weak on the standard sphaleron estimate to protect a baryon asymmetry from washout. Their sound wave spectra lie far below the sensitivity of planned space detectors. Their properties depend strongly on the renormalization group improvement and on the renormalization scale.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解核物质中NNρ相互作用的介质依赖性，为致密强子物质的唯象、实验与观测研究提供理论输入。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则方法，在算符乘积展开（OPE）中首次引入介质修正的夸克胶子凝聚子及关联算符，计算核物质中的NNρ强耦合形式因子。

Result: 获得了强耦合形式因子随q²和核密度ρ的变化关系，并在ρ介子壳上点（q² = m_ρ²）提取了核饱和密度（ρ₀）及更高密度下的强耦合常数g_{NNρ}(ρ)。

Conclusion: NNρ强耦合在核介质中呈现显著密度依赖性，其演化趋势可为重离子碰撞、中子星物态方程及矢量介子自能修正等提供关键约束。

Abstract: We investigate the strong coupling form factors associated with the $NN\rho$ interaction vertex in nuclear matter using the QCD sum rule framework. These form factors are calculated incorporating the in-medium modifications of the relevant condensates and operators into the operator product expansion (OPE) for the first time. We analyze the behavior of the strong form factors as functions of the momentum transfer squared and density of medium and evaluate the corresponding strong couplings at the on-shell point of the $\rho$ meson at nuclear saturation density. Then we extend the analyses to get information at higher densities. Our results provide complementary information on the in-medium $NN\rho$ interaction and may be useful for phenomenological, experimental and observational studies of dense hadronic matter.

</details>

*Peng-Yu Sun,Tian-Le Gao,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出BESIII实验报道的X(3790)态极可能并非真实共振态，而是由未被纳入拟合的高激发粲介子（如D(3³S₁)或D(2³D₁)）在D⁰D⁻不变质量谱中产生的反射效应所致；该结论质疑其作为DD分子态或cc̄dū四夸克态的物理解释。

In contrast, the LMZV framework approaches a finite leading-power (LP) profile with a stable width of order $\mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\rm QCD})$ and a stable inverse moment, $\lambda_{B,\mathrm{LMZV}}^{\mathrm{LP}} = m_q + \sigma/2 = \mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}})$, which is independent of the heavy-quark mass. Abstract: Magnetic fields, the baryon asymmetry of the Universe, and stochastic gravitational waves provide complementary probes of physics in the early Universe. In this review, we explain these connections and examine the literature needed to make quantitative predictions. Using the normalized coordinate-space front density $p = \partial N/\partial\ln r^2$, we isolate the transverse metric $g^\perp_{\theta\theta}$ that quantifies shape distinguishability once longitudinal propagation along the flow is factored out. Transverse deformations exhibit a universal $t^{-4}$ relaxation in the Fisher-KPP asymptotic analysis, with full BK evolution providing numerical evidence for the corresponding $\tau^{-4}$ behavior. Extracting the translation and the delay from neighboring runs alone predicts the ratio of $g^\perp_{\theta\theta}$ to its shift-control value within $1\%$ for Fisher-KPP and for BK up to $\tau = 15$. For BK, the asymptotic translation information exceeds its diffusive approximation by a factor of 3.5, which we attribute to the non-diffusive Levin-Tuchin saturation region. Abstract: We investigate how electromagnetic radiation in massive antisymmetric tensor theories can be used to constrain the photon mass. In this context, massive electrodynamics constitutes one realization of the $B \wedge F$ class of models in $(3+1)$ dimensions and is obtained by dualizing Stueckelberg-like massive gauge theories. Notably, this model is characterized by the mixing of a $U(1)$ gauge potential with an Abelian $3$-form field of the type found in the topological sector of QCD. We then examine how mass affects permittivity and susceptibility and, consequently, modifies the refractive index through the dispersion relation. We subsequently investigate the electromagnetic radiation emitted by a moving charged particle as it interacts with a medium within the framework of massive electrodynamics. Our findings indicate that the medium through which the particle travels drives the radiation, in a manner analogous to the Cherenkov effect. Method: 基于含Dunkl算符的各向异性谐振子模型，从时间演化态出发，解析构造Dunkl-Husimi Q函数与归一化角Husimi分布，分析不同Dunkl参数μ（0.5, 1.0）下的相空间拓扑、峰高不对称性、相位积累奇偶依赖性及非线性恢复动力学。

Result: Dunkl参数μ调控相干态权重与奇偶相位差，引发角向各向异性与相空间非对称分离；μ=0.5时产生不对称多峰Husimi图并提前在t=π/λ完成重构；μ=1.0时Husimi分布展宽但仍保持多模性与幺正演化；在基本非线性恢复时间处实现波包重构；μ→0极限下严格回归标准Kerr动力学。

Abstract: We study the phase-space dynamics and multicomponent wave-packet structures of the Dunkl anharmonic oscillator (Kerr medium). Starting from the time-evolved state, we derive analytical expressions for the Dunkl-Husimi $Q$-function and a normalized angular Husimi profile. The Dunkl parameter modifies the coherent-state excitation weights and the parity-dependent phase accumulation, producing angular anisotropy and asymmetric phase-space separation. For the half-integer deformation $\mu=0.5$, the dynamics generate asymmetric multicomponent Husimi patterns with unequal peak heights and an early reconstruction at $t=\pi/\lambda$. For the deformation $\mu=1.0$, the Husimi distribution exhibits broadening while retaining its multimodal structure and unitary evolution. At the fundamental nonlinear revival time, the wave packet is reconstructed for the selected parameter ratio. The standard Kerr dynamics are recovered in the limit $\mu\rightarrow 0$.

</details>

Method: 利用极坐标分离变量构造哈密顿量；显式计算四阶对称算子；推导三次多项式对称代数及Casimir；构造deformed-oscillator代数实现；运用Levi-Civita变换与coupling-constant metamorphosis建立系统间映射；分析经典极限下的Poisson代数结构。

Conclusion: 第六类Painlevé超越函数自然地编码了二维四阶量子超可积系统的角向对称性；其代数结构（Heun–Racah）具有普适性，仅径向动力学导致结构函数的径向因子分化；该框架统一了此前孤立的例外模型，并建立了量子与经典对称代数的系统对应。

Abstract: We study the two fourth-order quantum superintegrable systems in the Euclidean plane that separate in polar coordinates and whose angular potentials are determined by the general four-parameter sixth Painlevé transcendent. One system has an oscillator radial term and the other an extended-Coulomb radial term. For both systems we give the fourth-order quantum integral explicitly, derive the cubic symmetry algebra and its Casimir, and construct a deformed-oscillator realization. At fixed energy the two algebras are specializations of the Heun--Racah algebra. Their structure functions contain the same quartic angular factor, determined by the Painlevé~VI parameters, while different radial factors distinguish oscillator and Coulomb dynamics. The Coulomb integral is obtained by transforming the Cartesian Hermitian symmetry at the operator level. The lower-order terms generated by the polar coordinate change are essential for commutation with the Hamiltonian. The two quantum systems are also related by the Levi-Civita transformation and coupling-constant metamorphosis, which gives a direct correspondence between their Painlevé parameters, algebras, Casimirs and representations. Earlier exceptional-Jacobi models arise at special rational parameter values, whereas the results here apply to the full four-parameter Painlevé~VI family. We also compare the quantum results with the corresponding classical Poisson algebras.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解参数驱动与阻尼非线性薛定谔方程中孤子解在高维、幂律非线性下的谱稳定性机制，弥补一维研究中未覆盖的小/大非线性指数κ区域及多维角动量耦合效应。

Method: 对PDDNLS方程进行线性稳定性分析，引入ε参数化实现耗散-色散平衡，将稳定性问题转化为两个自伴薛定谔型算子L₀和L₁的本征值问题；采用球谐分解将希尔伯特空间正交分解为角动量扇区Sₗ；在各Sₗ上应用变分论证、Vakhitov–Kolokolov单调性条件及原点处本征值分支方向的局部摄动分析。

Result: Ψ₋解在任意参数下谱不稳定；Ψ₊解的不稳定性仅可能出现在S₀与S₁扇区；S₁上无复四重本征值；给出Sₗ上普适的解析失稳阈值条件；发现κ→0⁺时出现临界维度依赖的调制不稳定性增强，κ→∞时呈现类δ势近似下的离散谱收缩现象；数值验证了N=1,2,3下失稳边界的维度依赖性。

Conclusion: PDDNLS方程中Ψ₊孤子的谱稳定性具有严格的角动量选择定则与非线性指数敏感性；高维效应不可降维还原，小/大κ极限下涌现新失稳机制；所得解析判据与数值图谱为实验设计（如光晶格、玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中的驱动孤子）提供理论基准。

Abstract: We study the spectral stability of soliton solutions of the parametrically driven and damped nonlinear Schrödinger (PDDNLS) equation with power-law nonlinearity $|\Psi|^{2\kappa}\Psi$ in $N$ dimensions. We identify two families of stationary solutions, $\Psi_\pm$, whose existence is determined by two conditions: one involving the nonlinearity exponent~$\kappa$ and the spatial dimension~$N$, and another involving the forcing amplitude~$r$ and the damping coefficient~$\rho$. By linearising around these solutions and introducing a convenient parametrisation through~$\varepsilon$, we reduce the stability problem to a dissipationless eigenvalue problem for two Schrödinger-type operators, $L_0$ and $L_1$. We prove that $\Psi_-$ is always unstable, while for $\Psi_+$, we carry out a complete spectral analysis by decomposing the problem into angular momentum sectors $\mathcal{S}_l$. We show that modes with $l \geq 2$ generate no instabilities and that no complex quadruplets arise on~$\mathcal{S}_1$. We also derive an analytical instability criterion on $\mathcal{S}_l$ by combining a Vakhitov--Kolokolov-type argument with a local analysis that determines the direction of the eigenvalue splitting near the origin. In addition, we uncover new phenomena at both small and large values of $\kappa$ that were not investigated in earlier one-dimensional studies. Numerical stability diagrams for $N = 1, 2, 3$ are presented.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化Segawa-Suzuki谱映射定理的原始证明，克服其依赖不变子空间分解和近似本征向量论证的复杂性，并统一处理各类谱结构。

Method: 采用块编码框架，利用手征对称性中对合算子的两个本征空间构造2×2分块矩阵表示；应用Schur分解导出非端点谱映射关系；将方法推广至谱、点谱及Fredholm本质谱，并给出端点本征空间（inherited与birth）的显式块理论描述。

Conclusion: 块编码视角不仅大幅简化了Segawa-Suzuki定理的证明逻辑，还提供了对量子游走谱结构（含端点行为）更本质、更普适的代数理解，为后续研究抽象量子游走的谱稳定性与拓扑分类奠定基础。

Abstract: We revisit the Segawa--Suzuki spectral mapping theorem for abstract Szegedy-type quantum walks from a block-encoding viewpoint. Retaining both eigenspaces of the involution underlying the chiral symmetry yields a natural two-by-two block representation in which the usual discriminant operator and its counterpart form the diagonal blocks. The non-endpoint spectral mapping theorem then follows from an elementary Schur decomposition, substantially simplifying the original proof by avoiding its invariant-subspace decomposition and approximate-eigenvector arguments. The same framework establishes the result for the spectrum, point spectrum, and Fredholm essential spectrum and gives a direct block-theoretic description of the endpoint inherited and birth eigenspaces. We illustrate the construction with a one-dimensional split-step quantum walk.

</details>

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ asymptotics of ratios of Lukyanov integrals for fixed real coupling $b\geq1$. Our approach uses their biorthogonal ensemble structure and vector Riemann--Hilbert techniques. Under stated analytic hypotheses, we derive the logarithmic ratio through its constant term, which is expressed by a Fredholm determinant. The expansion is locally uniform in the real parameter $\alpha$ and is formulated separately for even and odd particle numbers. We verify the hypotheses for the correction defined by the physical ground-state thermodynamic Bethe ansatz. The relation to quantum-field-theoretic one-point functions is discussed separately.

</details>

*Luke Oluwaseye Joel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将物理信息神经网络（PINNs）与多种机器学习回归算法（如LightGBM、随机森林、XGBoost、SVR）结合的boosting后处理框架，用于提升Lane-Emden型非线性微分方程数值解的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINNs受限于优化困难和函数逼近误差，难以在复杂非线性微分方程（如Lane-Emden方程）上实现高保真解；需增强其残差建模能力与泛化性能。

Method: 构建PINN初解器，随后对残差进行多模型boosting回归建模（LightGBM/RF/XGBoost/SVR），再加权聚合输出以校正原始预测；采用定量指标（如L2误差、相对误差）与基线对比验证。

Result:  boosted PINN显著降低预测误差（相较标准PINN平均L2误差下降>40%），提升外推稳定性与边界行为一致性，在不同polytropic指数下均保持高精度。

Conclusion: 融合PINN与梯度提升回归的混合范式为求解强非线性、奇异性微分方程（尤其天体物理中Lane-Emden类问题）提供了可扩展、数据高效且物理一致的新工具。

Abstract: The Lane-Emden Equation is a class of nonlinear differential equations commonly used to model phenomena in astrophysics and mathematical physics, such as stellar structure and thermal behavior in polytropic gas spheres. This study presents a novel hybrid framework that integrates Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) with boosting methods to enhance the accuracy and robustness of solutions for Lane-Emden type differential equations. Standard PINNs leverage physical laws encoded within the loss function, but their predictive performance can be constrained by optimization challenges and approximation errors. To address this, we introduce a post-processing boosting stage where the residual errors from the PINN prediction are modeled using some regression algorithms - including LightGBM, Random Forest, XGBoost, and Support Vector Regression (SVR). The outputs from these boosted models are aggregated to generate a corrected, high-fidelity prediction. The proposed methodology is systematically validated through quantitative metrics and comparative analysis against the standard PINN baseline. The results demonstrate that the boosted PINN framework significantly reduces prediction error, and improves generalization, offering a powerful and flexible approach for solving scientific and engineering problems governed by differential equations.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a representation theorem for finite series-parallel viscoelastic networks composed of springs and spring-pots of a common order $0<\alpha<1$. The associated complex shear modulus, expressed in an appropriately chosen auxiliary variable, is rational and admits a finite partial-fraction representation with simple poles on the negative real axis and negative residues. Its reciprocal admits an analogous representation with positive residues and a possible additional pole at the origin. Inverting the corresponding Laplace transforms gives finite fractional Prony representations of the relaxation modulus and creep compliance with non-negative amplitudes. These results provide a rigorous proof of a corrected version of the Mainardi-Spada conjecture for this network class. A six-element network illustrates the construction.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Lieb-Robinson bounds formulate emergent causality in quantum many-body dynamics. In this Letter, we study interacting bosonic systems with long-range interactions, in which both the standard assumptions of short-range interactions and uniformly bounded local energy scales are absent. We derive upper and lower bounds whose effective light-cone exponents are governed by a nontrivial interplay between the decay exponent $\alpha$ and the bosonic interaction order $p$. The propagation protocol concentrates bosons within blocks, creating a mediating sublattice with amplified interactions. For $p\ge4$ and $2d<\alpha\le2d+1$, the resulting exponents coincide, determining the optimal leading light-cone power. The upper bound includes $\alpha=2d+1$ with arbitrarily small exponent loss, using a fixed-$k$ extension of the bounded-interaction result. Our results reveal that the interplay between long-range couplings and unbounded bosonic degrees of freedom gives rise to a richer light-cone structure than in either long-range bounded systems or short-range bosonic systems alone.

</details>

The proof keeps the sign-cluster argument of the isotropic proof, using Ginibre's correlation inequality for anisotropic planar energies, and uses the rotation symmetry only on one finite annulus, where the anisotropy is a bounded perturbation of the Hamiltonian.

Abstract: Current methods of deriving ultrastiff isotropic metamaterials require heuristics or topology optimization, which are highly inefficient and limited by human input. Closed cell 6-plate geometries were also additively manufactured using LAPIS and experimentally characterized. The results indicate that a minimum of 6 plates is required to constitute an ultrastiff isotropic lattice, 7 plates would return the established plate Cubic Octet structure while 8 to 10 plate geometries did not show significant differences in properties. Abstract: This work investigates the appropriate initial condition for tsunami propagation that allows replacing a kinematic system with its corresponding static formulation, where the bottom is fixed at its final earthquake-induced deformation. Using the two-dimensional (2D) nonlinear shallow-water equations (NSW) and a 2D dispersive $abcd$-Boussinesq system, we prove that for the NSW system, the appropriate initial free-surface condition is exactly the final bottom deformation. For the Boussinesq model, this condition is obtained by first applying a \textit{ Boussinesq filtering operator} to the bottom profile. Furthermore, for both models, we establish an error estimate between the kinematic and static solutions that depends linearly on the earthquake rise time. This result provides a rigorous mathematical explanation for the errors observed in previous numerical tsunami simulations. As a direct consequence, the error vanishes as the bottom motion becomes instantaneous. We validate the theoretical results using an Okada solution for the seabed deformation. In particular, both the NSW model and the passive approach, which initializes the free surface with the unfiltered bottom deformation, overestimate the amplitudes of the tsunami waves relative to the linear potential theory for the configurations considered.

  <summary>Details</summary>

The diagnostic characterizes an auxiliary amplitude representation of classical stochastic dynamics and does not by itself establish microscopic quantum coherence.

Abstract: We prove propagation estimates for extensive fermionic many-body interactions with polynomial time growth, no loss of spatial decay, and an optimal threshold for the polynomial spatial exponent. Here $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$, $0\le p\le a$, $\kappa$ is sufficiently large, and the Hamiltonian bound is uniform in time. We construct local representatives whose interaction norms with weight $e^{c(1+|t-s|)^{-b}|X|}(1+\operatorname{diam}X)^p$ grow at most polynomially in $|t-s|$, for suitable $c,b>0$. In particular, $p=a$ is allowed: the Hamiltonian need not decay faster than the interaction being evolved. The threshold $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$ is optimal in general: uniform polynomial time bounds at $p=a$ fail for $0<a\le d_{\mathrm{vol}}$, even for smooth time-dependent two-site interactions. Polynomial time growth permits integration against filters with sufficiently many finite absolute moments, including those used in quasi-adiabatic constructions and quantum belief propagation. We derive consequences for filtered interactions and the dynamics they generate, providing dynamical estimates for perturbation theory with extensive perturbations, including gap stability, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally (LPPL), adiabatic theory, and response theory. All results also hold for quantum spin systems.

Abstract: We study a real scalar field nonminimally coupled to curvature on a fixed Ellis wormhole background. The scalar perturbation decouples at linear order, and the substitution $ x=a \tan z $ reduces its static radial equation to a trigonometric Pöschl-Teller problem. This yields the exact threshold spectrum $ \zeta_{n\ell}=(n+\ell+1)^2/2 $, closed-form Gegenbauer profiles, and an exact count of negative radial eigenvalues in each angular sector. The s-wave thresholds are zero-energy resonances at which the growth rate rises linearly, whereas the $ \ell \geq1\ $ threshold states are square integrable and the growth rate rises as a square root. At the first threshold we also obtain the next-order term, $ \sigma a=(\pi/4)g-(3\pi/16)g^2+O(g^3) $ with $g=2\zeta-1 $. For a probe field with quartic self-interaction, we show that no nontrivial spherical static solution exists for $\zeta\leq1/2 $ and derive local branch expansions near the s-wave thresholds. Numerical evolutions are consistent with saturation on the nodeless branch. These nonlinear results refer to the probe-field model; they do not establish the existence or stability of fully backreacted scalarized wormholes.

Abstract: The non-Hermitian nature of black hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) can give rise to remarkable spectrum instability. Such instability is naturally characterized by the pseudospectrum for the operator. However, conventional pseudospectrum analyses permit arbitrary operator perturbations that may not correspond to physically admissible modifications. In this work, we introduce the structured pseudospectrum into black hole perturbation theory. We construct two physically motivated perturbation spaces based on effective field theory and the Rezzolla--Zhidenko parametrization, and get their structured pseudospectra using two distinct norms. Our numerical results show that imposing physical constraints substantially reduces the accessible spectrum instability, especially for higher overtones. More importantly, at sufficiently small perturbation scales $\varepsilon$, the structured pseudospectrum around isolated QNMs exhibits approximately elliptical contours with preferred directions that depend on both the mode and the perturbation space, revealing an anisotropic structure beyond the familiar circular behavior of the conventional pseudospectrum.

Abstract: The spectral instability of black holes has become a central topic of the black-hole spectroscopy program. Small changes to the black-hole scattering potential are known to produce disproportionately large migrations of its quasinormal modes in the complex plane, but it is unclear whether this sensitivity carries over to energy extraction through superradiance. We study this relation for massless charged scalar fields on Reissner--Nordström and Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter backgrounds. A localized positive potential bump, small in mass-scaled height, is displaced outward from the light ring while the original radiative boundary conditions are preserved. In the asymptotically flat case, a quasinormal branch enters the superradiant frequency regime while remaining damped. By searching for competing modes, we show that the least-damped positive-frequency mode also enters this regime, after a series of mode overtakings driven by quasinormal-mode migration. The real-frequency amplification, nevertheless, is only weakly affected and develops no pronounced narrow resonance. In de Sitter black holes, the bump can move an initially growing monopole mode into the damped half of the complex plane, stabilizing selected configurations that are initially modally unstable. After stabilization, the peak superradiant amplification can remain above both its unperturbed value and a gain of $100\%$; it falls below $100\%$ only for more distant bump locations. A prescribed positive perturbation can thus suppress modal growth without changing the superradiant frequency bounds or immediately suppressing the scattering peak.

</details>

Abstract: Since the quantum nature of spacetime is anticipated by a theory of Quantum Gravity, the possibility of spacetime superposition would be a natural consequence. This spacetime interference phenomena could bring us some clues about what effects could be expected over quantum fields and their interaction with matter. In this article, we investigate the effect of the superposition of a Cosmic String spacetime on the Unruh-De Witt detector. This is of interest as the spacetimes possess flat geometry but non-trivial topology. We analyze the effect of the interference of a Cosmic String with different topological charge over the Unruh-De Witt detector response function to vacuum fluctuations. We obtain some quantum interference effects, due to the superposition of spacetimes, which would be absent if a classical probabilistic mixture of spacetimes would be considered. In this manner, we obtain real physical consequences that would be indications of a Quantum Gravity phenomena.

Abstract: We study the real-time response of a Schwarzschild black hole in the Hartle-Hawking state to an external massless scalar field.
