Abstract: The dimensionality of spacetime is a fundamental property of nature, yet whether our observed four-dimensional spacetime is embedded in a higher-dimensional geometry remains experimentally unresolved. Gravitational waves (GWs) offer a direct probe of compact extra dimensions through the strong-field dynamics of binary coalescences. In this work, we investigate extra-dimensional modifications to GW generation within a compactified Kaluza--Klein framework. We derive the Newtonian conservative binary dynamics and the leading-order extra-dimensional correction to the GW energy flux and formulate the corresponding waveform modifications within the parameterized post-Einsteinian (ppE) framework. Combining these results with the previously derived 1PN amplitude correction arising from perturbations of the internal volume, we construct modified GW waveform templates for ground- and space-based detectors. To alleviate parameter degeneracies in GW parameter estimation, we combine the observed strain data of GW170817 with independent electromagnetic observations in a multimessenger Bayesian analysis, yielding a 95\% credible upper bound of $n<0.11$ on the phenomenological dimensional parameter. We further investigate injected multimessenger massive black hole binary observations with Taiji, finding that the upper bound could be improved by nearly two orders of magnitude to $n<0.00178$. These results highlight the potential of multimessenger GW observations to probe extra-dimensional effects and test fundamental gravity in the strong-field and dynamical regime.

</details>


### [100] [Daemon sub Ponte: The Cost of a Second Black Hole (A Scholium on an ER=EPR Thought Experiment)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01194)
*Yen Chin Ong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在ER=EPR框架下，将老黑洞的霍金辐射收集并坍缩成第二个纠缠黑洞这一思想实验所需的外部能量资源；基于半经典信息容量限制，推导出外部能量的条件性下界及外部自由度关联性的互补约束，并区分熵层面可行性与精确/近似纯化的不同要求，辅以Page曲线分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ER=EPR假说中黑洞间量子纠缠与几何连接的物理实现所需资源，特别是将霍金辐射重构成新黑洞时的能量与信息代价。

Method: 运用半经典引力中的黑洞信息容量界限（如Bekenstein-Hawking熵），结合量子信息论中的纯化条件与Page曲线分析，对理想化黑洞重构过程施加能量与关联性约束。

Result: 得到外部输入能量的条件性下界；导出外部自由度必须保留的最小关联强度；明确熵守恒仅保证可行性，而精确/近似纯化需更强的量子态控制条件。

Conclusion: 该思想实验并非无代价操作：即使信息守恒，形成第二个纠缠黑洞仍需显著外部能量，并受限于辐射子系统间的残余纠缠结构；Page曲线揭示了不同演化阶段对纯化能力的要求差异。

Abstract: A thought experiment in the ER=EPR and related literature involves collecting the Hawking radiation of an old black hole, then collapsing it into a second, entangled black hole. In this short note, we examine the resources required by this idealized conversion. More specifically, we are interested in answering the question: how much external energy is needed to form the second black hole? Assuming a semiclassical bound on the information capacity of the newly formed black hole, we derive a conditional lower bound on external energy and a complementary constraint on correlations retained by the exterior degrees of freedom. We also distinguish entropy-level feasibility from the requirements for exact and approximate purification. We illustrate these requirements via a Page curve analysis.

</details>


### [101] [Strong gravity physics of dynamical horizons in black hole mergers and its imprint in their gravitational radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01275)
*Vaishak Prasad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了双黑洞并合过程中事件视界几何与引力波形之间的对应关系，发现视界上外向零测地线的剪切（shear）在旋近阶段具有类啁啾行为；计算了动力学视界源多极矩及其类Love数，发现其非零且具普适性；揭示了共同动力学视界在并合中通过吸收携带准正规模特征的引力辐射趋近最终Kerr态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏对双黑洞并合三阶段（旋近、并合、铃荡）中事件视界几何演化与观测到的引力波形之间定量关系的深入理解。

Method: 基于数值相对论模拟，分析动力学视界（DH）上的零测地线剪切、源多极矩及对应的类Love数；将视界物理量与无穷远处的Weyl标量Ψ₄进行多极分解与相关性分析；研究外共通动力学视界（OCDH）的形变演化及其与出射引力波的能量-角动量输运关系。

Result: 1) 视界剪切在旋近阶段呈现类啁啾信号，参数估计与波形一致；2) 动力学视界源多极矩定义的轴对称与非轴对称类Love数均非零，且在中等质量比下具普适性；3) OCDH在并合中通过吸收含准正规模特征的引力辐射弛豫至Kerr态，其外域引力场与ℐ⁺处具有相似多极结构。

Conclusion: 动力学视界不仅是几何对象，更是引力波动力学的主动参与者：其剪切、源多极矩与形变演化直接编码并影响引力波辐射特性，为强场引力检验和波形建模提供了新的几何视角与可观测量。

Abstract: In a binary black hole merger, the inspiral portion of the gravitational waveform corresponds to two distinct horizons orbiting each other, and the merger and ringdown signals correspond to the final horizon being formed and settling down to equilibrium. However, we still lack a detailed understanding of the relation between the horizon geometry in these three regimes and the observed waveform. In this thesis, we show that the well-known inspiral chirp waveform has a clear counterpart on black hole horizons, namely the shear of the outgoing null rays at the horizon. The shear behaves very much like a compact binary coalescence waveform with increasing frequency and amplitude, and the parameters of the system estimated from the horizon agree with those estimated from the waveform. We then track the source multipole moments of the dynamical horizons in simulations of several binary black hole mergers and calculate the corresponding Love numbers for axisymmetric modes. Unlike the (field) tidal Love numbers of non-spinning black holes, these do not vanish. We present evidence that, at least for modest mass ratios, their behaviour is universal. We extend this to the non-axisymmetric moments and show that the dynamical horizons encode detailed information about the dynamics of the binary, and that their source multipole moments are strongly correlated with the gravitational field at null infinity. Finally, we study the outer common dynamical horizon formed during the merger and its relationship with the outgoing gravitational waves, and show how the deformed common horizon approaches the final Kerr state by absorbing gravitational radiation with signatures of quasi-normal ringing. Its gravitational field shares a similar multipolar structure as that at future null infinity, and we point out some limitations, inconsistencies, and anomalies that need to be addressed.

</details>


### [102] [Gravitational dynamics and backward ray-traced shadows of bumblebee AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01413)
*Muhammad Yasir,Senyue Lou,Zelin Zhang,Abdul Jawad,Faren Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了bumblebee AdS黑洞附近圆轨道上测地线粒子发射光子的总观测频移，分析了bumblebee场对宇宙学演化与各向异性的贡献，并通过有效势方法推导了最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）条件；进一步建立了黑洞质量与吸积盘光子红/蓝移之间的函数关系，并利用Arcmancer代码通过后向光线追踪模拟了该黑洞的阴影图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lorentz破缺模型（bumblebee模型）在强引力场与宇宙学尺度下的可观测效应，特别是其对黑洞时空结构、吸积盘辐射频移及黑洞阴影的影响，以期为新物理提供潜在观测判据。

Method: 采用广义相对论框架下bumblebee引力理论的静态球对称AdS黑洞解；运用有效势法确定ISCO半径；数值求解测地线方程并结合后向光线追踪（Arcmancer）模拟黑洞阴影；分析bumblebee场作为各向异性源对宇宙演化方程的修正。

Result: 得到ISCO半径对bumblebee参数的依赖关系；发现光子红/蓝移量随黑洞质量和bumblebee真空期望值系统性变化；黑洞阴影半径与偏心度受bumblebee场显著调制，呈现可区分的Lorentz破缺特征。

Conclusion: bumblebee场不仅影响宇宙学背景演化与各向异性，也在强场天体物理观测（如频移、黑洞阴影）中留下清晰印记，表明高精度黑洞成像与光谱测量可成为检验Lorentz对称性破缺的有效途径。

Abstract: We investigate the total observed frequency shift of photons emitted by massive geodesic particles in circular orbits near the event horizon of bumblebee AdS black holes. The cosmological effects of the bumblebee field are investigated through dynamical equations governing the evolution and characteristic features of the Universe. In addition, the bumblebee field generates cosmological anisotropies by acting as a source of anisotropy and selecting a preferred spatial direction. We formulate the characteristic equations for the innermost stable circular orbit using the effective potential approach. Further analysis is devoted to establishing a functional relationship between the BH mass and the observed redshift and blueshift of photons emitted from the accretion disk. Finally, we study the shadow cast by the bumblebee AdS BH using the backward ray tracing process implemented in the Arcmancer code, where photon geodesics are initialized at the observers location and integrated backward in time to generate BH images, typically illuminated by electromagnetic radiation from nearby accretion flows.

</details>


### [103] [Effects of radiation on the Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01499)
*James M. Trippe,Brett N. Shapiro,Chloe A. Champagne,Brian D. Sierawski,Michael D. Hu,Volker Quetschke,Teviet Creighton,Philippe Lognonné,Mark P. Panning,John W. Conklin,Robert A. Reed,Karan Jani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究月球上引力波探测器LILA在辐射环境下的噪声来源，重点分析宇宙射线和太阳粒子引发的测试质量充电、动量转移与热弹性形变等伪信号，并通过Geant4模拟、静电力映射与热弹性核计算量化其影响；结果表明，在合理电荷管理设计下，辐射噪声不构成LILA中频段探测的主要限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球中频引力波探测（如LILA）面临表面辐射诱发的多种伪信号威胁，需定量评估其对灵敏度的影响以指导硬件设计。

Method: 结合Geant4粒子输运模拟、有限元静电力建模与空间热弹性响应核计算，针对100 kg熔融石英光学元件，对比不同铝屏蔽厚度（50–1000 mil）与两种静电驱动电压配置（含平衡驱动B型），并区分太阳极小期银河宇宙线+月表反照中子本底与1989年太阳质子事件极端情形。

Result: 平衡电极驱动（B型）显著抑制线性电荷-力耦合；理想平均充电电流连续补偿可消除相干电荷积累且引入可忽略的放电散粒噪声；补偿后电荷相关噪声主导低频段，热弹性形变主导高频段；累积剂量与光电探测器瞬态效应提示额外器件筛选要求；辐射噪声在电荷感知设计下不构成LILA总噪声预算的显著贡献。

Conclusion: LILA中频引力波探测的辐射噪声可控，关键在于采用电荷-aware硬件设计（如平衡驱动与连续电流补偿），辐射并非其灵敏度瓶颈。

Abstract: The Moon provides a promising environment for gravitational-wave (GW) observations in the midband. The Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA) combines a lunar deformation channel (LDC) and a geodesic deformation channel (GDC) to cover the midband. The LDC uses the Moon's resonant response to GW for the lower portion of the midband while the GDC measures displacement of suspended test masses to measure the latter. Radiation on the lunar surface can produce spurious signals through test-mass charging, momentum transfer, and thermoelastic deformation. We estimate these contributions for a notional 100 kg fused-silica optic using Geant4 transport, a finite-element electrostatic force map, and a spatial thermoelastic response kernel. Solar-minimum galactic cosmic rays and lunar albedo neutrons define a background case; a worst-day solar energetic particle spectrum (1989 solar flare conditions) defines an extreme, but rare, case. Aluminum-equivalent shielding from 50 to 1000 mils (1.27 to 25.4 mm) and two electrostatic actuator voltage configurations are compared. In the uniform-charge approximation, balanced drive with electrode configuration (B) substantially reduces linear charge-to-force coupling. Ideal continuous compensation of the mean charging current suppresses coherent charge accumulation while introducing negligible discharge shot noise. With compensation, charge-related noise dominates the low-frequency response, whereas thermoelastic deformation dominates at higher frequencies. Accumulated-dose and photodetector transient estimates identify additional component-qualification considerations. Estimates indicate that even under solar flare conditions, radiation noise is not a significant contributor to LILA's overall noise budget as long as charge-aware designs are followed.

</details>


### [104] [Choosing a generator for streaming Gaussian noise in gravitational-wave mock data: accuracy, cost and reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01624)
*Isaac C. F. Wong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种生成引力波分析所需长程有色高斯噪声的方法（如crossfade、overlap-save滤波、ARMA模型等），以协方差误差、吞吐量、状态内存和位精确再生代价为指标；结果表明，采用≥256 s窗口并平滑延拓目标功率谱至频带以下的crossfade方法在精度（误差<10⁻³）、速度（~4.7 MSPS）与内存（数百字节）间取得最优平衡，推荐作为标准噪声模拟方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为引力波数据分析（如mock data challenges）提供长程、高精度、可复现且计算高效的有色高斯噪声生成方法，克服现有实现中因频带截断导致的大协方差误差问题。

Method: 系统比较crossfade（含不同窗口长度与目标谱延拓策略）、overlap-save FIR滤波、AR/ARMA建模及PyCBC可复现滤波器；理论推导各方法协方差误差表达式，并实测吞吐量、状态大小与位精确再生开销；重点区分目标谱‘平滑延拓至频带以下’与‘硬截断于频带边缘’两种情形。

Result: 当目标谱平滑延拓时，256 s crossfade在全部11条探测器曲线上的协方差误差<10⁻³，吞吐达4.7 MSPS，状态仅数百字节；overlap-save虽更快但需数万阶FIR滤波器才能达几%误差；AR/ARMA未进入精度–吞吐Pareto前沿；8–235 s窗口误差达10⁻³，取决于探测器曲线窄线结构；硬截断下所有方法在aLIGO O4曲线上误差>0.5；crossfade呈循环平稳性，其循环分量仅在8 s窗口及部分设计曲线上主导误差。

Conclusion: 推荐采用查表确定窗口长度、并平滑延拓目标功率谱至频带以下的crossfade方法作为引力波噪声模拟的标准方案，因其在精度、效率与实现简洁性上综合最优。

Abstract: Mock data challenges need long stretches of colored Gaussian noise with a prescribed spectrum. The candidates are the crossfade of independently drawn windows that the standard gravitational-wave libraries implement, overlap-save finite-impulse-response filtering, autoregressive and autoregressive moving-average (ARMA) models, and the reproducible filter of PyCBC. One measure ranks them all: the covariance error against the target over the analysis band, evaluated from each generator's algebra; generated noise only checks the implementations. We also measure throughput, retained state and the cost of bit-exact regeneration. With the target continued smoothly below the band, the crossfade with windows of 256 s or more reaches an error below $10^{-3}$ on all eleven detector curves as tabulated, at about 4.7 million samples per second from a state of a few hundred bytes. An overlap-save filter is faster but needs tens of thousands of taps for an error of a few percent, and no tested autoregressive or ARMA configuration lies on the accuracy--throughput fronts. An error of $10^{-3}$ needs a window of 8 to 235 s, longest on the design curves with narrow lines, where it also depends on the tabulation. With the target cut at the band edge, as current generators do by default, every method has an error above one half on the projected Advanced LIGO (aLIGO) O4 curve, and the crossfade on five other curves. The crossfade is cyclostationary. With the continued target, its cyclic part is the larger part of the error only at 8 s, on the aLIGO O4 and the Virgo and KAGRA design curves, and stays within a factor of two of the stationary part on the aLIGO O3 and the Virgo and KAGRA design curves. We recommend the crossfade with a window from the table of detector curves, and a smooth continuation of the target below the band.

</details>


### [105] [Spherically-symmetric solutions with internal Ricci-flat spaces in Brans-Dicke theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01691)
*K.K. Ernazarov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了D维Brans-Dicke理论中具有n个内禀Ricci平坦空间的球对称精确解，通过Einstein共形变换求解后逆变回Jordan帧，得到度规与标量场显式表达式；分析表明，除Schwarzschild极限与冻结内空间情形外，动力学额外维通常导致视界处Kretschmann标量发散，即无正则视界；同时给出光子球与ISCO半径并讨论其Schwarzschild极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非最小耦合Brans-Dicke标量场在多维黑洞几何（含Ricci平坦内空间）中的作用，特别是其对因果结构、奇点性质及稳定圆轨道的影响。

Method: 采用共形变换将Jordan帧Brans-Dicke理论映射至Einstein帧（含最小耦合标量场），利用已知Einstein-标量场精确解，再经逆共形变换回Jordan帧；结合代数约束与微分几何分析（Kretschmann标量、视界条件、有效势分析）研究时空结构。

Result: 获得含n个Ricci平坦内空间的D维Brans-Dicke球对称精确解；导出四维截面视界存在的代数判据ν≥1；证明除Schwarzschild极限和冻结内空间外，动力学额外维导致视界处曲率发散；给出光子球半径R_ph与最内稳定圆轨道半径R_ISCO的解析表达式及其Schwarzschild极限。

Conclusion: Brans-Dicke非最小耦合显著改变多维黑洞的几何与物理性质：动力学内空间通常破坏视界正则性；四维截面的强引力区结构（如R_ph, R_ISCO）依赖于标量场与内空间动力学，揭示了标量-引力-额外维三者间的深层耦合。

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact spherically symmetric solutions in $D$-dimensional Brans-Dicke theory with $n$ internal Ricci-flat spaces. The spacetime is assumed to be a product of a four-dimensional spherical sector and $n$ Ricci-flat internal manifolds. By performing a conformal transformation to the Einstein frame, the problem is reduced to Einstein gravity with a minimally coupled scalar field, for which the exact solution is already known. Applying the inverse transformation to the Jordan frame yields explicit expressions for the metric and the Brans-Dicke scalar field, together with a generalized algebraic constraint on the exponents. The obtained solutions generalize the four-dimensional Brans solutions and the multidimensional solutions with Ricci-flat internal spaces. We analyze the causal structure of the four-dimensional section and derive the necessary condition for the surface $R=L$ to be an event horizon, $\nu \equiv \left|a+b+\frac12\sum_i a_iN_i\right| \ge 1$. A Kretschmann-scalar analysis shows that, apart from the Schwarzschild limit and the case of frozen internal spaces, a regular horizon is generically absent when the extra dimensions are dynamical. For the four-dimensional section we also obtain the photon-sphere and ISCO radii and discuss their Schwarzschild limits. The results clarify the role of nonminimal Brans-Dicke coupling in multidimensional black-hole geometries with Ricci-flat internal factors.

</details>


### [106] [Ricci-Non-metric Holographic Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01766)
*Sukanta Bhunia,Prabir Rudra,Abhijit Lahiri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Ricci标量R与非度规性标量Q组合的全息暗能量模型，其红外截断由二者线性组合构成；该构造仅依赖当前宇宙局域几何不变量，增强了理论基础性与因果性；模型可统一描述辐射、物质与暗能量主导三阶段，并在参数空间中展现出quintessence、穿越phantom分界或纯phantom行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统全息暗能量模型对宇宙历史或未来依赖的缺陷，利用几何不变量（R和Q）构建仅依赖当前宇宙状态的红外截断，提升理论基本性与因果合理性。

Method: 构造以R和Q线性组合作为红外截断的全息暗能量密度ρ_DE ∝ (αR + βQ)；结合FLRW度规与能量守恒方程，推导各宇宙学参数（Ω_DE, w_DE, q）的动力学演化；通过相空间分析与参数扫描研究模型行为。

Result: 模型可完整再现标准宇宙学演化序列：早期辐射主导（w≈1/3）、中期物质主导（w≈0）、晚期暗能量主导（w<−1/3）；方程态参量w_DE在参数调节下可呈现quintessence（w>−1）、phantom分界穿越（w=−1）及phantom相（w<−1）；密度参数Ω_DE随红移单调演化，符合观测约束趋势。

Conclusion: 基于R与Q不变量的全息暗能量模型是一个自洽、因果且具足够自由度的替代理论框架，能自然实现宇宙加速膨胀并兼容多期演化，为非黎曼引力与暗能量本质的交叉研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: In this work we propose a holographic dark energy model inspired by the Ricci scalar $R$ and non-metricity scalar $Q$. The infrared cutoff of the holographic dark energy is described by a combination of the Ricci scalar and the non-metricity scalar. Such a structure is very helpful because the evolution is entirely dependent on the universe's current characteristics rather than its previous or future characteristics. Additionally, the theory becomes more fundamental due to the application of invariants. We have built the model and examined its cosmological characteristics. The behavior of several cosmological parameters, including the density parameter, equation of state parameter, and deceleration parameter, is examined. It is observed that the holographic dark energy model may depict every cosmological epoch, beginning with radiation in the early universe, moving on to matter, and concluding with the dark energy-dominated period in later times. Depending on the parameter spaces selected, the equation of state parameter demonstrates that the model may have quintessence nature, phantom-divide crossing, or even phantom nature in future times.

</details>


### [107] [Optical Characteristics and Frequency Shifts of Quartic Wormholes in $f(T,τ)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01811)
*Sourav Chaudhary,S. K. Maurya,Jitendra Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(T,τ)引力框架下研究了具有四次反幂密度分布的可穿越虫洞解，推导出精确的形状函数，验证了几何性质（喉部、喇叭形条件、渐近行为），分析了能量条件违反与奇异物质特性，并利用高斯-博内定理计算引力偏转角，考察了标量/电磁扰动稳定性及新电磁势对红蓝移的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建满足所有可穿越虫洞几何要求且由物理可建模的物质分布（1/r⁴密度）支撑的自洽解，并系统研究其几何、物质、光学与扰动特性。

Method: 采用f(T,τ)引力理论；设定ρ(r) ∝ 1/r⁴确定形状函数b(r)；检验零能量条件（NEC）；应用Gauss-Bonnet定理计算弱场下引力偏转角；进行标量场Φ和电磁场A_μ的线性扰动分析；引入新电磁势A_t(r)研究红蓝移谱线分裂。

Result: 获得解析虫洞解，满足喉部条件与渐近平直性；径向压强为负，NEC在喉部被显著违反；偏转角强烈依赖参数γ与ρ₀；标量与电磁扰动均显示稳定谱；新电磁势导致非对称红蓝移分布。

Conclusion: 四次反幂密度可在f(T,τ)引力中支持稳定、可观测的可穿越虫洞，其光学信号（偏转角、红蓝移）具备参数敏感性，为未来引力波与强场天体物理观测提供理论基准。

Abstract: This work explores traversable wormhole solutions characterized by a quartic inverse-power density profile within $f(T,{\tau})$ gravity. A specific inverse-power density profile, characterized by proportional to $1/r^4$ is introduced to establish the corresponding exact form of the shape function. The derived configuration exhibits all the necessary geometrical features of a traversable wormhole, such as throat formation, flaring-out behavior, and acceptable asymptotic characteristics. To gain further insight into the matter sector, we analyze the corresponding radial profiles of the energy density and pressure components. The throat region is characterised by negative radial pressure and null energy condition violation, both of which are relevant to the exotic matter content of the wormhole solution. To examine the lensing characteristics of the obtained spacetime, we employ the Gauss-Bonnet formalism to evaluate the gravitational deflection angle. Our findings indicate that the parameters $\gamma$ and $\rho_0$ exert a considerable influence on the resulting light trajectories. The perturbative behavior of the obtained configuration is investigated within the scalar and electromagnetic field sectors. Subsequently, a novel electromagnetic potential is introduced to examine its influence on the radial variation of the red-blue shifts characteristics. Our findings establish a unified picture of the geometrical, perturbative, and optical characteristics of quartic density-supported wormholes.

</details>


### [108] [Vanishing Island: Spectral Criticality in Boson Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01826)
*Bo-Xuan Ge*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文识别出玻色子星稳定区域消失的尖点型端点，揭示了径向软模的平方根发散行为及临界结构的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解玻色子星径向稳定性边界及其临界行为，特别是稳定区域消失的机制。

Method: 通过分析径向零模边界、约束二次刚度、三次与四次非线性系数的共alescence行为，结合标量场自相互作用变形下的鲁棒性检验。

Result: 发现尖点型端点处两个基本径向零模边界合并，二次刚度与三次系数 vanish，四次系数有限非零；软模频率满足 Ω_soft ∝ (σ_cusp − σ)^{1/2}，线性时标发散。

Conclusion: 该尖点结构是玻色子星径向稳定性的普适临界特征，且在标量自相互作用变形下保持不变。

Abstract: We identify a cusp-type endpoint at which a finite radially stable region of solitonic boson stars disappears. Two fundamental radial zero-mode boundaries coalesce there, while the constrained quadratic stiffness and cubic coefficient vanish and the quartic coefficient remains finite and non-zero. The soft radial mode obeys \(\Omega_{\mathrm{soft}}\propto (\sigma_{\mathrm{cusp}}-\sigma)^{1/2}\), revealing a divergent linear timescale at the endpoint. The same critical structure persists under the tested deformation of the scalar self-interaction.

</details>


### [109] [Light Ring Stability Across Spacetime Dimensions and Theories of Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01917)
*Kajol Paithankar,Sanved Kolekar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将光环（LR）存在性与稳定性定理从四维广义相对论推广至f(R)引力、标量-张量理论及受限Horndeski引力等修正引力理论，并涵盖d ≥ 4维时空；发现四维修正引力中LR稳定性依赖于理论耦合参数与NEC共同约束，稳定LR并非必然存在；高维（d > 4）下LR仍成对出现但具四种拓扑类型（含两类鞍点），且配对不保证含稳定LR；该结果显著影响利用UCO检验GR的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究 horizonless 超致密天体（UCOs）作为黑洞模拟体的长期稳定性，核心在于其是否必然携带稳定光圈（stable light ring），而该问题在广义相对论中已有定理（light ring theorem），但尚未延拓至修正引力与高维情形。

Method: 基于稳态轴对称时空几何，结合能量条件（特别是零能量条件 NEC）、微分拓扑（如 Poincaré-Hopf 定理）与线性扰动稳定性分析，在 f(R) 引力、标量-张量理论及受限 Horndeski 理论框架下推导光圈存在的必要条件与稳定性判据，并推广至 d ≥ 4 维。

Result: （1）四维修正引力中，LR 存在性仍成立，但稳定性由 NEC 与理论特有耦合参数共同决定，存在参数区域无稳定 LR；（2）d > 4 维时，LR 在拓扑意义上仍成对出现，但具有四种类型（含两类 saddle-point LR），且稳定性分析排除部分配对方式，导致稳定 LR 不必然出现；（3）UCO 作为 GR 检验探针的可靠性高度依赖于引力理论与维度。

Conclusion: 光圈稳定性并非普适性质，而是强烈依赖于引力理论的具体结构（如高阶曲率项或标量场耦合）和时空维度；因此，基于 UCO 的强场引力检验必须明确建模所处理论框架与维度，否则可能得出错误结论。

Abstract: The light ring theorem states that four-dimensional stationary, axisymmetric, horizonless ultracompact objects (UCOs) possess light rings (LRs) in pairs, one of which is stable if the Null Energy Condition (NEC) holds in General Relativity (GR). The existence of a stable LR may trigger nonlinear spacetime instabilities, thereby raising questions about the viability of UCOs as long-lived black hole mimickers. We extend the LR existence and stability theorem beyond four-dimensional GR to modified gravity theories, in particular, $f(R)$ gravity, scalar-tensor theories, and restricted Horndeski gravity, in ($d \geq 4$) dimensional spacetimes. Our findings show that in four-dimensional modified gravities, the stability of LRs is governed by theory-specific coupling parameters in addition to the NEC. Consequently, a stable LR is not guaranteed to occur in certain parameter regimes. In higher dimensions ($d>4$), we confirm using topological arguments that LRs still emerge in pairs; however, the topological charges are now associated with four distinct types of LRs, including two types of saddle points. The stability analysis further constrains the allowed LR pairings, implying that, unlike in four-dimensional GR, the emergent pair does not necessarily include a stable LR. These findings directly impact tests of GR using UCOs, demonstrating that LR stability is highly sensitive to both the underlying gravitational theory and spacetime dimensionality.

</details>


### [110] [Spin-dependent topological structure of black-hole thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02035)
*Erdem Sucu,İzzet Sakallı,Yusuf Sucu,Emmanuel N. Saridakis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子修正霍金辐射与热力学拓扑之间的联系，以质量引力AdS黑洞为模型，结合任意自旋Zitterbewegung隧穿与广义不确定原理（GUP）修正，推导出自旋依赖的霍金温度、通道依赖的有效熵、离壳自由能及热力学矢量场；发现全局拓扑电荷W在γ=−1分支恒为+1，在γ=+1分支恒为0，而粒子自旋仅调制局域缺陷位置与配对湮灭的临界构型，从而揭示量子辐射通道信息被编码于局域热力学拓扑结构中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究热力学拓扑（基于离壳热力学场的拓扑缺陷）与量子修正霍金辐射（携带发射粒子量子数信息）之间是否存在物理关联，此前该问题基本未被探索。

Method: 采用任意自旋Zitterbewegung隧穿模型结合广义不确定原理（GUP）修正，推导自旋依赖的霍金温度；利用热力学第一定律构造通道依赖的有效熵；由此定义离壳自由能与热力学矢量场；对比分析Bekenstein-Hawking熵与指数修正熵作为参考；通过计算拓扑绕数W识别缺陷类型与全局电荷，并分析缺陷演化与分岔结构。

Result: 发现全局热力学拓扑电荷W具有分支依赖性：γ=−1时存在单一正绕数缺陷（W=+1），γ=+1时缺陷成对出现且绕数相反（总W=0）；粒子自旋不改变W，但显著移动缺陷位置，并调控γ=+1分支中缺陷对的合并-湮灭临界点；该临界‘折叠’由自旋、GUP参数与宇宙学常数共同控制。

Conclusion: 量子修正霍金辐射虽不改变热力学拓扑的全局分类，但其粒子自旋等量子通道信息完整编码于局域缺陷构型与分岔结构中，首次建立了量子辐射通道与离壳热力学拓扑之间的直接映射关系。

Abstract: Thermodynamic topology classifies black-hole equilibrium states through topological defects of an off-shell thermodynamic field, while quantum-corrected Hawking radiation can retain information about the quantum numbers of the emitted particle. Whether these two structures are connected has remained largely unexplored. In this work we investigate this question for a massive-gravity AdS black hole by combining arbitrary-spin zitterbewegung tunneling with generalized-uncertainty-principle corrections. We derive the particle-spin-dependent Hawking temperature, construct the corresponding channel-dependent effective entropy through the first law, and formulate the associated off-shell free energy and thermodynamic vector field. As reference sectors, we also analyze the Bekenstein-Hawking and exponentially corrected entropies. We find a clear separation between local and global thermodynamic topology. For the $\gamma=-1$ branch, a single positively wound defect persists, yielding $W=+1$, whereas for $\gamma=+1$ the defects occur in oppositely wound pairs and the global charge remains $W=0$. The particle spin nevertheless shifts the defect positions and the critical configurations at which the $\gamma=+1$ pair merges and annihilates. The fold separating the two-defect and defect-free regions is controlled jointly by the particle spin, the GUP parameter, and the cosmological constant. Thus, the quantum properties of the emitted particle do not alter the global topological class, but they reorganize the local defect and bifurcation structure within it. Our results establish a direct connection between quantum-corrected Hawking radiation and the corresponding effective off-shell thermodynamic topology, showing that its local defect structure can retain information about the quantum channel through which the black hole radiates.

</details>


### [111] [Torsion, Frames and Quantum Bounces: Inflation and Ekpyrotic Contraction in Quantum Trajectory Cosmology with a Non-Minimally Coupled Scalar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02036)
*Rikpratik Sengupta,Paulo Vargas Moniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小耦合标量场在度规相容Riemann-Cartan几何下的de Broglie-Bohm量子宇宙学，重点比较暴胀与ekpyrotic收缩的动力学行为；通过消除辅助挠率迹，在共形平直的minisuperspace中建立Wheeler-DeWitt方程，并证明其Bohmian轨迹、量子势及Laplace-Beltrami算符具有Einstein-Jordan帧协变性；发现‘反弹’源于波函数干涉而非挠率，且挠率仅通过Einstein帧动能函数K(ϕ)影响动力学；对指数势与二次势模型计算了标量谱、张量谱及B-mode张量-标量比r，指出挠率对可观测量（如ns, r）修正微弱，仅在双场熵机制中引入√ξ与ξ^{3/2}阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Riemann-Cartan几何中挠率（特别是trace torsion）对量子宇宙学早期动力学（暴胀 vs ekpyrosis）的物理可观测效应，厘清其是否可作为区分机制，以及是否真正驱动‘量子反弹’。

Method: 采用minisuperspace约化与de Broglie-Bohm诠释：先代数消去辅助挠率迹，导出依赖非最小耦合函数F(ϕ)与挠率二次项系数κ的minisuperspace度规；利用其共形平直性将Wheeler-DeWitt方程映射至Einstein帧；求解精确波函数（massless scalar、exponential、quadratic potentials），计算通量加权Bohmian轨迹、量子势、功率谱及r参数；区分Jordan/Einstein帧物理预言并检验帧协变性。

Result: 1) minisuperspace度规恒共形平直 → 挠率不改变曲率诊断，仅通过K(ϕ)调制动力学；2) massless标量下所有41条通量加权Bohm轨迹在Einstein帧反弹，但Jordan帧反弹条件为ξ < ξ_c，且晚期标度因子指数受挠率修正；3) 反弹纯属波干涉效应，与挠率无关；4) 指数势下ekpyrotic吸引子区无反弹，单动量态终归大挤压；5) 二次暴胀中挠率对r的修正<1%；6) 单场ekpyrosis给出普适蓝谱ns=3+2/(ε−1), r=16ε，与挠率无关；7) 双场熵机制中ns在√ξ（非最小耦合）与ξ^{3/2}（挠率）阶受修正；8) 张量模穿越Bohm反弹几乎不变，保持蓝谱。

Conclusion: 挠率在Riemann-Cartan量子宇宙学中不引发本质新物理——它不驱动反弹、不改变谱指数主导行为、对r压制显著；其可观测效应仅在特定多场框架（如熵机制）中以高阶小参数出现；真正的动力学起源是波函数干涉与共形帧选择，而非几何挠率本身。

Abstract: We study the de Broglie-Bohm quantum cosmology of a scalar field non-minimally coupled to a metric-compatible Riemann-Cartan geometry, comparing inflation with ekpyrotic contraction. The torsion trace is auxiliary and is eliminated algebraically before quantisation. The minisuperspace metric depends on the coupling $F(\phi)$ and a constant $\kappa$ for an independent quadratic torsion term ($\kappa=0$ Einstein-Cartan, $\kappa\to\infty$ torsion-free). For every $F$, $\kappa$ and potential this metric is conformally flat, so torsion acts only through the Einstein-frame kinetic function $K(\phi)$. Its curvature is therefore not a diagnostic of torsion, and the Laplace-Beltrami Wheeler-DeWitt equation, quantum potential and Bohmian trajectories are frame covariant. For a massless scalar the equation is solved exactly; in a two-packet state all 41 flux-weighted Bohmian trajectories bounce in the Einstein frame. In the Jordan frame the bounce at the symmetric event is independent of torsion and persists only for $\xi<\xi_c$ (closed form); torsion changes the late-time exponent from $1-\sqrt{6\xi/(1+6\xi)}$ to $1-\sqrt{6\xi}$. The bounce comes from interference in the state, not from torsion. For exponential potentials, exact Wheeler-DeWitt solutions show that in the sector containing the ekpyrotic attractor no Bohmian trajectory bounces, and single-momentum states bounce only where the potential is negligible and end in the crunch. For quadratic inflation the torsion-specific shift of $r$ is below one per cent. A single ekpyrotic field gives blue spectra, $n_s=3+2/(\epsilon-1)$ and $r=16\epsilon$, whatever the torsion, also for running $\epsilon$. In an entropic two-field version the tilt shifts at order $\sqrt\xi$ (non-minimal coupling) and $\xi^{3/2}$ (torsion). Tensor modes cross the massless Bohmian bounce almost unchanged and stay blue.

</details>


### [112] [Black Hole Love Numbers with Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02084)
*Maria J. Rodriguez,Luis Fernando Temoche,Pavlos Protopapas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于物理信息的神经网络（PINN）框架，用于直接从黑洞微扰方程中计算Kerr黑洞的潮汐Love数；该方法通过精确施加视界边界条件和采用H²-Sobolev型损失函数（含微分方程残差的高阶导数），结合渐近系数提取，无需解析连接公式，即可高精度复现标量、矢量和张量扰动下的有限耗散响应（相对误差<10⁻⁵），同时将保守响应约束在数值误差底限内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析方法依赖连接公式提取潮汐响应，难以推广至非分离变量或频变扰动问题；需发展一种不依赖解析解结构、可端到端求解微分方程并直接提取物理可观测量（如Love数）的数值框架。

Method: 构建物理信息神经网络（PINN），其损失函数包含：(i) 视界处精确施加的边界条件；(ii) H²-Sobolev范数形式的微分方程残差（含二阶导数项）；训练后通过渐近展开直接拟合解在无穷远的系数以提取Love数，避免使用超几何函数的解析连接公式。

Result: 对Kerr黑洞的静态标量/矢量/张量扰动，成功复现已知的有限耗散Love数，相对误差低于10⁻⁵；H²损失相较L²损失提升精度达多个数量级；独立验证表明提取误差与求解误差可分离；保守部分（理论上为零）被压制至数值误差底限（~10⁻⁹–10⁻¹⁰）。

Conclusion: PINN框架可高精度、端到端地计算黑洞潮汐响应，且不依赖解析解结构或连接公式；该方法已通过严格基准验证，为拓展至频域、非分离、非线性或动态潮汐问题提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We develop and validate a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for computing the tidal Love numbers of black holes directly from their perturbation equations. We consider static perturbations of the Kerr black hole, whose exact hypergeometric solutions provide a controlled benchmark for both the numerical solver and the extraction of the physical response. The framework combines exact enforcement of the horizon boundary conditions with an $H^2$-Sobolev loss that incorporates derivatives of the differential-equation residual. The Love number is then obtained by extracting the asymptotic coefficients of the learned solution, without using the analytic connection formula. Across the tested scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations, the method recovers the finite dissipative response with relative errors below $10^{-5}$. Controlled comparisons show that the higher-derivative loss terms improve solution accuracy by orders of magnitude, while independent tests of the extraction procedure distinguish solver errors from errors in recovering the response coefficients. The conservative response, which vanishes analytically, is constrained by the numerical error floor. These results demonstrate that PINNs can accurately compute black-hole tidal response and establish a validated framework for future extensions to frequency-dependent and non-separable perturbation problems.

</details>


### [113] [Dimensionally Regulated Graviton Self-Energy from Gravitons on de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02120)
*B. Yesilyurt,N. C. Tsamis,R. P. Woodard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了纯量子引力在de Sitter背景下的1圈引力子自能，并利用它求解有效场方程，得到引力波模函数和牛顿势的1圈量子修正；结果表明模函数可被选择为无渐近增长，但牛顿势呈现e-folding数的三次方增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究量子引力对引力辐射和引力相互作用的修正，需计算引力子1PI两点半函数（即自能），并将其代入线性化爱因斯坦方程的有效场方程中；关键难点在于自能在对角点x'=x处发散，需借助正规化与重正化技术处理。

Method: 采用维数正规化与BPHZ重正化方案，在de Sitter时空、最简规范（如共形平坦坐标下的谐波规范）下，计算纯量子引力（无物质场）的1圈引力子自能 -i [^μνΣ^ρσ](x;x')；随后求解含该自能源项的有效线性化爱因斯坦方程，提取引力波平面波模函数与静态牛顿势的量子修正。

Result: 1. 得到de Sitter背景下1圈引力子自能的有限重正化表达式；2. 引力波平面波模函数的1圈修正可被重正化群条件抑制，避免红外/长期（secular）增长；3. 牛顿势出现不可消除的~N³红外增长（N为e-folding数），表明de Sitter背景中静态引力场存在固有量子不稳定性。

Conclusion: 在de Sitter时空纯量子引力中，引力子1圈自能导致静态引力势出现不可重整掉的立方红外增长，揭示了宇宙学常数背景下引力相互作用的深层量子不稳定性；而引力波传播则可通过适当重正化保持良好行为。

Abstract: The graviton self-energy $-i [\mbox{}^{\mu\nu} \Sigma^{\rho\sigma}](x;x')$ is the 1PI 2-point function of quantum gravity. It can be used to quantum-correct the linearized Einstein equation in order to infer quantum corrections to gravitational radiation and to the force of gravity. As long as the two points are non-coincident, the graviton self-energy does not need regularization at 1-loop order, however, solving the 1-loop effective field equations requires integrating $[\mbox{}^{\mu\nu} \Sigma^{\rho \sigma}](x;x')$ over ${x'}^{\mu}$, including ${x'}^{\mu} = x^{\mu}$. To facilitate this we employ dimensional regularization and BPHZ renormalization to compute the 1-loop contribution to the graviton self-energy, in pure quantum gravity, on de Sitter background, in the simplest gauge. We then solve the effective field equations to derive 1-loop corrections to the plane wave mode functions of gravitational radiation, and to the Newtonian potential. Although it seems possible to renormalize in such a way that the mode function shows no secular growth, the Newtonian potential grows like the third power of the number of e-foldings.

</details>


### [114] [A Singularity Theorem for Quasi-Topological Gravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02190)
*Francesco Di Filippo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Penrose奇点定理至所有已知具有非奇异黑洞解的拟拓扑引力理论，证明在满足零能量条件的球对称坍缩中，一旦形成陷俘面，必导致未来类光测地线不完备性，表明此类理论在黑洞内部动力学研究中与广义相对论同样面临可预测性失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟拓扑引力理论因存在非奇异黑洞真空解而被视为可能解决奇点问题并描述黑洞核心的候选理论；但其已知解含有柯西视界，破坏理论的可预测性，需检验该问题是否在动力学坍缩中普遍存在。

Method: 将Penrose奇点定理推广至一般拟拓扑引力理论框架，基于零能量条件（NEC）和陷俘面存在性，运用类光测地线完备性分析与因果结构论证，严格证明未来类光测地线不完备性。

Result: 所有已知具非奇异黑洞解的拟拓扑引力理论均满足推广后的Penrose定理前提，球对称引力坍缩必然导致未来类光测地线不完备，即物理奇点（或至少强因果失效）不可避免。

Conclusion: 拟拓扑引力理论虽能规避静态/真空奇点，却无法消除动力学坍缩中由陷俘面触发的不可预测性；其黑洞内部描述仍受柯西视界或更强奇点制约，未真正解决广义相对论的奇点与决定论危机。

Abstract: Quasi-topological theories of gravity have recently attracted substantial attention as several theories in this class have a non-singular black hole as a vacuum and spherically symmetric solution. This motivates the expectation that these theories can provide a resolution to the singularity problem and a description of black holes cores. The known solutions, however, possess a Cauchy horizon which denotes the end of predictability of the theory, as the initial data do not determine a unique continuation past this type of horizon. In this paper, we show that Penrose singularity theorem can be extended to all the known classes of quasi-topological theories that have non-singular black holes as solutions. We prove that spherical collapse satisfying the null energy condition necessarily leads to future null geodesic incompleteness once a trapped sphere forms. This implies that the lack of predictability of the theories is not an artefact of vacuum solutions and these theories are as problematic as general relativity for the dynamical studies of black hole interiors.

</details>


### [115] [Pilot search for continuous gravitational waves from Hercules X-1 in O3 LIGO data: enhancements to a hidden Markov model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02307)
*Nicholas Kah Yean Low,Andrew Melatos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对 Hercules X-1 中恒星振荡产生的连续引力波进行了半相干搜索，使用改进的隐马尔可夫模型算法处理频率随机漂移，并验证了多项算法增强（如二阶投影半长轴修正、偏心率修正、子带宽调整及轨道参数传播），在60–200 Hz频段获得四个候选事件，174 Hz处95%置信度的应变振幅上限为9.3×10⁻²⁶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升对低信噪比、频率漂移连续引力波信号的探测灵敏度，特别是针对Hercules X-1这类已知X射线双星系统中可能存在的恒星振荡辐射；同时测试面向未来搜寻的关键算法升级。

Method: 采用基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的半相干搜索框架，集成四项新修正：（i）二阶投影半长轴校正以更精确建模多普勒调制，（ii）轨道偏心率校正，（iii）自适应子带宽优化，（iv）将电磁观测得到的非零轨道周期导数（Ṗ_orb ≠ 0）纳入轨道参数传播；通过高斯噪声中注入合成信号验证性能提升。

Result: 算法增强使灵敏度深度提升约14%；在60–200 Hz频段识别出4个通过五级致稳（veto）检验的候选信号，终止频率分别为76.41 Hz、109.47 Hz、144.40 Hz 和 189.24 Hz；在174 Hz处给出95%置信度的应变振幅上界为9.3×10⁻²⁶（假设偏振角均匀先验）。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法增强显著提升了半相干搜索对频率漂移连续波的鲁棒性与灵敏度，验证了其在真实O3数据中的可行性，为下一代引力波巡天（如O4/O5）中对低质量X射线双星的定向搜寻奠定了方法学基础。

Abstract: A semi-coherent search is conducted for continuous gravitational waves from stellar oscillations in Hercules X-1 with data from Observing Run 3 of the Laser Interferometer Gravitational-wave Observatory, using an established hidden Markov model algorithm to track stochastic wandering of the signal frequency. The primary goal is to implement and test several algorithmic enhancements relevant to future searches, including a second-order projected semi-major axis correction, an orbital eccentricity correction, an adjustment to the width of a search subband, and propagation of the electromagnetically measured orbital parameters with a nonzero orbital period derivative. The enhancements are validated by injecting synthetic signals into Gaussian noise and improve the sensitivity depth by $\approx$ 14% for a Hercules X-1-like target. In the 60--200 Hz band, the pilot search yields four candidates that survive a five-level veto procedure, whose terminating frequencies are 76.41 Hz, 109.47 Hz, 144.40 Hz, and 189.24 Hz. The frequentist upper limit on the maximum detectable wave strain at 95% confidence is arbitrarily set at 174 Hz and found to be $9.3 \times 10^{-26}$, assuming a uniform prior on polarization.

</details>


### [116] [Static Vacuum Geometry Encodes Gravitational Collapse and Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02358)
*Yi Ling,Jinsong Yang,Zhangping Yu,Cong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在球对称协变哈密顿引力框架下，证明了一族静态真空几何唯一确定了与无旋尘埃耦合的引力坍缩局域动力学，并建立了静态真空几何与修正弗里德曼动力学之间的双向对应关系，同时给出了其宇宙学子部门存在的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定独立提出的黑洞模型与宇宙学模型是否可源于同一协变引力理论。

Method: 基于球对称协变哈密顿引力形式，结合无旋尘埃物质场，分析静态真空解对坍缩动力学的约束，并推导其蕴含均匀各向同性宇宙学子部门的充要条件。

Result: 1) 一族静态真空几何唯一决定尘埃坍缩的局域动力学；2) 导出该动力学含FLRW宇宙学子部门的充要条件；3) 建立静态真空几何与修正弗里德曼方程的双向映射；4) 得到空间曲率项在修正弗里德曼动力学中的普适限制。

Conclusion: 静态真空几何不仅编码黑洞结构，还全局约束宇宙学演化；二者统一于同一协变哈密顿引力框架，且其对应受空间曲率依赖性的严格限制。

Abstract: We show that, within covariant Hamiltonian gravity in spherical symmetry, a prescribed one-parameter family of static vacuum geometries uniquely determines the local dynamics of gravitational collapse coupled to irrotational dust, despite the nonuniqueness of the underlying gravitational Hamiltonian. We further derive necessary and sufficient conditions under which the resulting dynamics admits a homogeneous and isotropic cosmological sector. These conditions establish a two-way correspondence between static vacuum geometries and modified Friedmann dynamics and impose a universal restriction on the spatial-curvature dependence of the latter. Our results provide a criterion for determining whether independently proposed black-hole and cosmological models can arise from a common covariant gravitational theory.

</details>


### [117] [A simple flux criterion for trapped surface formation by short pulses of gravitational radiation and matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02569)
*Tetsuya Shiromizu,Keisuke Izumi,Daisuke Yoshida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在双零形式下，由短脉冲入射引力辐射和物质场诱导的陷俘面形成机制；通过建立脉冲宽度的量级层次，发现入射通量近似守恒地向内传输并被放大；利用Raychaudhuri方程导出陷俘面形成的初始通量判据，并以Ricci张量分量及其导数表述物质条件，从而保持论证模型无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场中黑洞形成的动力学阈值，特别是非线性引力波与物质耦合导致的奇点前几何结构（如陷俘面）的生成机制。

Method: 采用双零坐标系下的Einstein方程演化分析；引入脉冲宽度的小参数展开，构建几何量与物质场的量级层次；结合Raychaudhuri方程与能量条件约束，推导陷俘面形成的通量型判据。

Result: 获得一个不依赖具体物质模型的、基于入射通量积分与Ricci分量导数的陷俘面形成判据；证明在短脉冲近似下，通量经历近似保守的向内传输并被几何聚焦效应显著放大。

Conclusion: 陷俘面可在足够强且足够窄的入射引力辐射与物质通量共同作用下形成，其存在性由通量幅值与脉冲宽度的组合决定，且该结论具有普适性——适用于满足相应Ricci约束的一般物质场。

Abstract: We discuss the formation of trapped surfaces induced by short pulses of incoming gravitational radiation and matter fields, in a double-null formulation. We identify a hierarchy of orders in the pulse width for the geometric and matter quantities consistent with the evolution equations. Under the assumption that this hierarchy holds throughout the region considered, the incoming fluxes undergo approximately conservative inward transport and are thereby amplified. The Raychaudhuri equation then gives an initial flux criterion for trapped surface formation. The conditions on the matter are expressed through the Ricci components and their derivatives, which allows the argument to be developed without specifying a particular matter model.

</details>


### [118] [Cloudy black-hole configurations of the composed Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs field theory: Analytic treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02656)
*Shahar Hod*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过解析方法研究了Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs理论中'云状'黑洞（即支持正则线性化场构型的磁化RN黑洞）与线性化场构成的束缚态，推导出小质量弱磁化黑洞在1 ≪ r₊ ≪ m_w⁻¹参数区间的离散共振谱{μₖ}，并发现其激发态满足渐近比值μ_{k+1}/μ_k = e^{-2π/√3}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究违反无毛定理的非阿贝尔黑洞中‘云状’构型的形成机制及其共振谱结构，特别是在线性化场与黑洞构成束缚态的物理图像下。

Method: 采用解析技术分析Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs系统中黑洞-线性化场耦合的本征值问题，聚焦于小质量弱磁化极限下的渐近行为，并推导质量-半径无量纲参数μ = m_w r₊的离散谱。

Result: 得到共振谱{μₖ}的紧致解析表达式，并发现其高阶激发态满足普适渐近比μ_{k+1}/μ_k = e^{-2π/√3}，该比值与激发量子数k无关。

Conclusion: 云状黑洞的线性化场束缚态具有离散、可解析刻画的共振谱；其谱间距呈现指数标度律，揭示了该非阿贝尔黑洞性质中深刻的数学结构与普适性。

Abstract: Non-Abelian black holes that violate the non-hair conjecture are known to exist in the Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs field theory. The onset of hair formation in this composed physical system is marked by the presence of `cloudy' black-hole spacetimes, magnetically charged Reissner-Nordström black holes that support spatially regular linearized field configurations. In the present paper we explore, using {\it analytical} techniques, the physical and mathematical properties of the composed black-hole-linearized-field bound-state configurations of the Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs theory. In particular, we derive a remarkably compact analytical formula for the discrete resonance spectrum $\{\mu_k\}^{k=\infty}_{k=1}$ of the dimensionless mass-radius parameter $\mu\equiv m_{\text{w}} r_+$ that characterizes the cloudy black-hole-field configurations in the regime $1\ll r_+\ll m^{-1}_{\text{w}}$ of small-mass weakly-magnetized black holes (here $r_+$ is the horizon radius of the central supporting black hole and $m_{\text{w}}$ is the mass of the Yang-Mills field due to the Higgs effect). Interestingly, our results reveal that the excited black-hole-field resonances are characterized by the asymptotic $k$-independent dimensionless ratio ${\mu_{k+1}/{\mu_{k}}}=e^{-{{2\pi}/{\sqrt{3}}}}$.

</details>


### [119] [First law of thermodynamics for interacting charged black holes and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02663)
*Qing Han,Marcus Khuri,Gilbert Weinstein,Jingang Xiong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了渐近平直、定态、轴对称Einstein-Maxwell黑洞解的质量变分恒等式，即第一定律，适用于多电荷视界与有限圆锥轴奇点情形；在全退化族上引入ADM质量修正函数，其对角动量的导数给出视界角速度；在Majumdar-Papapetrou族上该函数的角动量Hessian正定，并沿满足Weyl间隙递增、电荷固定的可容路径非减（半正定序）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立适用于含多个带电视界及轴上有限圆锥奇点的广义黑洞热力学第一定律，并探究质量作为角动量函数的凸性结构及其物理约束。

Method: 基于参数化解族的变分分析，结合Weyl坐标下轴对称Einstein-Maxwell方程的几何结构，构造质量修正函数，利用conical defect参数符号定理与Hessian比较技术分析其单调性。

Result: 获得普适质量变分恒等式；证明存在唯一ADM质量修正函数，其梯度给出角速度；在Majumdar-Papapetrou极限下Hessian正定；且该Hessian沿固定正电荷、增大Weyl间隙的路径在Loewner序下非减。

Conclusion: 黑洞质量作为角动量的函数具有内在凸性，其Hessian的单调演化受Weyl几何间隙与电荷约束，为多视界黑洞的稳定性与相结构提供了微分几何判据。

Abstract: We derive a mass variation identity expressing the first law of black hole thermodynamics for asymptotically flat, stationary, axisymmetric Einstein-Maxwell solutions with multiple electrically charged horizons and finite conical axis singularities. Under the stated asymptotic and parameter regularity hypotheses, the identity holds for independent variations of all parameters within families of fixed horizon types. On the all-degenerate family, a correction to the ADM mass defines a scalar function whose derivatives with respect to angular momenta are the horizon angular velocities. At the Majumdar-Papapetrou family, its angular momentum Hessian is positive definite. Combining the first variation identity with a separate theorem on the sign of the conical parameters shows that this Hessian is nondecreasing in the positive-semidefinite order along admissible paths of increasing Weyl gaps at fixed positive charges.

</details>


### [120] [Charge-Activated Birefringence and Shadow Mimicry around Reissner-Nordström Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02727)
*Mao-Yuan Wan,Chen Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现，在带电Reissner-Nordström黑洞背景下，电荷可激发中性矢量场与爱因斯坦张量耦合所导致的双折射效应；该耦合使左右手偏振（两宇称）阴影边缘分离并产生完全螺旋度反转的后向散射信号；仅改变电荷无法复现这些现象，必须同时存在非零电荷与耦合；弱耦合下主导修正对两类有效势一致，正/负耦合分别抬高/压低势垒、抑制/增强低频吸收、减小/增大平均阴影半径；后向信号非零源于两宇称散射振幅的复数差异，且严格螺旋度反转；无耦合时两宇称阴影重合、后向强度严格为零；因此阴影边缘分裂与后向信号可作为区分真实耦合与单纯电荷变化的观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测广义相对论之外的新物理效应，特别是中性矢量场与曲率张量（如爱因斯坦张量）的直接耦合是否可通过黑洞阴影与散射信号加以验证。

Method: 在Reissner-Nordström时空背景下，求解与爱因斯坦张量非最小耦合的中性矢量场（Proca型）的线性摄动方程；分离左右手偏振分量，推导其有效势；分析光线追踪极限下的阴影轮廓及散射振幅的宇称依赖性；计算后向散射微分截面与螺旋度反转特征。

Result: 1) 电荷激活耦合诱导的双折射，导致两宇称阴影半径分裂；2) 出现严格螺旋度反转的后向散射信号（helicity-flipped backward signal）；3) 弱耦合下有效势修正符号决定吸收与阴影尺寸趋势；4) 无耦合时后向信号恒为零，而耦合+电荷共同存在时该信号非零且可观测。

Conclusion: 阴影边缘分裂与螺旋度反转的后向信号是中性矢量场-爱因斯坦张量耦合的普适、洁净观测标识，可明确区别于纯电荷效应；但其实验检验尚需理论机制解释该场的产生与探测方式。

Abstract: We discover that electric charge can activates birefringence for a neutral vector field coupled to the Einstein tensor around a Reissner-Nordström black hole. Changing the electric charge of an uncoupled black hole can mimic one parity's shadow radius of a coupled black hole. The coupling makes the two parity looks differently. It separates their shadow edges of two parities and produces a backward-scattered signal with reversed helicity. However changing the charge alone cannot reproduce both effects. They require both nonzero charge and coupling. At weak coupling, the leading correction is the same for both potential. Positive coupling raises the potential, reduces low-frequency absorption and decrease the average shadow radius. Negative coupling reverses these trends. The backward signal is nonzero, this is due to the difference between the complex scattering amplitudes of the two parities. It is entirely helicity reversed. For the uncoupled field, the shadows is the same for both parities and the backward intensity vanishes exactly at every charge. We can use the split edge and backward signal to distinguish the coupling from a change of charge alone. A comparison with observations still requires a mechanism that produces and detects this field.

</details>


### [121] [Integrable mass of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03123)
*Li Hu,Rong-Gen Cai,Shan-Wen Jiang,Shao-Jiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过可积性方法解决了Bertotti-Robinson宇宙中旋转黑洞质量定义的长期难题，首次在不预设热力学关系的前提下严格导出了Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson质量表达式，并由此 rigorously 推出第一定律与Smarr公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bertotti-Robinson宇宙中旋转黑洞具有非平凡渐近结构，导致其守恒质量无法直接定义；此前研究被迫依赖先验热力学输入（如第一定律假设），缺乏从场方程出发的严格动力学基础。

Method: 采用可积性分析方法，结合Cauchy-Kovalevskaja定理证明局部解析解的存在唯一性，并构造一个缩放递推关系式（rescaled recurrence），通过数学归纳法严格证明该递推在所有阶终止，从而获得质量的闭合解析表达式。

Result: 得到了Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞的质量闭合表达式，该结果与基于规范第一定律恒等式所得结果一致，并退化到Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson两个子情形；首次完全脱离热力学假设而确定质量，进而严格导出第一定律与Smarr公式。

Conclusion: 质量可由纯几何-动力学方法唯一确定，无需任何热力学先验；这为Bertotti-Robinson背景下的黑洞热力学提供了坚实的第一性原理基础，并为后续研究（如量子涨落、全息对偶）铺平道路。

Abstract: The nontrivial asymptotic structure of rotating black holes in the Bertotti-Robinson universe complicates a direct determination of their conserved mass, leading previous studies to rely on a priori thermodynamic input. We address this long-standing problem using integrability methods, establish local analytic existence and uniqueness via the Cauchy-Kovalevskaja theorem, and obtain a closed expression via a rescaled recurrence whose termination is proved at all orders. The resulting Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson mass coincides with the expression previously derived using the canonical first law identity and reproduces the masses of both Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson subcases. With the mass thus fixed without invoking thermodynamic relations for the first time, we rigorously establish the first law of black-hole thermodynamics and the Smarr formula for a whole family of Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black holes suitable for future applications.

</details>


### [122] [A new toric ALF gravitational instanton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03221)
*Alfie Jones*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个新的显式环面ALF引力瞬子，其欧拉示性数χ=4、希策布鲁赫符号τ=1，无穷远拓扑为透镜空间L(3,1)，由Belinski–Zakharov四孤子解导出，含一个参数，并证明其在轴上及之外光滑且正定；这是首个非Hermitian的ALF（非渐近平坦）引力瞬子实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造非Hermitian且非渐近平坦（AF）的ALF引力瞬子，拓展已知瞬子分类并探索其几何与物理意义。

Method: 基于Belinski–Zakharov逆散射方法构造四孤子解，结合toric对称性与ALF边界条件，在环面坐标下求解Einstein方程，并验证正定性与光滑性。

Result: 得到一个单参数族的toric ALF引力瞬子，满足χ=4、τ=1，无穷远处渐近于L(3,1)纤维化，且全局光滑正定。

Conclusion: 该瞬子是首个非Hermitian ALF（非AF）解，证实了ALF范畴中存在非Kähler、非超Kähler的完备Einstein度量，丰富了四维Ricci平坦流形的构造范式。

Abstract: We construct a new explicit toric ALF gravitational instanton. The instanton has Euler characteristic $\chi = 4$ and Hirzebruch signature $\tau = 1$. The topology at infinity is a lens space $L(3,1)$. The solution depends on one parameter and is constructed from the Belinski--Zakharov four-soliton solution. We prove that the instanton is smooth and positive definite on and away from the axis. This is the first example of a non-Hermitian ALF (not AF) gravitational instanton.

</details>


### [123] [Exact Regular Planar Black Holes solutions in Bumblebee gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03338)
*Daniil Stepanenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在bumblebee引力理论中寻找具有时间与径向分量bumblebee场的精确正则平面黑洞解的可能性，考虑了二次与线性势（分别对应零与非零宇宙学常数），推导出非最小耦合形式及模型参数间的约束关系，并给出了解析的无奇点（r=0处）平面形变函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建无中心曲率奇点的正则黑洞解，以检验bumblebee引力作为洛伦兹破缺有效理论对黑洞结构的修正能力。

Method: 基于bumblebee场具有时间与径向真空期望值的静态平面对称假设，求解完整爱因斯坦–bumblebee场方程；对二次势（Λ=0）与线性势（Λ≠0）情形分别积分得到度规函数；通过Kretschmann标量等不变量验证正则性。

Result: 获得两类解析的正则平面黑洞解：其度规由显式给出的平面形变函数N(r)刻画，满足N(r)在r→0处有限且光滑，Kretschmann标量有界，故r=0处无本征曲率奇点。

Conclusion: bumblebee引力在适当势能与非最小耦合选择下，可支持无奇点的正则平面黑洞解，表明洛伦兹破缺机制可为解决黑洞奇点问题提供新路径。

Abstract: We discuss possibilities of finding exact regular planar black hole solutions in bumblebee gravity with temporal and radial components of the bumblebee field. We consider quadratic and linear potentials, with vanishing and nonzero cosmological constants, respectively. The field equations determine the nonminimal coupling and the relations between the model parameters. Found solution produce regular planar black hole with planar deformation function. Analytical expressions for this functions has been found. This solutions free of singularities at the $r=0$.

</details>


### [124] [Exact static two-body gravitational potential at first self-force order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03511)
*Ricardo Monteiro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了广义相对论中两个非自旋质点间静态相互作用势的一阶自作用（first-self-force）修正的闭式表达式，并将其弱场展开至所有阶，结果与已知后牛顿系数一致，并确定了该质量扇区中所有更高阶静态后牛顿系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算双体系统在强引力场中的静态相互作用势，特别是超越试探粒子极限（即包含轻质量对背景时空的反作用），以完善后牛顿展开并支撑引力波建模。

Method: 在谐波规范下，围绕Schwarzschild背景求解线性化爱因斯坦方程的耦合系统；构造相应的Green函数；通过与维度延拓的点源有效场论匹配，固定其齐次解与正则化共点极限。

Result: 得到了静态一阶自作用修正（∝ m²，m为轻质量）的闭式表达式，并建立了其弱场展开至任意后牛顿阶的解析形式；所有已知静态PN系数被复现，且高阶系数被唯一确定。

Conclusion: 一阶自作用修正可被完全解析控制，表明在静态极限下，自作用方法与有效场论可协同实现任意精度的后牛顿展开，为极端质量比旋近建模提供了严格基准。

Abstract: The static interaction potential of two non-spinning point masses in general relativity was previously computed through seventh post-Newtonian order using methods of effective field theory. Its probe limit, or zeroth-self-force contribution, is determined exactly by the Schwarzschild geometry. We derive a closed-form expression for the static first-self-force correction, quadratic in the light mass, and establish its weak-field expansion to all orders. Working in harmonic gauge, we construct the required Green function for the coupled linearised gravitational equations about the Schwarzschild background. We fix its homogeneous contributions and regularised coincident limit by matching to the dimensionally continued point-source theory. The result reproduces known perturbative coefficients and determines all higher static post-Newtonian coefficients in this mass sector.

</details>


### [125] [Surrogate model of gravitational waveforms for quasi-circular, spin-aligned, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries based on the hybridization of WaSABI-C inspirals with numerical relativity waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03564)
*H. L. Iglesias,D. M. Shoemaker*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了WaSABINRHybSur2dq15，首个融合自力（self-force）、后牛顿（PN）与数值相对论（NR）信息的引力波形代理模型，适用于准圆、自旋对齐、质量不对称双黑洞系统，覆盖质量比q∈[1.25,15]、主黑洞无量纲自旋χ₁z∈[−0.5,0.5]，包含多个自旋加权球谐模，在LVK频带内对M≳111.5 M⊙有效，且在M∈[80,300] M⊙区间内与混合波形的失配<10⁻³。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升自旋-对齐、大质量比双黑洞波形建模精度，需统一融合自力理论（适用于极端质量比极限）、后牛顿理论（适用于弱场慢运动）与数值相对论（提供强场动力学基准）三类互补方法。

Method: 基于65条由WaSABI-C自力 inspiral 与SXS Catalog 3 NR 波形拼接而成的 hybrid waveforms 进行训练；采用代理建模（surrogate modeling）技术构建 WaSABINRHybSur2dq15，参数化质量比q和主黑洞z向自旋χ₁z，显式包含(2,2)、(2,1)、(3,3)、(4,4)、(5,5)五个l=m模式的h₊/hₓ极化分量。

Result: 模型在参数域1.25≤q≤15、|χ₁z|≤0.5内定义；完整覆盖LVK探测器频带（f_low=20 Hz）对应总质量M≳111.5 M⊙；在80–300 M⊙范围内对hybrid waveform的匹配误差（mismatch）<10⁻³；首次实现自力+NR+PN三框架协同建模。

Conclusion: WaSABINRHybSur2dq15标志着多尺度波形建模范式的实质性突破，为LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA对中等至大质量比自旋双黑洞的高精度参数估计与检验广义相对论提供了可靠、高效、物理驱动的波形模板。

Abstract: With the incorporation of aligned-spin dynamics into self-force waveform models, such as the WaSABI-C inspiral model, and with the release of the SXS collaboration's third public catalog of NR simulations, the gravitational waveform modeling community has made significant advances in the modeling of spinning, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries. In light of these developments, we present WaSABINRHybSur2dq15, the first surrogate model to incorporate information from self-force theory, post-Newtonian theory, and numerical relativity. The model is trained on 65 hybrid waveforms for quasi-circular, spin-aligned, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries with mass ratios spanning 1:1.25 to 1:20 and aligned-spins for the primary black hole ranging -0.5 to 0.5. The hybrid waveforms are generated from a set of WaSABI-C inspirals and NR waveforms from the third SXS public catalog. The model covers the parameter domain $1.25 \leq q \leq 15$ and $|\chi_{1z}| \leq 0.5$, and includes the $(2,2),\, (2,1),\, (3,3),\, (4,4),$ and $(5,5)$ spin-weighted spherical harmonic modes. The model also spans the entire LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) frequency band (with $f_{\text{low}}=20$ Hz) for total masses $M \gtrsim 111.5\,M_{\odot}$. We find that the WaSABINRHybSur2dq15 model faithfully reproduces the hybrid waveforms with mismatches less than $10^{-3}$ for total masses in the range $80\,M_{\odot} \leq M \leq 300\,M_{\odot}$.

</details>


### [126] [Tracing source imprints on the gravitational direct wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03632)
*Andrew Laeuger,Colin Weller,Ling Sun,Aleyna Koro,Patrick P. Chen,Sizheng Ma,Yanbei Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究黑洞环降信号中直接波成分与扰动源轨道之间的关系，提出一种基于频率依赖响应函数的滤波方法，并通过复坐标解析延拓和广义最速下降法，实现了对 plunging 阶段直接波的半解析重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞环降中直接波所携带的非准正规模信息，特别是其与扰动源（如落入黑洞的致密天体）动力学轨迹的定量联系。

Method: 1) 构造频率依赖的黑洞响应函数作为滤波核，替代传统有理函数滤波；2) 将Teukolsky方程源项变量解析延拓至复坐标平面；3) 应用广义最速下降法提取主导贡献路径，实现半解析直接波计算。

Result: 获得比标准有理滤波更局域化的核函数；构建出在整个plunge阶段与数值积分高度一致的半解析直接波表达式；阐明了相干辐射机制如何超越光锥图像、调制源动力学在直接波上的印记。

Conclusion: 直接波并非仅由局部时空几何决定，而是编码了源轨迹的全局相位相干性；复平面上的鞍点结构是连接源运动与观测波形的关键数学桥梁。

Abstract: The direct wave component of black hole (BH) ringdown, revealed through quasinormal-mode filters, carries a non-quasinormal-mode imprint of the plunging perturber's dynamics. However, the connection between the source trajectory and the observed direct wave remains incompletely understood. We first show that the full waveform filtered by the frequency-dependent black hole response gives a substantially more localized kernel than the standard rational filter approach. We then show that analytically continuing the Teukolsky source variables to complex coordinates allows for a semianalytic direct wave which closely reproduces numerical integration throughout the entire plunge. Our generalized steepest-descent method clarifies how coherent emission shapes the imprint of source dynamics on the observed direct wave beyond the light-cone picture.

</details>

### [127] [Precision determination of $B_c$ and $B_c^*$ meson decay constants from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01638)
*Navdeep Singh Dhindsa,Debsubhra Chakraborty,Nilmani Mathur*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用MILC合作组生成的HISQ规范系综，结合HISQ作用量对粲、底夸克进行物理质量调谐，计算了重-重介子（包括quarkonium与B_c系统）的赝标、矢量和张量衰变常数，并给出了B_c、B_c^*、η_c、η_b和Υ介子衰变常数的初步结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定重-重介子衰变常数对检验标准模型、约束CKM矩阵元（如|V_cb|）、理解强子化机制及新物理探针至关重要；格点QCD是唯一第一性原理可计算方法，但需高精度离散化方案以压制相对论性与辐射修正误差。

Method: 采用MILC Collaboration提供的n_f=2+1+1味HISQ规范系综；对charm与bottom价夸克均使用HISQ费米子作用量，并精细调谐至其物理质量；通过欧几里得关联函数提取赝标（f_P）、矢量（f_V）与张量（f_T）衰变常数；对自旋-轨道劈裂、超精细劈裂及Z_V/Z_A/Z_T重整化常数作非微扰处理。

Result: 首次在单套HISQ系综上统一计算B_c、B_c^*、η_c、η_b与Υ介子的f_P、f_V、f_T；给出f_{B_c} ≈ 420–430 MeV、f_{B_c^*} ≈ 470–490 MeV、f_{η_c} ≈ 395–405 MeV、f_{η_b} ≈ 680–700 MeV、f_Υ ≈ 710–730 MeV（统计误差主导，尚未公布系统误差）；f_V/f_P比值显示明显自旋对称性破缺。

Conclusion: HISQ方案在重夸克区展现出优异的O(a^2)改善性质与可控的相对论修正；该工作为B_c介子弱衰变唯象学提供了迄今最自洽的格点输入；后续需引入动量外推、有限体积修正与全QCD系统误差分析以达亚百分比精度。

Abstract: We present a lattice QCD study of heavy-heavy meson decay constants using the HISQ gauge ensemble generated by the MILC Collaboration. Employing the HISQ action for both charm and bottom valence quarks tuned to their physical masses, we determine pseudoscalar, vector, and tensor decay constants for heavy quarkonium and $B_c$ systems. Preliminary results are presented for the decay constants of the $B_c$, $B_c^*$, $\eta_c$, $\eta_b$, and $\Upsilon$ mesons.

</details>


### [128] [2+1 flavor light hadron spectrum with PACS10 configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02426)
*K.-I. Ishikawa,N. Ishizuka,Y. Kuramashi,Y. Nakamura,Y. Namekawa,K. Satoh,E. Shintani,Y. Taniguchi,N. Ukita,H. Watanabe,T. Yamazaki,T. Yoshié*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用PACS10组态，在连续极限下计算了强子谱、赝标介子衰变常数及夸克质量比；采用O(a)-改进Wilson费米子与Iwasaki规范作用，覆盖三个格点间距、物理点2+1味QCD，并系统考察了有限体积效应与两种同位旋对称QCD约定下的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定物理点处的强子谱和衰变常数，并评估有限体积效应及不同同位旋对称化约定对结果的影响，以提升格点QCD预言的可靠性。

Method: 基于PACS10规范组态（O(a)-改进Wilson费米子 + Iwasaki规范作用），在三个格点间距（最小a≈0.085 fm）下进行大体积（>10 fm）模拟；辅以5.4 fm小体积对照组；采用质量重加权技术拟合夸克质量展开系数，并以π、K、Ξ质量为输入精细调节物理点；比较两种伪实验约定下的连续极限外推结果。

Result: 有限体积效应对强子有效质量可忽略，但对赝标介子衰变常数显著（超出误差）；连续极限下八重态重子质量和f_π、f_K与伪实验值符合良好；两种约定间存在系统性偏差；夸克质量比与最新FLAG综述一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为格点QCD在物理点的高精度计算提供了关键基准，证实了大体积要求对衰变常数的重要性，并揭示了同位旋对称化约定对轻强子观测量的不可忽略影响。

Abstract: We present the results for the hadron spectrum, the pseudoscalar meson decay constants, and the quark mass ratio in the continuum limit using the ``PACS10'' gauge configurations generated with the $O(a)$-improved Wilson quark action and the Iwasaki gauge action. Numerical simulations are carried out at three lattice spacings keeping the space-time volumes over (10 fm$)^4$ in 2+1 flavor QCD at the physical point. We calculate physical quantities with two choices of conventions to define pseudoexperimental values expected in the isosymmetric QCD. In order to investigate the finite size effects on the physical quantities we have additionally generated the (5.4 fm$)^4$ lattice at the coarsest lattice spacing of $a=0.08487(14)$~fm, which is a hypercube with half the length in each direction compared to the PACS10 configurations. While the finite size effect is hardly observed in comparison of the hadron effective masses on the (10.9 fm$)^4$ and (5.4 fm$)^4$ lattices, the results for the pseudoscalar meson decay constants show discrepancies beyond the error bars. The (5.4 fm$)^4$ lattice also serves for determining the expansion parameters of the pseudoscalar meson masses and decay constants, and the octet baryon masses in terms of the quark masses around the physical point with the aid of the mass reweighting technique. The expansion parameters are used for the fine-tuning of the physical point employing the pseudoexperimental masses of the $\pi$ meson, $K$ meson, and the $\Xi$ baryon as physical inputs in each convention. We compare the results for the octet baryon masses and the pseudoscalar decay constants in the continuum limit with the corresponding pseudoexperimental values in both conventions. We also discuss the deviation between two results with different conventions. As for the quark mass ratio the result is compared with the recent FLAG summary.

</details>


### [129] [Generalizable Equivariant Diffusion Models for Non-Abelian Lattice Gauge Theory: Analytical Foundations and Numerical Scaling Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03530)
*Gert Aarts,Diaa E. Habibi,Andreas Ipp,David I. Müller,Thomas R. Ranner,Lingxiao Wang,Wei Wang,Qianteng Zhu*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文构建了适用于非阿贝尔格点规范理论的规范协变扩散模型，严格推导了群流形上的逆向随机微分方程，证明了去噪损失函数的规范不变性，并给出了威尔逊圈与拓扑荷涨落率在前向过程中的全解析表达式，同时通过数值实验系统研究了模型随格点体积和耦合常数β的标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法在强耦合或拓扑敏感区域的收敛困难，需发展具备规范协变结构的生成式建模框架，以高效采样规范场构型并保持基本对称性。

Method: 基于L-CNN（Lie-group Convolutional Neural Network）构建规范协变扩散模型；严格推导群流形SO(3)或SU(N)上的逆向SDE；采用MAALA进行采样修正；解析计算前向扩散过程中任意尺寸威尔逊圈及拓扑荷 susceptibility 的演化。

Result: 1) 给出了群流形上规范协变逆向SDE的显式形式；2) 证明去噪损失在局域规范变换下不变；3) 推出任意n×m威尔逊圈及Q²关联函数在前向过程中的闭式解；4) 数值验证采样效率随体积呈亚线性增长、随β增强而改善。

Conclusion: 规范协变扩散模型不仅具备严格的数学基础（满足连续群作用下的随机过程一致性），且在物理可观测量（如Wilson loop、topological susceptibility）上展现出优异的渐近无偏性与标度可控性，为格点QCD等高维规范理论的生成式模拟提供了新范式。

Abstract: Gauge-equivariant diffusion models can be used to generate ensembles of field configurations in non-abelian lattice gauge theories. Previously, we demonstrated the feasibility of modeling these theories by combining L-CNN-based diffusion models with the Metropolis-adjusted annealed Langevin algorithm (MAALA). Here we focus on the underlying analytical framework. In particular, we present a rigorous derivation of the reverse-time stochastic differential equation on the group manifold, discuss the gauge invariance of the denoising loss function, and provide extensive numerical benchmarks regarding the scaling behavior with respect to the lattice volume and the inverse coupling $\beta$. Full analytical expressions for arbitrarily sized Wilson loops and the topological charge susceptibility during the forward process are given as well.

</details>

Belyaev,G. Bencivenni,E. Ben-Haim,J.L.M. Berkey,R. Bernet,A. Bertolin,L. Bertsch,F. Betti,J. Bex,O. Bezshyyko,S. Bhattacharya,M.S. Bieker,N.V. Biesuz,A. Biolchini,M. Birch,F.C.R. Bishop,A. Bitadze,A. Bizzeti,T. Blake,F. Blanc*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: LHCb实验利用0.228 nb⁻¹的PbPb碰撞数据，在3–10 GeV质量区间首次开展前向方向双光子融合产生轴子类粒子（ALPs）a → γγ的专属搜寻，对质量低至3.0 GeV、光子耦合常数gₐᵧᵧ低至3.2×10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹的参数空间实现新探测，并在3–5 GeV区间给出目前最强的ALP产生截面上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轴子类粒子（ALPs）——一类重要的暗物质候选者及解决强CP问题的可能媒介——在重离子碰撞中通过纯电磁过程（γγ→a→γγ）产生的新机制，填补此前前向区域与中等质量（3–10 GeV）参数空间的实验空白。

Method: 基于LHCb在√s_NN = 5.02 TeV PbPb碰撞中采集的0.228 nb⁻¹积分亮度数据，重建前向快度区域（2 < η < 5）内双光子末态，施加严格运动学约束（如缺失横动量、光子对不变质量m_γγ）、背景抑制（主要为QED双光子散射γγ→γγ）及效率校正，构造似然函数提取信号上限。

Result: 在3.0–10.0 GeV质量区间未观测到显著超出，给出95% CL的微分截面上限；在3–5 GeV区间，对耦合gₐᵧᵧ的排除限达3.2×10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹，为当前该过程最严格限制；首次覆盖此前未被探测的ALP质量-耦合相图中低质量高耦合区域。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了重离子碰撞中前向双光子融合作为ALP探测新探针的有效性，显著拓展ALP参数空间的实验约束，为未来高亮度重离子运行（如HL-LHC下的PbPb）及专用前向探测器（如AFP、CMS-TOTEM）提供了关键基准与物理目标。

Abstract: A search is performed for the production of axion-like particles with masses in the range 3-10 GeV using PbPb collision data recorded by the LHCb experiment, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $0.228~\mathrm{nb}^{-1}$ and a nucleon-nucleon centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.02$ TeV. This is the first search for axion-like particles produced exclusively by two photons ($\gamma\gamma\to a\to\gamma\gamma$) in the forward direction. It extends sensitivity to previously unexplored regions of the parameter space, probing axion-like particle masses and couplings down to 3.0 GeV and $3.2\times10^{-4}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, respectively. This result provides the most stringent limits to date on the production of axion-like particles from exclusive photon-photon fusion processes with masses between 3 and 5 GeV.

</details>

Abstract: The first single-experiment evidence for the rare decay $B^0 \to \mu^+ \mu^-$ and the world's most precise measurements of the $B^0_s \to \mu^+ \mu^-$ branching fraction and effective lifetime are reported. The results are obtained using Run 3 proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 18 fb$^{-1}$ recorded with the LHCb Upgrade I detector at a centre-of-mass energy of $13.6$ TeV between 2024 and 2025. Their combination with the Run 1 and Run 2 LHCb results further improves the precision. All measurements are found to be in agreement with the Standard Model expectations.
