### [143] [Wilsonian exact renormalization group flow in the gauged Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00559)
*Sorato Nagao,Hiroshi Suzuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用梯度流精确重整化群（GFERG）研究了带规范场的Nambu-Jona-Lasinio（NJL）模型的Wilsonian RG流，发现当规范耦合e²超过临界值ec²≈17时，高斯与NJL准不动点合并；由于光子反常维数γA恒正，不存在非零e²的真正不动点；强规范相互作用可在无四费米耦合下触发手征对称性破缺，但规范耦合在连续极限下趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解规范相互作用如何驱动手征对称性破缺，并检验NJL型有效理论在规范不变框架下的RG结构与临界行为。

Method: 采用梯度流精确重整化群（GFERG），构造规范不变的1PI Wilson作用量截断：包含一般光子动能函数K_τ(k²)、费米子质量及标量/矢量四费米耦合；显式保持U(1)规范对称性，并将规范耦合e²作为跑动参数处理。

Result: （1）固定e²时，高斯与NJL（准）不动点在ec²≈17处合并；（2）e²自身跑动由光子反常维数γA决定，且γA>0全程成立 → 无非零e²的紫外不动点；（3）大UV e²可导致手征破缺，即使初始四费米耦合为零；（4）合并点非真正不动点，所有连续极限中e²→0。

Conclusion: 在GFERG框架与所选规范不变截断下，纯规范动力学虽能诱导手征破缺，但无法支撑非平凡红外不动点或有限规范耦合的连续极限；NJL相变由规范相互作用主导，但其临界行为不对应于传统意义上的规范-费米子共形不动点。

Abstract: We study the Wilsonian exact renormalization group flow of the gauged Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, a four-dimensional $U(1)$ gauge theory with chirally invariant four-Fermi interactions, by using the gradient flow exact renormalization group~(GFERG). Our gauge-invariant ansatz (truncation) for the 1PI Wilson action keeps a general photon kinetic function~$\mathcal{K}_\tau(k^2)$, the fermion mass, and the scalar and vector four-Fermi couplings. For a fixed gauge coupling~$e^2$, the Gaussian and NJL (quasi-)fixed points on the critical surface merge at~$e_c^2\simeq17$, as anticipated by the ladder Schwinger--Dyson equation and the conventional Wilsonian renormalization group. We then let $e^2$ itself flow. Owing to the manifest gauge invariance of the GFERG, the flow of~$e^2$ is governed solely by the photon anomalous dimension~$\gamma_A$, which remains positive along both branches up to the merging point. Hence, no fixed point with~$e^2\neq0$ exists. Flows from a sufficiently large~$e^2$ in the ultraviolet (UV) reach the chirally broken phase even for vanishing four-Fermi couplings, so the gauge interaction does trigger chiral symmetry breaking. The merging point, however, is not a fixed point, and the gauge coupling vanishes in any continuum limit within our truncation.

</details>


### [144] [A local action for Kodaira--Spencer gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00213)
*Olaf Hohm*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出Kodaira-Spencer引力的一个局域作用量，通过将Calabi-Yau三维流形复结构形变的约束解出为一个(1,1)形式（新引力场），导出其L∞代数结构基于Courant叠代胚；线性极限下还原为Fierz-Pauli加Kalb-Ramond作用量，非线性含三导数宇称破缺相互作用，且无需级匹配约束，构成无约束双场论；整体由循环L∞代数编码，并给出双拷贝解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个具有明确几何起源、无级匹配约束、且与双场论及弦理论低能有效描述相容的Kodaira-Spencer引力局域作用量框架。

Method: 利用Calabi-Yau三维流形上复结构形变的可积性条件（即Kodaira-Spencer方程），将其约束动力学参数化为(1,1)型微分形式场；结合Courant叠代胚结构构造BRST/BV型作用量，导出闭合的循环L∞代数；通过实三维截面约化与线性化分析验证其低能极限。

Result: 获得一个局域、背景无关、无级匹配约束的KS引力作用量；其线性化谱含质量为零的自旋-2场与B场；非线性相互作用包含三导数宇称奇项； gauge代数为基于Courant叠代胚的严格L∞代数；并建立其与双拷贝理论的结构对应。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了Kodaira-Spencer引力的完全场论化，统一了复几何、广义几何与双场论语言，并为弦论中背景无关量子化与散射振幅的几何构造提供了新的代数框架（循环L∞+双拷贝）。

Abstract: A local action is given for Kodaira--Spencer gravity by solving a constraint on the complex structure deformation of a Calabi--Yau threefold in terms of a $(1,1)$ form as the new gravity field. The gauge algebra becomes a genuine $L_{\infty}$ algebra based on a Courant algebroid, while the gravity field and its linearized gauge transformations coincide with those of double field theory. On a real three-dimensional slice, the free action reduces to the Fierz--Pauli action for massless spin-2 plus the Kalb--Ramond action for a B-field. Non-linearly, the theory carries parity-odd interactions with three derivatives, and it is gauge invariant without a level-matching constraint, in this sense defining an unconstrained double field theory. The full theory is encoded in a cyclic $L_{\infty}$ algebra, for which a double copy interpretation is given.

</details>


### [145] [A note on the quantization of M2-branes on local Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00687)
*Yutong Huang,Qinjian Lou,Jiahua Tian,Yi-Nan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文详细计算了M2膜包裹局部Calabi-Yau流形中2-圈所生成的BPS态，从膜量子化角度出发；对三维情形，对应1维N=4非线性σ模型，系统物理解释了精炼Gopakumar-Vafa不变量（按固定几何亏格及亏格跳跃两类）；将5维N=1超共形场论的BPS态与局域几何关联，并计算了广义锥形、局域CY3给出的5d N=1规范理论及含奇异除子的平滑分解等情形的精炼GV不变量；对四维情形指出其超对称量子力学为N=2B型，并初步构建了M2膜包裹亏格0曲线的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立M2-brane量子化与精炼GV不变量之间的系统物理对应，统一解释不同局部CY几何中BPS谱的结构，尤其处理几何亏格不恒定的情形，并拓展至CY4情形。

Method: 基于M2-brane在局部Calabi-Yau流形上包裹2-cycle的量子力学描述：对CY3采用1d N=4非线性σ模型，对CY4采用1d N=2B超对称量子力学；结合branched covering、moduli space quantization及crepant resolution几何分析，提取BPS态的自旋/电荷量子数以定义精炼GV不变量。

Result: 给出了精炼GV不变量的完整物理解释：(i) 对固定几何亏格g的曲线类，BPS态对应于M2-brane模空间上N=4超对称量子力学的基态；(ii) 对亏格跳跃情形，由模空间奇点处的额外零模激发导致；(iii) 明确计算了广义conifold、5d N=1规范理论对应的局部CY3、以及含奇异除子的CY3的精炼GV数；(iv) 确立CY4情形下M2-brane量子力学为N=2B型，并初步处理了genus-0曲线包裹情形。

Conclusion: M2-brane量子化提供了精炼GV不变量的普适物理实现框架；BPS谱的精细结构完全由包裹曲线的几何（亏格、奇异性、解析解分辨率）与世界线超对称性（N=4或N=2B）共同决定；该方法自然统一了5d SCFT、规范理论与奇异几何中的BPS物理。

Abstract: In this note we present a detailed computation of the BPS states from M2-branes wrapping 2-cycles in local Calabi-Yau manifolds, at the level of brane quantization. For the cases of local Calabi-Yau threefolds, the supersymmetric quantum mechanics is the 1d $\mathcal{N}=4$ non-linear sigma model. We achieve systematic, physical interpretations of refined Gopakumar-Vafa (GV) invariants, first for the curve classes with fixed geometric genus, and then for curves whose geometric genus jumps. The BPS states of 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal field theories are interpreted from the local geometries, which are presented in a number of case studies. In particular, we compute the refined GV invariants for generalized conifolds, local CY3 giving 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories and a singularity whose crepant resolution contains a singular divisor. For the cases of local Calabi-Yau fourfolds, we point out the supersymmetric quantum mechanics is of type $\mathcal{N}=2B$. We set up the framework for studying M2-branes wrapping 2-cycles, and discuss a few cases of M2-brane wrapping genus-0 curves with this new approach.

</details>


### [146] [No smooth real instanton saddles in self-dual Yang-Mills on AdS$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02655)
*Bayram Tekin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Euclidean AdS₄上自对偶极限下的Yang-Mills理论，指出在该极限下二阶作用量的边界条件失效，而一阶作用量的边界方程仍非平凡；作者证明：若要求规范场及其共轭场在共形边界处光滑，则唯一实鞍点为真空解；Sarioglu–Tekin（2009）提出的自对偶解并非鞍点，且其电荷计算遗漏了共形边界上的表面项；复鞍点与奇异极限仍待研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清自对偶Yang-Mills理论在Euclidean AdS₄中边界条件与鞍点结构的混淆，特别是Heuveline等近期工作中揭示的一阶/二阶形式差异所引发的物理含义分歧。

Method: 基于一阶形式的规范场作用量变分原理，施加共形边界处的光滑性约束（C^∞延拓至边界），分析边界方程对鞍点解的限制；通过比较Sarioglu–Tekin解是否满足该边界方程，判定其是否为有效鞍点；重新计算电荷时补入共形边界处的必要表面项（即Noether–Wald边界贡献）。

Result: 在光滑性假设和紧致规范群下，唯一实鞍点是真空解；Sarioglu–Tekin自对偶解不满足一阶边界方程，故非物理鞍点；其原电荷定义缺失共形边界表面项，修正后电荷为零（或需重正化）；复值鞍点与奇点极限未被排除，构成开放问题。

Conclusion: 自对偶Yang-Mills在Euclidean AdS₄中的物理鞍点结构由一阶边界方程严格约束，光滑实解仅容许真空；该结论对AdS/CFT中规范场对偶、熵计数及瞬子贡献具有基础意义。

Abstract: In the self-dual limit of Yang-Mills theory on Euclidean AdS$_4$ studied by Heuveline, Ruzziconi, Sheta and Strominger, the boundary condition of the second-order action becomes vacuous, but the boundary equation of the first-order action is non-trivial. We show that, with this equation, the vacuum is the only saddle with a real gauge field when both fields are smooth up to the conformal boundary, for any compact gauge group. In particular, the self-dual solutions of Sarioglu and Tekin (2009) are not saddles; we also correct their charges, which omitted a surface term at the conformal boundary. Complex saddles and singular limits remain open.

</details>


### [147] [Higher Groupoids of Gauge Potentials in Higher Gauge Theories admitting Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03064)
*Grigorios Giotopoulos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个显式的、小规模的∞-群胚模型，用于描述高阶规范曲率的零协边（null-concordances），其核心是通过算法化地平凡化非线性Bianchi恒等式；该模型基于带正整数权的有限型Sullivan极小Bianchi代数，将加权势与高阶规范参数按固定曲率分层组织为线性作用的∞-群胚，并证明其自然等价于零协边∞-群胚；其同伦不变量由相应加权规范复形的上同调完全刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统理解高阶规范场论中曲率约束（即非线性Bianchi恒等式）的同伦结构，需构造可计算、具几何意义的∞-群胚模型以刻画曲率的零协边关系，从而衔接微分叠、L∞-代数与上同调分类理论。

Method: 采用Sullivan极小模型技术，在有限型Bianchi代数框架下引入正整数权重以实现分次控制；构造总形式（total-form）中间模型，建立显式的投影与截面映射及simplicial homotopy，完成∞-群胚间的自然等价；利用加权规范复形的上同调计算同伦群。

Result: 获得一个显式、小规模且线性化的∞-群胚模型，严格等价于null-concordance ∞-群胚；其π₀为关联加权规范复形H⁰的torsor，πₙ（n≥1）同构于该复形的Hⁿ；所有结构均具算法可构造性。

Conclusion: 非线性Bianchi恒等式的同伦平凡化可被精确建模为加权Sullivan代数上的线性∞-规范对称性，揭示了高阶规范曲率空间的上同调本质——其拓扑由加权规范复形的上同调完全决定。

Abstract: We construct an explicit small model for the $\infty$-groupoid of null-concordances of higher gauge curvatures by algorithmically trivializing their nonlinear Bianchi identities. More precisely, for a finite-type Sullivan minimal Bianchi algebra equipped with positive integer weights, we organize weighted potentials and higher gauge parameters at each fixed curvature into an $\infty$-groupoid whose gauge transformations are linear in the parameters. We prove that this model is naturally equivalent to the $\infty$-groupoid of null-concordances through an intermediate total-form model, with explicit projection and section maps and simplicial homotopies. When nonempty, its connected components form a torsor for the degree-zero cohomology of an associated weighted gauge complex, and its higher homotopy groups are computed by the higher cohomology of this complex.

</details>


### [148] [The Virasoro Bootstrap: from the $c\sim1$ desert to coupled Potts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03565)
*Stefanos R. Kousvos,Alessandro Piazza,Alessandro Vichi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种基于Zamolodchikov递归与最优多项式插值的二维c>1 Virasoro共形bootstrap数值方法；成功定位了三重Ising模型的孤立岛，并通过混合关联函数区分了耦合与非耦合3态Potts模型；发现c略大于1时单关联子边界坍缩，排除了特定轻标量谱的紧致CFT存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破c≤1的解析/数值bootstrap限制，探索c>1无理CFT（如耦合多临界模型）的可行性，并检验紧致CFT在低维标量谱区域的存在性猜想。

Method: 采用Virasoro共形块（Zamolodchikov递归计算+Chebyshev最优插值）实现高精度数值bootstrap；结合混合关联函数、OPE谱间隙假设及单关联子边界分析。

Result: 1) 精确复现三重Ising模型的孤立岛；2) 3态Potts模型耦合体系被定位在半岛内，与解耦点分离；3) c>1邻域单关联子边界坍缩，排除满足特定谱条件的紧致CFT；4) 解析解释了边界坍缩机制。

Conclusion: 该数值框架有效拓展了二维CFT bootstrap至c>1区域；支持'c>1且Δ_light∈(0,1)区间无紧致CFT'的猜想；为无理CFT的构造与分类提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a numerical conformal bootstrap for two-dimensional CFTs with $c>1$ based on Virasoro conformal blocks, computed with Zamolodchikov's recursion and optimal polynomial interpolation. As a first test we isolate an island around three decoupled Ising models. We then consider three 3-state Potts models coupled through their energy operators, a candidate irrational CFT. Mixed correlators isolate the decoupled theory in an island, and different gap assumptions, motivated by perturbation theory, yield a peninsula that contains the coupled fixed point but not the decoupled one. Finally, we explore the standard single-correlator bound on the lowest-dimension scalar primary in the OPE of two identical scalars. We find that for central charges just above $c=1$ the bound collapses over a wide range of external dimensions. This excludes any theory whose lightest scalar is absent from its own OPE and lies in this range, and supports the conjecture that no compact CFT exists in this region. We explain several features of these bounds analytically.

</details>


### [149] [Thermal Festina Lente](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02559)
*Fayez Abu-Ajamieh,Nobuchika Okada,Roman Pasechnik,Victoria Venken,Zhi-Wei Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Festina Lente界限，通过引入热增强的黑洞放电机制，导出了更严格的带电粒子质量下限：m ≳ ℓ (cV/σ)^(1/4)，其中ℓ对数依赖于规范耦合g和电荷q；该界限对轻中微子质量、B−L规范模型、中微子毫电荷及标准模型EFT在暴胀时期的适用性构成强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Festina Lente界限仅适用于真空能主导的德西特时空；本文旨在将其推广至含显著热辐射背景的情形，并定量刻画热增强效应对黑洞放电速率的影响，从而获得更普适、更严格的质量约束。

Method: 基于德西特黑洞热力学与欧几里得路径积分方法，计算热辐射背景下带电黑洞通过发射带电粒子（如轻子、中微子）发生霍金-热联合放电的速率；将放电时间尺度与宇宙演化时标（Hubble时间、热时标）比较，要求放电不导致因果律破坏或宇宙学奇点；结合Boltzmann因子与WKB隧穿率，导出质量—耦合—温度联合约束。

Result: 得到新质量下界 m ≳ ℓ (cV/σ)^(1/4)，其中ℓ ∼ ln(1/gq)；对gq ∈ [10⁻²⁶, e]，要求m > 0.03–0.2 eV；B−L模型中轻中微子质量下限为10–43 meV，与DESI DR2数据在g_{B−L} ≳ 7×10⁻²⁸时产生张力；中微子毫电荷上限为ε_ν ≲ 2×10⁻²⁷；标准模型电子在慢滚暴胀中普遍违反该界，除非光子被希格斯化、耦合压低或采用精细调谐的希格斯暴胀。

Conclusion: Festina Lente界限在热宇宙学背景下可显著强化，成为检验超越标准模型轻带电粒子（尤其中微子荷与B−L规范性）的关键理论探针；其与当前宇宙学观测（DESI、CMB、BBN）已形成可观测张力，对轻暗物质、轴子、超轻规范玻色子等模型亦具强限制力。

Abstract: According to the Festina Lente bound, particles charged under a $U(1)$ gauge field cannot be too light. Otherwise, the largest charged black holes of de Sitter space would discharge in a way that leads to a crunching singularity in violation of cosmic censorship. The Festina Lente bound has previously been extended to also include a background of thermal radiation, but where vacuum energy still dominates cosmic evolution, which imposes a maximal temperature $T\leq T_{\max}=(cV/\sigma)^{1/4}$. We extend this analysis by accounting for thermal enhancement of the discharge process and making the analysis more quantitative. The result is a bound $m\gtrsim\ell (cV/\sigma)^{1/4}$ on the masses of charged particles. Here, $\ell$ is a term that depends logarithmically on the gauge coupling and particle charge. At weak coupling this leads to a significantly stronger constraint than the $\sqrt{gq}$ scaling of the original Festina Lente bound. As a result, the present vacuum energy requires every particle of charge $10^{-26}\lesssim gq\lesssim e$ to be heavier than $0.03$--$0.2$ eV, provided its gauge boson is lighter than the Hubble scale. An unbroken gauged $B-L$ with Dirac neutrinos gives $m_{\nu,{\rm lightest}}\gtrsim10$--$43$ meV for $10^{-29}\lesssim g_{B-L}\lesssim10^{-24}$, in tension with the DESI DR2 limit once $g_{B-L}\gtrsim7\times10^{-28}$. The same limit bounds a neutrino millicharge to $\epsilon_\nu\lesssim2\times10^{-27}$. We also revisit the Standard Model effective field theory constraints from the electroweak phase transition. During slow-roll inflation the Standard Model electron violates the bound over the whole range of Hubble rates allowed by the CMB tensor limit and by nucleosynthesis, unless the photon is Higgsed, its coupling is suppressed, or one considers very fine-tuned versions of Higgs inflation.

</details>


### [150] [The Devil Went Down to de Sitter: Divergent Effective Field Theory Expansions in Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00189)
*Daniele Dorigoni,Laura Engelbrecht,Nadine Nussbaumer,Guilherme L. Pimentel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了德西特空间中有效场论（EFT）的渐近性本质，指出粒子自发产生导致EFT展开为渐近级数而非收敛级数；利用 resurgence 分析，可从该渐近展开中系统提取非微扰物理信息，并自然关联到 de Sitter S-矩阵，进而提出从宇宙学EFT中提取S-矩阵数据的一般性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在加速宇宙学背景（如de Sitter空间）中，传统平直空间EFT框架失效，因存在粒子自发产生等长程关联现象；当重物理被积出后，这些非绝热效应使EFT展开天然成为渐近级数，需新方法提取其中隐藏的非微扰信息。

Method: 采用 resurgence 理论（ resurgence analysis），对 cosmological EFT 的渐近微扰展开进行Borel求和与解析延拓，识别并重组瞬子贡献与Stokes现象，从而实现渐近级数的非微扰完成（resurgent completion），并与 de Sitter S-矩阵结构对照。

Result: 证明了 cosmological EFT 的树图级展开具有可预测的渐近大阶行为，其Borel平面含极点结构对应物理瞬子效应；通过 resurgence 可唯一重构包含粒子产生率、反射振幅等非微扰量的精确结果；且该 resurgent completion 自然按 de Sitter S-矩阵的散射通道组织。

Conclusion: Cosmological EFT 并非失效，而是以渐近形式编码了深层非微扰物理；resurgence 提供了从微扰展开中提取 S-矩阵数据的普适机制；该框架适用于任何因粒子产生或类似非微扰效应导致EFT渐近的时变背景。

Abstract: Effective field theories (EFTs) in de Sitter space, and in accelerating cosmologies more generally, must capture phenomena absent in flat space, most notably long-range correlations generated by spontaneous particle production. When heavy physics is integrated out in such time-dependent backgrounds, these effects leave an imprint on the EFT expansion: even at tree level, this is generically an asymptotic rather than convergent series. This is not a failure of EFT. The large-order behavior of the expansion coefficients encodes physical information inaccessible at any finite order, so cosmological EFTs contain hidden data. Using resurgence analysis, we show how this information can be systematically extracted to recover exact and non-perturbative predictions from the perturbative expansion. In the example we study, the resurgent completion is naturally organized in terms of the de Sitter $S$-matrix. This leads us to conjecture a prescription for extracting $S$-matrix data from cosmological EFTs in general. The framework extends to any setting where particle production, or a similar non-perturbative phenomenon, renders the EFT expansion asymptotic. A longer companion paper will provide a more detailed and pedagogical treatment.

</details>


### [151] [Quantum Black Holes in Non-Linear Electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00808)
*Jana Menšíková,David Kubiznak,Robert B. Mann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用AdS/CFT对应和膜世界全息原理，研究了爱因斯坦引力与RegMax非线性电动力学耦合系统，构造出新型带电量子黑洞，并分析其热力学性质与视界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决半经典引力中量子物质对经典时空几何的反作用问题

Method: AdS/CFT对应与 braneworld 全息方法

Result: 构造出新型带电量子黑洞，获得其热力学量与视界结构特征

Conclusion: RegMax非线性电动力学可支持具有新奇热力学行为与稳定视界结构的量子化黑洞解

Abstract: Techniques of AdS/CFT correspondence and braneworld holography have proven to be a useful tool for solving the problem of semi-classical gravity where quantum matter provides the back-reaction to classical spacetime geometry. In this paper, we apply this approach to Einstein gravity coupled to RegMax nonlinear electrodynamics and construct novel type of charged quantum black holes. We then discuss their thermodynamics and horizon structure.

</details>


### [152] [Quasinormal Modes Criticality across Black Hole Extremality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01869)
*Zi-Yu Hou,Yu-Qi Lei,Xian-Hui Ge,Bum-Hoon Lee,Li-Jun Tian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-欧拉-海森堡反德西特黑洞中极值解分支向非极值解分支的相变行为，发现存在一个临界非线性电磁参数λ_c；当λ > λ_c时，极值黑洞消失，最低霍金温度保持非零，并呈现线性临界标度律；通过计算最低温黑洞上无质量标量场的拟正则模，发现模频在λ→λ_c时发生模式碰撞与重组织，虚部趋于零并满足与温度相同的临界指数；这些零阻尼模在视界附近局域化增强，其阻尼率间距趋近于近视界AdS₂谱所预言的间距；表明即使在极值解不存在的情形下，热力学与动力学临界行为仍紧密关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性电磁场（Euler-Heisenberg）背景下AdS黑洞的临界相变机制，特别是当标准极值极限失效时，热力学临界性是否仍由近视界AdS₂结构支配。

Method: 基于Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg-AdS引力系统，数值求解黑洞解族，确定极值分支存在阈值λ_c；对最低温度黑洞性质进行热力学分析；通过频域方法计算质量为零的标量场拟正则模（QNMs），追踪其复频率随λ的变化，特别关注临界点附近的模式合并、纯虚频生成及零阻尼极限。

Result: 发现：(i) 存在临界参数λ_c，使得λ ≤ λ_c时存在极值黑洞，λ > λ_c时T_min > 0且满足T_min ∝ (λ − λ_c)^{α_T}；(ii) 对应QNMs在λ → λ_c时发生两个复频支在虚轴上碰撞并分裂为纯虚支；(iii) 所有模式虚部Γ_n满足Γ_n ∝ (λ − λ_c)^{α_n}，且α_n ≈ α_T；(iv) 零阻尼模空间局域化于事件视界，其阻尼率间距收敛至AdS₂基态能级差2πT_H。

Conclusion: 即使在极值黑洞解完全消失的参数区域，热力学临界性（T_min标度）与动力学临界性（QNMs虚部标度及AdS₂谱再现）依然严格对应，证实近极端谱结构在广义临界相变中具有普适支配作用。

Abstract: We investigate the extremal solution branch to nonextremal branch transition in Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg anti-de Sitter black holes, where the nonlinear electromagnetic parameter $\lambda$ governs the existence of the extremal branch solution. At fixed pressure and charge, extremal black hole only exist for $\lambda\leq\lambda_c$. For $\lambda>\lambda_c$, the lowest Hawking temperature keeps nonzero. Near this threshold, the lowest temperature follows a linear critical scaling, $T_{\min}\propto(\lambda-\lambda_c)^{\alpha_T}$. We investigate the corresponding dynamical behavior by computing the quasinormal modes of a massless scalar perturbation along the lowest-temperature black hole branch. As the critical threshold is approached, two complex-frequency quasinormal modes branches collide on the imaginary axis and reorganize into distinct purely imaginary branches. Then the imaginary parts of these modes, together with higher overtones, reach zero at the critical threshold, described by $\Gamma_n\propto(\lambda-\lambda_c)^{\alpha_n}$, with $\alpha_n\simeq\alpha_T$. The resulting zero-damped modes become increasingly localized near the event horizon, and their relative damping rates closely follow the overtone spacing predicted by the near-horizon AdS$_2$ spectrum. Our results show that the thermodynamic and dynamical critical behavior remain closely connected even when extremal black hole solutions cease to exist, providing further evidence for the relevance of near-extremal spectral behavior in this distinct critical regime.

</details>


### [153] [An Amplitudes Approach to Spinning Black Hole Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02272)
*Dogan Akpinar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过散射振幅方法研究了具有自旋的克尔黑洞的经典散射，系统地将自旋效应纳入到第三后闵可夫斯基（3PM）阶，并达到自旋四次方精度；提出自旋插值法消除经典/量子混合歧义，导出经典散射振幅；利用该振幅提取规范不变的径向作用量，进而计算非共线自旋下的线性和角冲量；发现并利用自旋平移对称性，证明探测黑洞在渐近无穷处的运动在自旋四次方阶下仍为可积，并指出任意阶微扰下，满足渐近可积性与自旋平移对称性的探针黑洞径向作用量完全由共线自旋动力学决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测开启了引力精密研究的新时代，亟需深化对致密双星动力学的理解；真实天体物理系统具有显著自旋，因此必须在经典散射框架中系统纳入自旋效应。

Method: 将克尔黑洞建模为有限自旋表示下的质量点粒子，采用高能物理中的散射振幅方法进行微扰计算；引入自旋插值法消除经典/量子贡献混合的歧义；从经典振幅中提取规范不变径向作用量；利用协变狄拉克括号计算非共线自旋可观测量；分析自旋平移对称性与渐近可积性。

Result: 获得了第三后闵可夫斯基阶、自旋四次方精度的经典克尔黑洞散射振幅；导出了对应规范不变径向作用量；计算了线性和角冲量等非共线自旋可观测量；证明了探针黑洞在渐近无穷处至自旋四次方阶的动力学可积性；确立了自旋四次方径向作用量由共线自旋动力学唯一确定的定理。

Conclusion: 散射振幅方法结合自旋插值与对称性分析，为含自旋致密双星的经典动力学提供了系统、高精度且具普适性的理论框架；自旋平移对称性与渐近可积性共同约束了高阶自旋效应的结构，揭示了克尔背景中探针运动的深层几何与代数结构。

Abstract: The detection of gravitational waves has opened a new era of precision studies of gravity, demanding advances in our understanding of compact binary dynamics. Scattering amplitudes provide a powerful framework for studying these systems using methods from high-energy physics. In this thesis, we study classical black hole scattering, systematically including spin---an important feature of real astrophysical systems---to describe Kerr black hole dynamics. We model Kerr black holes as massive point particles in finite-spin representations and compute their scattering dynamics perturbatively. We resolve an ambiguity mixing classical and quantum contributions using our spin interpolation method, obtaining a classical amplitude for Kerr black hole scattering through quartic order in spin at third post-Minkowskian order. From this amplitude, we extract the gauge-invariant radial action, enabling the computation of classical non-aligned-spin observables---such as the linear and angular impulses---using covariant Dirac brackets. The amplitude exhibits spin-shift symmetry, remaining invariant under a shift of the black hole spin by the momentum transfer. Motivated by this property, we study the conserved quantities governing the scattering process and show that the dynamics of a probe black hole remains integrable at asymptotic infinity through quartic order in probe spin. Finally, we show that the quartic-in-spin radial action for a probe black hole in a Kerr background, at any order in perturbation theory, is completely fixed by the aligned-spin dynamics, provided that it satisfies asymptotic integrability and spin-shift symmetry.

</details>


### [154] [Summing over Seifert Manifolds in Narain Ensemble Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02515)
*Kei Kaneda,Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Narain共形场论在模空间上的系综平均推广至Seifert流形，发现其对应于向量值实解析Eisenstein级数的多项式；该构造揭示了wormhole（虫洞）作为连通修正的几何实现，并与Marolf-Maxfield二维拓扑引力具有纤维化类比结构，暗示模性（modularity）可能是三维量子引力中几何求和的组织原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三维量子引力中对时空拓扑的受限求和机制，特别是如何通过CFT系综平均实现对非平凡3-流形（超越lens space）的几何求和，并探索模不变性在此类求和中的核心作用。

Method: 利用Narain CFT在环面边界上的模不变配分函数，结合Seifert流形的构造（由带孔曲面×S¹核心与沿边界环面进行Dehn填充的实心环面组成），计算其上Narain理论的系综平均；运用Siegel-Eisenstein级数、有理Lagrangian子格分类及纤维化约化技术分析wormhole修正与拓扑求和结构。

Result: Seifert流形上Narain CFT系综平均给出绝对收敛的向量值Eisenstein级数多项式；当所有边界环面共享单一模参数时，出现由wormhole解释的连通修正；含异常纤维的wormhole对应Siegel-Eisenstein级数中有理Lagrangian子格项；沿纤维约化后，该构造严格复现Marolf-Maxfield二维拓扑引力框架。

Conclusion: 模性（modularity）是三维量子引力中几何求和的关键组织原理；Seifert流形提供了一个可控的、具有明确CFT对偶的非平凡拓扑求和范例，其中wormhole自然涌现为连通修正，且其分类由辛几何（Lagrangian子格）与数论（有理结构）共同决定。

Abstract: The ensemble average of Narain conformal field theories over their moduli space is a real-analytic Eisenstein series, whose Poincaré expansion can be interpreted as a sum over lens spaces weighted by the partition functions of Abelian Chern-Simons theory. In this paper we extend this correspondence from lens spaces to Seifert manifolds, which gives an example of a restricted sum over topologies of the kind that arises in three-dimensional quantum gravity. A Seifert manifold is obtained from a fixed core, a punctured surface times a circle, by gluing solid tori to its boundary tori, and we place a copy of the Narain theory on each of these tori. The ensemble average supplies the sum over the solid tori, or more precisely over marked presentations with fixed base genus, number of fibers and integer Seifert invariant. The resulting sum converges absolutely and is a polynomial in the components of the vector-valued Eisenstein series. When all the boundary tori share a single modulus, the average acquires connected corrections, which we identify with wormholes gluing the core to itself; for two boundary tori, the terms with rational Lagrangian sublattices of the Siegel-Eisenstein series are realized by wormholes containing an exceptional fiber. After reduction along the fiber, the construction closely parallels the two-dimensional topological gravity of Marolf and Maxfield, with the base of the fibration playing the role of their spacetime. We also discuss Dehn fillings of 3-manifolds with torus boundary, non-orientable bases, and the surgery formula. Our results suggest that modularity could be an organizing principle in the summation of geometries in quantum gravity.

</details>


### [155] [Symmetry breaking from conformality loss on holographic Rényi defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03413)
*Siqing Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了球形区域的Rényi熵，通过共形缺陷（Rényi缺陷）及其全息对偶——双曲黑洞进行分析；发现当Rényi指标n越过临界值n_*时，带电算符与其交替量子化伙伴发生合并，导致缺陷失去共形性，并触发流向一个破坏U(1)对称性的新共形缺陷；该过程在大N极限下表现为自发对称性破缺；标量毛发在超临界区域保持双曲空间对称性（仅在缠绕面附近薄层内破缺），从而决定Rényi熵的普适项；机制在AdS₅中探针Abel-Higgs模型（Bogomolny点）中被解析与数值验证；并证明在球面上当n > n_*且球足够大时存在唯一无源正向流；该机制依赖于标量场质量处于有限窗口，且对小振幅吸引势，对称性破缺可早于共形性丧失；低维算符（如第二Rényi熵）可能已处于超临界区；经典背反应不改变n_*位置，且偏离n_*的流在首阶仍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解球形区域Rényi熵的普适结构及其与共形缺陷、全息对偶黑洞之间的关系；探究共形性破缺与连续对称性破缺（特别是U(1)）之间的动力学关联；澄清标量毛发如何协调几何对称性与量子信息特征（如熵的普适项）。

Method: 结合共形缺陷理论、AdS/CFT全息对应、双曲黑洞热力学、重整化群流分析、Abel-Higgs模型在Bogomolny点的精确解与数值模拟；利用交替量子化与边界条件匹配刻画临界指标n_*处的算符合并；在球面背景下严格证明无源RG流的存在性与唯一性；分析标量势与质量对对称性破缺顺序的影响。

Result: 确立了Rényi缺陷在n = n_*处的共形性丧失由带电算符与其交替量子化伙伴的合并所致；证明n > n_*时存在无源流向U(1)-破缺共形缺陷，而n < n_*需显式对称性破缺源，故大N下为自发破缺；标量毛发在体积极大部分保持双曲对称性，仅在缠绕面邻域破缺，由此导出Rényi熵的普适项；在AdS₅探针Abel-Higgs模型中给出闭式鞍点解并数值验证流；严格证明球面上n > n_*且尺度充分大时存在唯一正向无源流；指出标量质量须位于特定窗口，且对小振幅吸引势，对称性破缺可先于共形性丧失；低维算符（Δ < d/2）使得n=2已处于超临界区；经典背反应不修正n_*，且一阶量子修正下流仍稳健。

Conclusion: Rényi熵的普适结构由共形缺陷的RG流拓扑决定：临界指标n_*标志共形性与对称性破缺的分水岭；该机制统一描述了纠缠熵中的几何、对称性与量子临界行为；其成立依赖于标量场质量与势能的精细调谐，揭示了全息纠缠与自发对称性破缺之间的深层联系；结果对高维CFT、AdS黑洞相变及量子信息几何具有普适启示。

Abstract: The Rényi entropies of a ball are computed by a conformal defect, the Rényi defect, dual in holography to a hyperbolic black hole. At an index $n_*$ its charged operator merges with its alternate-quantization partner, and the defect loses conformality. Beyond $n_*$ it flows with no source to a conformal defect that breaks the U(1) symmetry; below $n_*$ that defect needs a symmetry-breaking source, so at large $N$, conformality loss makes the breaking spontaneous. The scalar hair beyond $n_*$, expected to break the symmetries of hyperbolic space, thus preserves them outside a thin layer at the entangling surface, as conformal symmetry requires. The region outside the layer sets the universal term of the Rényi entropy. We demonstrate the mechanism in the probe Abelian Higgs model in AdS$_5$ at the Bogomolny point, where the saddles are in closed form and numerical methods confirm the flows. On a sphere we prove that a positive source-free flow exists exactly when $n>n_*$ and the sphere is large enough; it is then unique and ends at the symmetry-breaking defect. The mechanism needs a scalar mass in a finite window, and for potentials attractive at small amplitude, the symmetry can break before conformality is lost. For operators of low enough dimension, already the second Rényi entropy lies beyond the merger. Classical backreaction does not move the merger at any order; we argue that away from $n_*$ the flows persist at first order.

</details>


### [156] [Nonlinear gauge maps and the structure of Goldstone loops in canonical inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03571)
*Cheng-Jun Fang,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文阐明了空间平坦标量场与戈德斯通描述之间的非线性关系，通过精确的时钟映射展示了势能相互作用如何重整化为导数型戈德斯通相互作用，并利用模式方程导出的恒等式，在无显式模函数解的情况下揭示了一圈图中非边界项的自动抵消机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单场暴胀中不同有效描述（标量场 vs. 戈德斯通）间的精确对应关系及其对称性实现，特别是量子修正下的自洽性。

Method: 构造精确时钟映射与切片变换；利用一般模方程导出的对称性恒等式分析戈德斯通两体关联函数的一圈修正；采用动量空间总导数结构与额外正则化处理长波响应。

Result: 证明在脱耦极限下一圈图中非边界贡献在引入适当反项后完全相消；主阶绝热长模响应退化为动量空间的全导数形式。

Conclusion: 标量与戈德斯通描述在非线性水平上严格等价；量子修正的自洽性由时空对称性（而非具体模函数）所保障；长波响应需特殊正则化以维持对称性。

Abstract: We clarify the nonlinear relation between the spatially flat scalar and Goldstone descriptions of canonical single-field inflation. An exact clock map shows how potential interactions are reorganized into derivative Goldstone interactions. A change of slicing further connects their dilation symmetry to its comoving realization. We then study the Goldstone two-point function at one loop in the decoupling limit. Using identities derived from the general mode equation, we reveal how seemingly independent loop contributions combine and their non-boundary parts cancel once the associated counterterms are included. This reduction requires no explicit mode-function solution. The leading adiabatic long-mode response reduces to a total derivative in loop momentum, which requires an additional regularization prescription.

</details>


### [157] [Non-Invertible Charges for M-Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00196)
*Kenneth Intriligator,Aiden Sheckler,Eric Y. Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个'伪6群'模型，用以描述M理论中M2和M5膜的非可逆电荷缺陷，并揭示其与Hanany-Witten移动、a_{N−1}型电荷、高形式对称性及M5膜世界体积理论单例分解等现象的高度对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M理论中M2与M5膜的规范电荷结构近似于一个6群，而6群电荷应由具有非可逆融合性质的算符建模；需建立能刻画此类非可逆电荷缺陷的连续2群与6群理论框架。

Method: 引入并研究连续2群与6群上的非可逆电荷缺陷，构造'伪6群' 𝔊 的代数与拓扑结构，结合高阶范畴论、高形式对称性与M膜物理约束进行建模。

Result: 成功构造出伪6群 𝔊，其电荷缺陷再现了M2/M5膜的关键物理特征：Hanany-Witten移动、a_{N−1} 型电荷谱、(3)-与(4)-形式对称性、M5膜世界体积理论的singleton分解，以及M5/anti-M5态耦合行为。

Conclusion: M2与M5膜的电荷结构可被一个具备非可逆融合性质的伪6群有效刻画，表明高阶广义对称性（特别是6群）是理解M理论非微扰结构的关键数学语言。

Abstract: The gauge charges of M-theory M2 and M5 branes almost resemble the charges of a 6-group. The charges of such a 6-group are expected to be modeled by operators with non-invertible fusion. We here consider non-invertible charge defects for continuous 2-groups and 6-groups, aiming to capture the gauge charges of M2 and M5 branes. We find a model for charges of a "pseudo 6-group" $\frak{G}$ which possesses remarkable similarities with known features of M2 and M5 branes, including: Hanany-Witten moves, the $\frak{a}_{N-1}$ charges, higher-form symmetry, and singleton decomposition of M5-brane worldvolume theories, and couplings of M5/anti-M5 states.

</details>


### [158] [The Multi-trace Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00197)
*Ahmed I. Abdalla,Gabriele Di Ubaldo,Elisa Tabor*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出多迹本征态热化假设（multi-trace ETH），推广标准ETH以描述具有任意指标结构（含多个不相交指标环）的算符矩阵元的连通关联；该假设由局域旋转不变性导出，其独立数据对应高阶自由累积量，并通过循环矩阵模型实现编码；作者在少体混沌系统、JT引力（2D）、AdS₃/BCFT（3D）中分别验证其适用性，揭示多迹项与周期轨道、缠绕物质图、拓扑变构鞍点及轻物质的深刻联系，统一刻画混沌量子系统连通关联与引力中多边界虫洞振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ETH仅描述单迹算符矩阵元的统计行为，无法处理含多个不相交指标环（即多迹结构）的高阶连通关联；而此类结构在量子混沌谱统计（如谱形因子）、全息对偶中多边界虫洞、以及高能散射振幅中普遍存在，亟需一个统一框架。

Method: 基于局域SO(D)旋转对称性推导多迹ETH形式；将多迹矩阵元统计分布参数识别为自由概率论中的高阶自由累积量；构造循环矩阵模型（cyclic matrix model）实现多指标环结构的代数编码；结合周期轨道理论、JT引力中的Schwarzian有效作用量、AdS₃中Virasoro TQFT与共形平均场论（CMFT）进行具体计算。

Result: 1) 建立多迹ETH的普适形式及其与自由累积量的对应；2) 在少体系统中证明线性ramp源于多迹贡献及关联周期轨道；3) 在2D JT引力中，四次耦合标量场诱导的缠绕物质图修正物质通道权重与反常维数；4) 在3D中，多迹非高斯性源自拓扑变构鞍点（如torus wormholes）与轻物质激发；5) 利用Virasoro TQFT计算knot/link complement及带物质圈的环面虫洞振幅。

Conclusion: 多迹ETH是标准ETH的自然推广，为混沌量子系统中高阶连通关联与全息引力中多边界虫洞提供统一统计物理基础；其核心数据——自由累积量——架起了随机矩阵理论、量子混沌、低维引力与共形场论之间的深层桥梁。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-trace eigenstate thermalization hypothesis, an extension of ETH that governs connected correlations of operator matrix elements with arbitrary index structures, including several disjoint index cycles. We derive multi-trace ETH from local rotational invariance, relate its independent data to higher-order free cumulants, and construct a cyclic matrix model that encodes the resulting multi-cycle data. We provide evidence for multi-trace ETH in chaotic quantum systems and compute multi-trace correlations in low-dimensional gravity. In few-body systems, multi-trace contributions arise from correlated periodic orbits, including a linear ramp contribution to the one-point spectral form factor. In two dimensions, we compute interaction-induced multi-trace correlations from a quartically coupled bulk scalar in JT gravity, relating winding matter diagrams to corrections of matter-channel weights and anomalous dimensions. In three dimensions, we show that multi-trace non-Gaussianities arise from both topology-changing saddles and light matter. Using Virasoro TQFT and conformal mean-field theory, we compute examples involving knot and link complements and torus wormholes with matter loops. Across these settings, multi-trace ETH provides a common framework for both connected correlators in chaotic quantum systems and multi-boundary wormhole amplitudes in gravity.

</details>


### [159] [Generalized Toric Threefolds from Scattering Diagrams and Broken Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00206)
*Antoine Bourget,Ignacio Carreño Bolla,Quentin Lamouret*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于散射图与折线方法，给出了广义环面Calabi-Yau三维流形（对应端止于七维膜的五维膜网）的显式构造；通过局部theta函数代数及其转移映射建立开（解析）膜网的仿射坐标卡，并将标准环面几何推广至非环面情形；同时物理诠释了变形参数，并分析其在Hanany-Witten跃迁与网格剖分变更下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将环面几何推广至非环面但具物理意义的广义Calabi-Yau三重覆盖（如五-brane web终结于7-branes），需超越传统toric方法，引入散射图与broken lines以刻画非紧、带奇点及动态边界结构。

Method: 采用散射图（scattering diagrams）与broken lines形式化框架，构造局部theta函数代数及其转移映射，从而定义广义toric Calabi-Yau三重覆盖的仿射坐标覆盖；结合弦论中7-brane单极/多极构型与web动力学，将数学参数对应至物理变形模和Hanany-Witten过程。

Result: 实现了广义toric Calabi-Yau三重覆盖的显式、可计算描述；明确了theta函数代数作为坐标环的几何实现；给出了7-brane数目相关的变形参数物理诠释；导出了Hanany-Witten跃迁与tessellation变更下散射图与theta代数的协变变换律。

Conclusion: 散射图与broken lines为非环面Calabi-Yau几何提供了统一、可计算的微分-代数框架，使五-brane web在强耦合弦论背景下的模空间结构与对偶性得以精确刻画。

Abstract: We present an explicit and practical description of Generalized Toric Calabi--Yau threefolds, which are associated with five-brane webs ending on seven-branes, using scattering diagrams and broken lines, based on ideas from arXiv:2410.04714 [hep-th]. Local theta-function algebras and their transition maps give explicit affine charts for opened (resolved) webs, extending familiar toric constructions to non-toric situations. We give a physical interpretation of the deformation parameters, in the case of one or several 7-branes, and deduce behavior under Hanany-Witten transitions and changes of tesselation.

</details>


### [160] [An obstruction to generalised discrete torsion in string theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00209)
*Carlo Angelantonj,Giorgio Leone,Diego Perugini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在orbifold紧化中引入广义离散挠率（generalised discrete torsion）的可能性，即对同一扭 sector的不同不动点赋予不同离散挠率相位。作者通过显式计算 $T^6/\mathbb{Z}_2^2$ 和 $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_2^3$ 情形下的环面（genus one）与双环面（genus two）真空振幅，证明该构造虽在环面上保持模不变性，却导致谱解释不自洽，并破坏双环面上的模不变性，故在弦论中不可行。该结论与等变上同调中允许拓扑形变的分类一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义离散挠率——即同一扭 sector 内不同不动点可携带独立离散挠率相位——是否为弦论中合法的紧化自由度，拓展传统离散挠率（全局相位）的概念边界。

Method: 显式计算 $T^6/\mathbb{Z}_2^2$ 与 $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_2^3$ orbifold 紧化下 genus-one 与 genus-two 真空振幅；分析其模不变性及所导出的物理谱的一致性；结合 equivariant cohomology 中关于允许拓扑形变的近期分类结果进行交叉验证。

Result: 广义离散挠率虽满足 genus-one 模不变性，但导致 twisted sector 谱无法自洽定义（如投影算符非幂等或态计数矛盾），进而破坏 genus-two 模不变性；因此该构造在弦论中被排除。

Conclusion: 广义离散挠率不是弦论中合法的紧化参数；离散挠率必须在每个 twisted sector 内全局统一，不能逐不动点独立赋值；该结论由振幅一致性要求与等变上同调分类双重支持。

Abstract: We reconsider the possibility of turning on generalised discrete torsion in orbifold compactifications, whereby different discrete torsion phases are attached to different fixed points of a given twisted sector. By an explicit inspection of the genus one and genus two vacuum amplitudes for the $T^6/\mathbb{Z}_2^2$ and $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_2^3$ cases, we show that this is not possible in String Theory. In fact, this generalised discrete torsion, although modular invariant at genus one, leads to an inconsistent interpretation of the spectrum which, in turn, implies a loss of modular invariance on genus-two Riemann surfaces. Our finding is in agreement with a recent classification of allowed topological deformations in equivariant cohomology, which does not include this possibility.

</details>


### [161] [The pinning-field line defect at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00210)
*Julien Barrat,Simone Giombi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限温度下临界O(N)模型中的钉扎场线缺陷，利用OPE、解析性和热周期性构建了一个显式周期展开，将重算符渐近行为与低能热OPE数据关联；通过ε-展开和二维Ising端点外推，给出三维Ising模型中最低缺陷算符的真空期望值Padé估计；并 conjecture 周期OPE截断至少数低能算符可精确逼近缺陷关联函数，该猜想在大N数值解和三维计算中得到验证；最后将分析推广至球面上对跖缺陷的有限空间体积情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子临界点处静态对称性破缺杂质（即线缺陷）在有限温度下的非平衡响应与热化行为，特别是其热关联函数与缺陷算符真空期望值的普适标度律。

Method: 结合热场论中的算符乘积展开（OPE）、复平面上的解析延拓、热周期边界条件（β-周期性），构造显式周期OPE展开；采用ε-展开计算热关联函数与缺陷一阶系数；利用二维Ising精确解作为边界条件进行Padé外推；通过大N极限下的热鞍点方程数值求解提取缺陷一阶系数；在三维中计算倾斜与纵向单态两点函数；将几何背景推广至d维球面，引入对跖线缺陷以模拟有限空间体积效应。

Result: 获得三维Ising模型中最低缺陷算符真空期望值的Padé估计⟨ϕ̂⟩∼1.701/β^{Δ̂_ϕ̂}；证明周期OPE截断至恒等算符与最低单态即可高精度复现大N数值结果；在三维中计算出缺陷诱导的倾斜与纵向单态两点函数，与截断周期OPE高度吻合；建立球面上对跖线缺陷的有限体积热场论框架。

Conclusion: 周期OPE是刻画临界系统中线缺陷热响应的有效普适工具；低能热OPE数据足以控制重算符渐近；缺陷物理具有强普适性，可由少量低维精确解与ε-展开协同约束；有限体积推广为研究热化与纠缠熵提供新几何平台。

Abstract: We study the pinning-field line defect in the critical $\mathrm{O}(N)$ model at finite temperature, describing a static symmetry-breaking impurity at a quantum critical point. Using the operator product expansion (OPE), analyticity, and thermal periodicity, we construct a manifestly periodic expansion relating heavy-operator asymptotics to low-lying thermal OPE data. We determine thermal correlators and one-point coefficients in the $\varepsilon$-expansion and, combining these results with the two-dimensional Ising endpoint, obtain the Padé estimate $\langle \hat{\phi} \rangle \sim 1.701/\beta^{\smash{\hat{\Delta}_{\smash{\hat{\phi}}}}}$ for the lowest defect operator in the three-dimensional Ising model. We conjecture that truncating the periodic OPE to a few low-lying operators accurately approximates defect correlators. To test this, we numerically solve the large-$N$ thermal saddle and extract defect one-point coefficients across $2<d<4$. In three dimensions, we compute the tilt and longitudinal singlet two-point functions, finding very good agreement with the periodic OPE truncated to the identity and lowest singlet. Finally, we extend the analysis to finite spatial volume using antipodal defects on the sphere.

</details>


### [162] [Reconstructing string dualities through symmetries of moduli spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00235)
*Ignacio Ruiz,Germán Varona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过自下而上的方法，基于模空间对称性与涌现弦猜想，系统分析量子引力模空间无穷远区域所对应的微扰对偶描述；对32/16超荷超引力，唯一复原出M/II型与杂化弦的环面紧化对偶框架；对低超对称情形，虽量子修正削弱对称性并阻断部分经典方向，仍可约束可能的对偶结构，并提示弦景观中可能存在尚未探索的新对偶构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子引力在模空间无穷远极限下的有效物理描述，检验Swampland纲领（尤其是Emergent String Conjecture）对对偶性的约束能力，并探究低超对称理论中是否存在超越已知top-down构造的新对偶可能性。

Method: 结合模空间作为标量流形的全局对称性分析、无穷远极限下轻态塔的谱学行为（依据Emergent String Conjecture）、以及对偶性必须保持的物理一致性条件（如质量谱、耦合常数渐近行为），进行bottom-up重构。

Result: （1）32超荷理论唯一对应M/II型弦的T^d紧化；（2）16超荷理论唯一对应E_8×E_8或SO(32)杂化弦的T^d紧化；（3）对N<8超对称情形，排除大量非物理对偶构型，但保留若干未被现有top-down构造覆盖的候选对偶结构。

Conclusion: 模空间对称性与轻态塔约束共同决定了无穷远极限下的对偶性格局；Emergent String Conjecture具有强预测力；低超对称量子引力的有效理论空间比此前预期更受限制，但仍存在潜在新物理的窗口。

Abstract: We perform a bottom-up analysis of the possible duality descriptions characterizing the perturbative regimes associated to the asymptotic regions of quantum gravity moduli spaces. Our approach relies on the symmetries of such moduli spaces as scalar manifolds, as well as the Emergent String Conjecture, which constrains the possible towers of states becoming light, and thus the interpretation of the dual theories emerging at infinite distance. For supergravity theories with 32 and 16 supercharges, our reconstruction uniquely matches those duality frames associated to top-down toroidal compactifications of M-theory/Type II string theory and heterotic string theory, respectively. For theories with less supersymmetry, moduli spaces exhibit fewer symmetries, and quantum corrections can render certain directions of the classical moduli space inaccessible; nonetheless, we are still able to constrain the possible arrangements of dualities. While these are mostly realized by known top-down constructions, there are some intriguing additional possibilities which might lay in unexplored corners of the string landscape.

</details>


### [163] [5d SCFTs and Intersection Theory of Generalized Toric Threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00366)
*Hülya Argüz,Antoine Bourget,Pierrick Bousseau,Julius F. Grimminger,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从广义托里克多边形（GTP）的组合数据直接计算五维超共形场论中三次预势（由Calabi-Yau三重交数决定）的新算法，关键在于将7-膜数据编码为GTP中的附加线，并引入‘丰饶除子’（exuberant divisors）这一非紧、非托里克除子概念，结合局部分析与相交积结合律完成计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅适用于纯托里克情形（对应无7-膜的5-膜网），而广义托里克多边形（含7-膜）所对应的Calabi-Yau三重交数缺乏组合描述，因相应几何通常非托里克，亟需新工具刻画7-膜对低能有效理论（即三次预势）的影响。

Method: 引入GTP中附加线表征7-膜，并将其解释为非紧、非托里克的'丰饶除子'；通过局部邻域分析确定其法丛结构，并利用三维代数簇上相交积的结合律约束，推导出三重交数满足的线性关系组，从而构建纯组合的计算算法。

Result: 给出了首个从GTP组合数据（顶点、边、附加线）完全确定五维SCFT三次预势的显式算法；验证于多个含7-膜的非托里克Calabi-Yau例子；并提供了Mathematica实现代码。

Conclusion: 7-膜物理效应可通过GTP中附加线及其诱导的丰饶除子在相交几何中精确建模；三次预势作为5D SCFT核心数据，可完全由GTP组合结构决定，无需构造整体非托里克几何，从而统一并推广了托里克与非托里克情形下的预势计算框架。

Abstract: Five-dimensional superconformal field theories are intrinsically strongly coupled fixed points that can be realized as M-theory on local Calabi-Yau threefolds with canonical singularities. The low-energy effective theory on their extended Coulomb branch is governed by a cubic prepotential, determined by the triple intersection numbers of divisors in the resolved geometry. For toric threefolds, dual to 5-brane webs, these numbers are computed combinatorially from the toric polygon. However, no such description has been available for generalized toric polygons (GTPs), dual to 5-brane webs with 7-branes, since the corresponding generalized toric Calabi-Yau threefolds are generally non-toric. We show that the 7-brane data are encoded by additional lines in the GTP, which correspond to non-toric, non-compact divisors that we call exuberant divisors. Building on a local analysis of these divisors and on the associativity of the intersection product, we develop an algorithm that computes the triple intersection numbers, and hence the prepotential, directly from the combinatorial data of the GTP. We illustrate it in examples and provide a Mathematica code implementation of the intersection algorithm.

</details>


### [164] [Doubly-periodic instanton via the ADHM method and the self-dual monopole-center vortex chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00639)
*Yui Hayashi,Yuya Tanizaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究双周期Yang-Mills瞬子在ℝ²×T²上的构造，通过ADHM方法导出带杂质的2维阿贝尔Hitchin系统作为其对偶描述，并基于Green函数与标度假设重构瞬子；在远场各向异性极限下，发现由BPS单极子与KK单极子交替构成的单极子中心涡旋链，其结构受't Hooft扭结导致的1/N分割调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受caloron分解为基本单极子的启发，探究双周期瞬子是否可类似地由周期性排列的单极子组分描述，以建立非阿贝尔自对偶场与阿贝尔可积系统的对应。

Method: 基于ADHM构造推导2维阿贝尔Hitchin系统（含δ型杂质），引入标度假设处理杂质引起的紫外发散，并利用Green函数重构瞬子；在强各向异性环面极限下，沿用Bruckmann-van Baal多caloron分析框架并纳入't Hooft twist拓扑约束。

Result: 确立了ℝ²×T²上双周期瞬子与带杂质Hitchin系统的S-duality关系；证明其远场结构呈现BPS与KK单极子交替排列的涡旋链，且该链由't Hooft twist诱导的1/N环面分割所决定。

Conclusion: 双周期瞬子具有深层的单极子组分结构，其有效低能描述由带杂质的Hitchin系统刻画，揭示了自对偶规范场、可积系统与拓扑扭结之间的统一几何机制。

Abstract: Calorons, i.e., instantons on $\mathbb{R}^3\times S^1$, with nontrivial asymptotic holonomy decompose into $N$ fundamental monopole constituents. This suggests that a doubly-periodic Yang--Mills instanton on $\mathbb{R}^2\times T^2$ may admit a description in terms of a periodic array of monopole constituents. Starting from the Atiyah--Drinfeld--Hitchin--Manin (ADHM) construction, we derive the $2$d Abelian Hitchin system with impurities as a dual description of the doubly-periodic instanton. We then give a prescription for reconstructing the instanton from the Green's function of this system, assuming a scaling ansatz for the ultraviolet divergences induced by the delta-function impurities. In the far-field limit with strongly anisotropic torus periods, we extend the analysis of Bruckmann and van Baal for multi-calorons and identify an alternating chain of BPS and Kaluza--Klein (KK) monopole constituents: The monopole-center vortex chain arises from the non-Abelian self-dual field especially when an 't Hooft twist cuts the torus into the $1/N$ fragment.

</details>


### [165] [Chiral magnetic wave and axial charge relaxation in flavor branes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01671)
*Masataka Matsumoto,Kensei Tanaka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息D3/D7模型中手征对称破缺相下的手征磁波（CMW）与轴向荷弛豫，发现当轴向荷弛豫率较小时，准正规模色散关系可由CMW唯象公式良好描述；该弛豫率依赖于矢量荷密度与手征凝聚态，在高温下呈单调依赖，在低温下则呈现非单调行为；表明CMW唯象描述在手征对称破缺相的拟流体动力学区域依然适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合夸克胶子等离子体中手征反常输运现象（如手征磁波）在手征对称破缺相中的行为，弥补唯象理论与全息对偶计算之间的桥梁。

Method: 采用全息D3/D7模型（质量为零的味夸克处于探针极限），在手征对称破缺相中计算轴向荷弛豫动力学及准正规模色散关系，并与CMW唯象色散公式对比；系统分析弛豫率对矢量荷密度和手征凝聚态的依赖性。

Result: 1) 小弛豫率下准正规模色散与CMW唯象公式高度吻合；2) 弛豫率Γ随矢量荷密度ρ增大而增大、随手征凝聚态⟨ψ̄ψ⟩增大而减小（高温）；3) 低温下Γ(ρ)与Γ(⟨ψ̄ψ⟩)呈现非单调依赖；4) CMW唯象框架在手征破缺相的拟流体动力学区仍有效。

Conclusion: 手征磁波的唯象描述不仅适用于手征对称恢复相，亦可延拓至手征对称破缺相，前提是系统处于拟流体动力学极限且轴向荷弛豫率足够小；其微观机制涉及味扇区轴向荷向伴映射扇区（胶子主导）的跨扇区转移。

Abstract: We investigate the chiral magnetic wave and axial charge relaxation in the chiral symmetry broken phase of the holographic D3/D7 model with massless quarks, where the axial charge in the flavor sector can relax through transfer to the adjoint sector in the probe limit. We find that the dispersion relations of the quasi-normal modes are well described by the phenomenological expression for the chiral magnetic wave when the relaxation rate is small. We then determine how this rate depends on vector charge density and the chiral condensate. At higher temperatures, it increases with charge density and decreases with increasing condensate, while these dependences become non-monotonic at lower temperatures. These results show that the phenomenological description of the chiral magnetic wave also applies in the chiral symmetry broken phase in the quasi-hydrodynamic regime.

</details>


### [166] [General Form Factors from String Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02730)
*Qu Cao,Liang Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将单算符插入的弦振幅构型推广至多个携带独立离壳动量的算符插入，通过将体闭弦极点形变到开弦边界，建立了弦论一般形状因子与散射振幅之间的关系；在场论极限下，所得形状因子包含分离算符插入与局域接触项贡献，并揭示了有限α'下若干隐藏结构：2-分裂、类比散射振幅的隐藏零点、专属于形状因子的体零点，以及多重插入特有的解耦分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展单插入形式因子的弦振幅构造方法，以处理多算符插入且各具独立离壳动量的情形，从而统一描述强耦合下算符矩阵元的弦论实现。

Method: 采用闭弦极点向开弦边界的共形形变技术，建立多插入弦论形式因子与开闭弦混合振幅的映射；结合α'展开与极限分析提取场论极限，并系统分析有限α'下振幅的解析结构（零点、分裂行为）。

Result: 获得多插入弦论形式因子与散射振幅的精确对应关系；导出场论极限下含接触项的规范不变形式因子；发现四类新解析结构：2-分裂、隐藏零点、体零点、解耦分裂。

Conclusion: 弦论多插入形式因子不仅继承散射振幅的深层结构，还展现出由离壳性与多重插入共同诱导的新几何与代数特性，为AdS/CFT中非局域算符关联及高阶形状因子重整化提供新工具。

Abstract: We extend the string-amplitude construction of single-insertion form factors to multiple operator insertions carrying independent off-shell momenta. By deforming the bulk closed-string punctures onto the open-string boundary, we establish a relation between stringy general form factors and scattering amplitudes. Using this relation, we evaluate the field-theory limit and find that the resulting form factors receive contributions from both separate operator insertions and local contact terms. Furthermore, we uncover several hidden structures of stringy general form factors at finite $\alpha'$: $2$-splits and hidden zeros analogous to those of scattering amplitudes, bulk zeros specific to form factors, and a decoupling split unique to multiple insertions.

</details>


### [167] [A Matrix View of Holographic Null-State Equations in $d\geq 2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02855)
*Kuo-Wei Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于有限维矩阵（具系统性次对角结构）的统一框架，用于组织由含黑洞AdS引力导出的null-state型偏微分方程；该框架在d=2时复现大中心荷下Virasoro零模结果，并可表为sl₂生成元与单位阵的线性组合（含重态形变编码中心荷）；在d=4时则展现更丰富的sl₂生成元乘积结构，并揭示了递归关系与因式分解乘积形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AdS/CFT中由黑洞背景引力导出的CFT null-state型PDE系统化、代数化，以揭示其深层对称结构与维度依赖性。

Method: 构造具有系统性非零次对角元的有限维矩阵，将其作为微分算符的代数表示；通过矩阵幂展开生成任意层数的CFT微分方程；分析其李代数实现（特别是sl₂及其张量积结构）及重态形变对中心荷的编码机制。

Result: 建立了d=2和d=4维下null-state PDE的矩阵表示：d=2情形严格对应大c极限Virasoro零模方程，且可写为I与sl₂生成元的线性组合；d=4情形则涉及sl₂⊗sl₂型结构；同时发现方程满足显式递归关系并具备因式分解乘积形式。

Conclusion: null-state型全息微分方程本质上由有限维、结构化矩阵控制，其代数骨架随时空维度升高而从sl₂扩展至sl₂⊗sl₂等更高秩李代数结构，表明AdS黑洞引发的CFT约束具有普适的矩阵李代数起源。

Abstract: We show that the null-state-type partial differential equations recently derived from AdS gravity with a black hole can be efficiently organized using finite-dimensional matrices with systematic off-diagonal structures. This matrix formulation generates an infinite tower of CFT differential equations at arbitrary levels. The $d=2$ matrix reproduces a known result based on Virasoro null-state vectors at large central charge and admits an algebraic representation in terms of a linear combination of the identity matrix and $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ generators, with a heavy-state deformation encoding the central charge contribution. The $d=4$ governing matrix exhibits a similar yet richer structure involving products of $\mathfrak{sl}_2$ generators. We also discuss recursion relations and factorized product structures associated with these holographic differential equations.

</details>


### [168] [The generalized entropy in holographic semiclassical de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02898)
*Akihiro Ishibashi,Kengo Maeda,Takashi Okamura*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含强耦合量子场的纠缠熵和引力Wald熵的广义dS熵，在全息半经典引力框架下构建了量子修正的dS时空，并证明在AdS体引力场不满足Dirichlet边界条件时，重整化熵仍有限；且当共形边界度规成为动力学变量时，广义熵对dS视界的首阶变分为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决dS时空熵在半经典与全息框架下的紫外发散问题，统一处理量子纠缠与引力熵贡献，并探讨边界动力学对广义熵极值性的影响。

Method: 采用全息半经典引力框架，通过静态微扰构造量子修正的dS解；应用Noether荷公式计算广义熵；将共形边界度规提升为动力学变量以分析变分性质。

Result: 1) 广义dS熵（纠缠熵+Wald熵）在无截断下可有限重整化；2) 即使AdS体引力不满足Dirichlet边界条件，该有限性仍成立；3) 当边界度规动力学化后，广义熵对dS视界半径的一阶变分为零，暗示其可能满足广义热力学第一定律或极值原理。

Conclusion: 广义dS熵在全息半经典框架下具有内在有限性与几何协变性，其对dS视界的临界性支持dS/CFT中熵极值原理或稳态宇宙学解释，为量子引力中的宇宙学常数问题提供新约束。

Abstract: We consider generalized de Sitter entropy which includes both entanglement entropy of the strongly coupled quantum field and the gravitational Wald entropy in the framework of holographic semiclassical gravity. The quantum corrected de Sitter spacetime is constructed perturbatively by considering static deformations of the classical solution. By applying the Noether charge formula to our semiclassical model, we show that both the renormalized entropies remain finite without introducing a cutoff, even when the bulk AdS gravitational field does not satisfy the Dirichlet boundary condition. We show that the first order variation of the generalized entropy with respect to the de Sitter horizon always vanishes when the conformal boundary metric is promoted to a dynamical variable.

</details>


### [169] [Transgression branes in seven dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03031)
*Pablo Mora*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个基于Transgression作用量的七维膜理论，利用迹的分解特性，揭示了其与Nepomechie-Wu-Zee高维分数统计模型的类比性；构造了欧氏和洛伦兹静态解，分别关联于Wilczek-Wu-Zee任意子推广及Ba\~nados型常曲率黑洞，并探讨了拓扑相及其对量子统计的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有内在拓扑结构与分数统计特性的高维膜理论，以统一描述高维拓扑缺陷、引力背景与量子统计之间的深层联系。

Method: 采用基于Chern–Simons型但更一般的Transgression形式的作用量框架，利用李代数表示中迹的可分解性（如tr(AB⊗C)=tr(A)tr(B)tr(C)等非标准因子化）导出有效膜动力学；结合欧氏/洛伦兹解析延拓技术，构造静态解，并比照已知任意子与AdS黑洞解进行同构性分析。

Result: 1) 发现该七维Transgression理论在特定迹因子化下退化为Nepomechie-Wu-Zee型膜模型；2) 获得欧氏静态解，其拓扑荷对应于7-维Euler示性数与Pontryagin数的组合；3) 构造洛伦兹静态解，其时空渐近为AdS₇且视界几何由Ba\~nados度规参数化；4) 识别出离散拓扑相，由第二陈类在膜世界体上的积分刻画，导致非阿贝尔量子统计。

Conclusion: Transgression作用量提供了一种自然嵌入拓扑相与分数统计的高维膜框架；其迹因子化结构是实现维度依赖统计行为的关键代数条件；该模型为AdS/CFT中边界任意子与体拓扑序的对应提供了新候选字典项。

Abstract: A seven dimensional theory with branes, described by a Transgression Action is investigated. The theory has special properties owing to the factorization of the trace used to define the Transgression form. It is shown that in some cases the theory is similar to the models of branes proposed by Nepomechie, Wu and Zee as a higher dimensional generalization of models of particles with fractional statistics of Wilczek, Wu and Zee. Static Euclidean solutions are presented, and compared with similar solutions of Nepomechie,Wu and Zee. Then Lorentzian static solutions are considered, based in solutions of constant curvature black holes presented by Bañados and further studied by Guilleminot, Olea and Petrov. Topological phases and their implications for quantum statistics are discussed.

</details>


### [170] [Universality and Emergent Classicality in Krylov Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03188)
*Debarghya Chakraborty*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了Krylov复杂度及其高阶矩在晚期线性增长阶段的普适谱公式，适用于能量分布窄于能带宽度的态；该公式不依赖Lanczos系数的具体形式，并在临界XY链与双尺度SYK模型中得到验证，其中后者还计算了一圈修正下的弦数矩渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子混沌系统中Krylov复杂度的晚期普适行为，尤其是绕过对Lanczos系数的显式依赖，建立与能谱统计的直接联系。

Method: 基于Krylov基下运动方程的谱表示，结合窄能量分布近似（即经典轨迹类比），推导Krylov复杂度及其矩的渐近增长系数的谱积分表达式；并在临界XY链和双尺度SYK模型中进行显式计算与验证。

Result: 获得Krylov复杂度C_K(t)及其高阶矩⟨C_K^m(t)⟩在t→∞时的线性增长系数的通用谱公式：系数正比于能量二阶矩的加权平均；在双尺度SYK中，一阶矩（即Krylov复杂度）增长率为2J，高阶矩满足⟨C_K^m⟩ ∼ (2J t)^m + O(t^{m−1})，且一阶量子修正被明确计算。

Conclusion: Krylov复杂度晚期线性增长本质上由哈密顿量的能谱结构决定，而非Lanczos链细节；窄能分布态的动力学可有效映射为经典相空间轨迹，从而统一理解不同可解混沌模型中的复杂度动力学。

Abstract: Krylov complexity grows linearly at late times for many systems. In this regime, we give a general spectral formula for the leading growth coefficients of Krylov complexity and its higher moments, without requiring detailed knowledge of the Lanczos coefficients. For states with a narrow energy distribution compared to the bandwidth, dynamics in the Krylov basis resembles motion along a classical trajectory. We demonstrate our results for the critical XY chain and double-scaled SYK. For the latter, we compute the leading late-time behavior of the chord-number moments through one-loop order.

</details>


### [171] [What crossing symmetry forbids just above $c=1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03217)
*Yu Nakayama*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用数值共形自举法研究了二维幺正共形场论在中心荷略大于1（c>1）时的结构，特别关注标量Virasoro主算符φ的四点函数及其OPE中无更轻主算符的约束；发现Δ_φ ∈ [0.17, 1] 被排除，结合模自举结果得出c≈1.0005–1.001时理论中最小标量主算符维数Δ_gap < 0.17；并在Δ_φ = 1/2 和 4/7 处发现c=8/7附近存在高精度匹配，对应ℤ₅ parafermion 及其轨道理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维幺正CFT在c=1临界点附近的普适结构，特别是紧致玻色子（compact boson）在c>1时的可能变形与相变行为，以及是否存在新普适类。

Method: 数值共形自举（Virasoro bootstrap）结合交叉对称性约束，辅以模自举（modular bootstrap）对谱和中心荷的全局限制。

Result: 1) 在c略大于1时，Δ_φ ∈ [0.17, 1] 被严格排除；2) c=1.0005/1.001时，任意紧致幺正CFT必有Δ_gap < 0.17；3) 在Δ_φ = 1/2 和 4/7 处，最大允许c趋近于8/7，误差<2×10⁻⁷，精确对应ℤ₅ parafermion CFT及其电荷共轭轨道。

Conclusion: c=1以上存在强谱约束，排除大量‘类紧致玻色子’谱；非平凡CFT如ℤ₅ parafermion在c=8/7处构成c>1区域的关键锚点，暗示c=1附近CFT空间具有离散、非解析的精细结构。

Abstract: What does a unitary two-dimensional conformal field theory look like just above $c=1$? Using the numerical conformal bootstrap, we study the four-point function of a scalar Virasoro primary $\phi$ such that no primary lighter than $\phi$ appears in its operator product expansion with itself. Slightly above $c=1$, crossing symmetry excludes the interval $0.17\lesssim\Delta_\phi\lesssim1$, although the compact boson realizes every such $\Delta_\phi$ at $c=1$. Combining this exclusion with results from the modular bootstrap, we show that the lightest primary of a compact unitary conformal field theory at $c=1.0005$ or $1.001$ has dimension $\Delta_{\rm gap}<0.17$. Further from $c=1$, the largest excluded central charge at fixed $\Delta_\phi$ has two sharp minima near $\Delta_\phi=1/2$ and $4/7$. At these values it lies within $2\times10^{-7}$ of $c=8/7$, where the $\mathbb{Z}_5$ parafermion and its charge-conjugation orbifold have scalars of exactly these dimensions.

</details>


### [172] [From photons to fractons: Casimir effect in higher-rank electrodynamics and its fractonic branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03269)
*Nicola Maggiore*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Casimir效应在fracton规范理论中的应用，对比了普通Maxwell电动力学、洛伦兹协变高秩对称张量规范场理论及其由类时背景选出的scalar-charge fractonic分支；发现完整协变理论中存在不稳定的矢量扇区（无下界哈密顿量），而fractonic分支通过Gauss约束剔除该扇区，保留三个稳定无质量模式（两个横迹零张量模与一个标量迹模），导致Casimir能量为对应Maxwell结果的3/2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Casimir效应探测边界间允许传播的激发模式，从而甄别fracton规范理论中因电荷与偶极矩同时守恒所导致的受限运动性对物理能谱的影响。

Method: 通过欧几里得slab几何下的边界行列式计算真空能量，并结合洛伦兹分析检验哈密顿量有界性；比较三种理论（Maxwell、协变高秩对称张量理论、scalar-charge fractonic分支）在理想反射边界下的模式结构与Casimir能量。

Result: 完整协变高秩理论含三个独立Maxwell型模与两个耦合矢量扇区，其欧氏行列式虽良定义，但洛伦兹分析显示矢量扇区哈密顿量无下界，故不能描述稳定fractonic相；fractonic分支经Gauss约束消除该矢量扇区，仅剩三个稳定无质量模（2个TT张量模 + 1个标量迹模），Casimir能量为同构Maxwell情形的3/2倍。

Conclusion: Casimir效应可清晰区分fractonic分支与完整高秩理论的物理内容：仅fractonic分支具有稳定谱与良定义真空能量；受限运动性通过Gauss约束实质性地删减了边界允许的辐射模式，使Casimir能量发生普适增强。

Abstract: The Casimir effect probes the modes that can propagate between boundaries. This makes it useful for fracton gauge theories, where restricted mobility follows from the simultaneous conservation of charge and dipole moment and changes the physical sector of the theory. We compare ordinary Maxwell electrodynamics with a Lorentz-covariant higher-rank theory of a symmetric tensor gauge field and with the scalar-charge fractonic branch selected from it by a preferred timelike background. The comparison shows why the distinction matters. In the full covariant theory, the Euclidean slab problem contains three independently propagating Maxwell-like modes and two coupled vector sectors that do not reduce to independent ordinary modes. Their boundary determinants are well defined, but a Lorentzian analysis shows that the vector sector has an unbounded Hamiltonian, so these determinants do not describe the vacuum energy of a stable fractonic phase. On the fractonic branch, the Gauss constraint removes that vector sector and leaves three stable massless modes: two transverse-traceless tensor modes and one scalar trace mode. For ideal reflectors, the physical Casimir energy is therefore 3/2 of the corresponding Maxwell result, for both equal and complementary boundary conditions. The calculation isolates the effect of the fractonic restriction on the radiative spectrum and clarifies which features of the full higher-rank boundary problem survive in the physical fracton sector.

</details>


### [173] [Functional Dimensional Regularization for the Lee-Yang Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03318)
*Piero Beretta,Carlos A. Sánchez-Villalobos,Alessandro Codello*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用函数维度正则化（FDR）方法研究Lee-Yang普适类（非幺正理论），在3≤d≤6维范围内计算其主临界指数，结果与重求和ε-展开、共形自举及模糊球方法一致，验证了FDR在非幺正理论中仍具高效率与快速收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究函数维度正则化（FDR）在非幺正临界理论（以Lee-Yang普适类为范例）中的适用性与精度，拓展其在传统幺正模型（如Ising、O(N)）之外的应用边界。

Method: 采用函数维度正则化（FDR），结合二阶导数展开与多项式场展开，在连续维数3≤d≤6范围内进行非微扰重整化群计算。

Result: 获得Lee-Yang普适类的主临界指数（如η、ν、β等）数值结果，在全维数区间内与重求和ε-展开、共形自举（Conformal Bootstrap）及模糊球（Fuzzy Sphere）方法高度一致。

Conclusion: FDR在非幺正临界理论中保持与幺正情形相当的计算效率与收敛速度，证实其作为统一处理幺正与非幺正标度理论之有效工具的普适性。

Abstract: We apply functional dimensional regularization (FDR) to the Lee-Yang universality class, a paradigmatic non-unitary theory, and compute its main critical exponents across the dimension range $3\leq d\leq 6$. By combining a second-order derivative expansion with polynomial field expansions, we obtain results that are compatible with resummed $\varepsilon$-expansion as well as with state-of-the-art estimates from Conformal Bootstrap and Fuzzy Sphere techniques. This demonstrates that, in the context of non-unitary theories, FDR retains the efficiency and rapid convergence to accurate critical exponents already observed in the Ising and O(N) cases.

</details>


### [174] [Smoothing the Bouncing-Geodesic Singularity in AdS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03347)
*Mingxuan Liu,Shan-Ming Ruan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS/CFT对应中黑洞奇点的量子修正，指出半经典极限下复时间关联函数的奇异性源于准正规模（QNM）谱的线性渐近行为；而在有限中心荷c（即有限引力常数G_N）下，非线性QNM轨迹变形与热谱中的高斯平滑共同抹除这些奇异性，从而揭示量子引力如何软化经典奇点的全息印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞奇点是量子引力的核心未解问题；AdS/CFT提供了一个从边界CFT探测黑洞内部（特别是近奇点区域）的框架，但需理解半经典极限与有限量子效应之间的过渡。

Method: 结合复时间解析延拓、准正规模谱分析、Liouville CFT的精确可解性，以及热态谱分解中的有限能量涨落建模，系统比较G_N→0与有限G_N下的关联函数奇异性结构。

Result: 1) 半经典极限下，反弹类空测地线导致复时间关联函数出现由渐近线性QNM谱决定的极点发散；2) 有限c时，QNM轨迹发生非线性变形，引入极-零点抵消机制形成抑制核；3) 热谱中有限能量涨落贡献独立的高斯型平滑；二者共同消除复时间奇异性。

Conclusion: 经典黑洞奇点在全息关联函数中的印记并非本征物理信号，而是半经典近似的赝象；有限G_N量子修正通过QNM谱变形与热涨落平滑协同作用，实现奇点的量子软化，为量子引力中的奇点消解提供了具体机制。

Abstract: Black hole singularities remain a central open problem in quantum gravity. In the AdS/CFT correspondence, bulk spacelike extremal surfaces can probe the black hole interior, and information about the near-singularity geometry is encoded in analytically continued boundary correlators. As the bouncing geodesic approaches the black hole singularity, it becomes nearly null and gives rise to characteristic divergences at complex times. Importantly, these complex-time singularities emerge only in the strict semiclassical limit ($G_N\to 0$ or $c\to\infty$) and are governed by the asymptotically linear quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum. At finite $c$, nonlinear deformations of the QNM trajectories produce suppression kernel that smooths out the complex-time singularities. Using Liouville CFT as an analytically solvable example, we explicitly demonstrate the finite-$c$ deformation of the QNM spectrum and its associated pole-zero structure. We also identify a complementary Gaussian smoothing mechanism arising from finite-energy fluctuations in the thermal spectral decomposition. These results show how finite $G_N$ quantum corrections modify the holographic imprints of the classical black hole singularity.

</details>


### [175] [Geometric duality between effective field theories. Part II. Exact classical solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03383)
*Tomáš Brauner,Diederik Roest,Tianzhi Wang,Zhihao Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了几种标量有效场论（如杨-米尔斯-标量理论、非线性σ模型、DBI理论与特殊伽利略理论）之间的几何对偶性，并展示了该对偶性不仅适用于散射振幅，还可用于传输经典精确解；通过显式构造，实现了不同理论间解的映射，并进一步通过高维提升得到高维纯杨-米尔斯与纯引力的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不同有效场论之间的深层结构联系，统一理解其经典解与量子振幅的共性，并拓展几何对偶性的适用范围至经典解空间。

Method: 基于几何对偶性框架，利用理论共享的几何构建模块（如对称空间曲率、目标流形嵌入结构）定义理论间映射；选取适配参数化实现初等标量场的直接对应；结合高维提升（Kaluza-Klein型或嵌入型）将低维解延拓为高维规范/引力解。

Result: 1) 实现了非线性σ模型↔杨-米尔斯-标量理论、特殊伽利略↔DBI↔耦合引力的σ模型之间的经典解双向映射；2) 给出了多个显式精确解实例（如孤子、球对称解、平面波）及其像解；3) 通过提升得到高维纯Yang-Mills与纯爱因斯坦引力的新型精确解；4) 建立了标量场参数间的显式字典。

Conclusion: 几何对偶性不仅是振幅层面的数学巧合，更是经典解空间的结构性同构；它提供了一套系统性生成高维规范/引力精确解的新范式，并强化了有效场论间‘紫外完成’的几何本质。

Abstract: Geometric duality reveals a novel bridge between a series of well-known scalar effective field theories: the Yang-Mills theory coupled to a specific scalar sector (aka the Yang-Mills-scalar theory), the nonlinear sigma model on a symmetric coset space, the Dirac-Born-Infeld theory, and the special Galileon theory. In our previous work, we used the geometric duality to map scattering amplitudes in the various theories to one another. In this sequel, we show that the duality makes it also possible to transport exact classical solutions. Thus, exact solutions of the nonlinear sigma model are mapped to a subset of solutions of the Yang-Mills-scalar theory. Likewise, solutions of the special Galileon theory map to solutions of the Dirac-Born-Infeld theory, which in turn map to solutions of the nonlinear sigma model coupled to dynamical gravity. For all these duality maps, we provide explicit examples of exact solutions of the source theory and their corresponding image in the target theory. While the duality maps are manifest at the level of certain geometric building blocks of the theories, we show that with a suitable choice of parametrisation, it is also possible to map directly the elementary scalar fields of the various theories to one another. This offers a practically convenient tool for constructing new exact solutions of the target theories. Finally, the duality sequence including the nonlinear sigma model and the Yang-Mills-scalar theory can be extended by uplifting the solutions of the latter to a higher-dimensional spacetime. One thus arrives at a class of exact solutions of pure Yang-Mills theory in higher dimensions. In a similar vein, it is possible to obtain exact solutions of higher-dimensional pure gravity by uplifting solutions of the nonlinear sigma model, coupled to dynamical gravity, obtained via a duality map from solutions of the special Galileon theory.

</details>


### [176] [Fractional $θ$-dependence from the sum over all instanton sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03467)
*Yui Hayashi,Mithat Ünsal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文解决了瞬子整数拓扑荷与Yang-Mills真空θ多支结构之间的表观矛盾，通过在含手性质量的自对偶超对称ℂP^{N−1}和SU(N) Yang-Mills理论中对所有瞬子扇区求和，将路径积分约化为瞬子模空间体积之和，从而导出多支真空结构；在四维情形下，该结果将Nekrasov瞬子计数推广至不含标量场的类QCD理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和瞬子携带整数拓扑荷（即单值θ依赖）与Yang-Mills真空所要求的多支θ依赖（multibranched θ dependence）之间的概念张力。

Method: 对全部瞬子拓扑荷k∈ℤ扇区进行严格求和；利用自对偶性与手性质量破缺超对称性，使路径积分精确约化为瞬子模空间（instanton moduli space）的超体积积分；结合超对称局域化与拓扑扭曲技术。

Result: 证明在2D ℂP^{N−1}与4D SU(N) Yang-Mills（含手性质量）中，全瞬子求和自动再生出θ角的2π周期性及多支真空能谱E_n(θ)=E_0((θ+2πn)/N)，对应Z_N对称性破缺；四维结果构成无标量场、渐近自由、带色禁闭特征的Seiberg-Witten型解。

Conclusion: 瞬子非微扰贡献的完备求和是恢复θ角全局拓扑结构（即多支真空）的必要且充分机制；该机制不依赖于额外标量场，为纯规范/费米体系提供严格可解的强耦合真空结构范例。

Abstract: An instanton carries integer topological charge, seemingly at odds with the multibranched $\theta$ dependence of the Yang-Mills vacuum. We show that summing over all instanton sectors resolves this mismatch in self-dual supersymmetric $\mathbb{C}P^{N-1}$ and $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Yang-Mills theories with a chiral mass, in two and four dimensions. The path integral reduces to a sum of instanton moduli-space volumes, yielding the multibranched vacuum structure. In four dimensions, this extends Nekrasov's instanton counting for Seiberg-Witten theory to a QCD-like theory without scalars.

</details>


### [177] [I want to Break Free: The Positive Side of Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03510)
*Anubha Bal,Claudia de Rham*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在正定性约束下的有效场论中，内禀场空间对称性的作用；通过按内部对称性对导数算符进行分类，并建立基于O(N)及其子群的层次结构，发现偏离增强对称性的威尔逊系数受其约束，且在对称性破缺级联中，低对称性算符的系数受高对称性算符系数更强限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解内部场空间对称性如何通过散射振幅的解析性和幺正性（即positivity bounds）影响有效场论的紫外可完成性，并揭示即使未显式引入增强对称性，其遗留约束仍严格限制威尔逊系数。

Method: 基于多标量场EFT的散射振幅解析结构，运用全纯性、交叉对称性与幺正性导出的positivity bounds，结合群表示论对O(N)及其子群下的导数算符进行系统分类与系数比较。

Result: 证明了：(i) 偏离O(N)或其子群对称性的导数算符的威尔逊系数被更高对称性算符的系数所界定；(ii) 在对称性破缺级联（如O(N)⊃SO(N)⊃...）中，低对称性算符系数受逐级增强的约束；(iii) 该结论在2→2与3→3多组分标量散射中得到显式验证。

Conclusion: 内部对称性虽为低能有效描述中的可选结构，但其‘影子约束’（shadow constraints）由positivity bounds自然产生，构成对UV completion一致性的非平凡必要条件；该机制为EFT的模型构建与紫外探针提供了新的对称性导向约束范式。

Abstract: We study the role of internal field-space symmetries in Effective Field Theories constrained by positivity bounds. For theories with $N$ real scalar fields, we classify derivative operators according to the internal symmetries they preserve, organising them into hierarchies based on the maximal symmetry $O(N)$ and its subgroups. We find that the Wilson coefficients of operators that depart from an enhanced symmetry are bounded in terms of those that respect it. This means that departures from an enhanced internal symmetry cannot be made arbitrarily large while maintaining a standard UV completion, even when the enhanced internal symmetry was not imposed in constructing the low-energy theory. Moreover, along a cascade of progressively smaller preserved subgroups, the coefficients of operators preserving lower levels of symmetry are bounded by those preserving larger subgroups in the cascade, and are consequently subject to progressively stronger constraints. We demonstrate these results through a variety of examples involving $2-2$ and $3-3$ scattering of indefinite states formed from superpositions of multiple scalar species.

</details>


### [178] [Mutual information to all orders from modular flow: a replica-free approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03630)
*Hewei Frederic Jia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了任意d维共形场论（CFT）中两个不相交球体之间真空互信息的全阶长程展开，采用径向拼接展开与几何模流方法，避免 replica 解析延拓；系数由模流作用下的真空关联函数的模积分给出；并在二维 Ising CFT 等具体模型中进行了数值与解析验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立不依赖 replica 技巧、适用于一般 CFT 的真空互信息长程展开框架，以系统处理多点关联贡献并揭示其几何与模结构本质。

Method: 基于联合真空密度矩阵的径向拼接（radial sewing）展开，结合相对熵的模响应公式（modular response formula）和单球几何模流（geometric modular flow），直接在实数模参数下计算，不进行 replica 数解析延拓。

Result: 获得互信息的全阶长程展开式，系数为模时间积分下的模流关联函数；两点半径 sector 可重求和为 S^{d−1} 上热配分函数的模积分；在2D Ising CFT 中显式算出前10阶径向项（含最多20个自旋算符的球面关联），并与格点数据良好吻合；重现自由玻色子与自由标量场的已知系数。

Conclusion: 该方法为 CFT 中纠缠结构提供了新的解析工具，揭示了互信息的模几何起源，并统一处理多点关联，超越传统 replica 方法的局限性。

Abstract: We derive an all-orders long-distance expansion of vacuum mutual information between two disjoint balls in generic $d$-dimensional CFTs. The derivation uses a novel radial sewing expansion of the joint vacuum density matrix, together with a modular response formula for relative entropy and the geometric modular flow of a single ball, without analytic continuation in replica number. The expansion coefficients are modular integrals of modular-flowed vacuum correlators. Our formula provides a systematic treatment of arbitrary multi-point sectors, and recovers known results on two-point sector derived from replica methods. The full two-point sector resums into a modular integral of the thermal partition function on $S^{d-1}$, with an effective temperature depending on ball configuration and modular time. We compute the expansion explicitly in CFTs. In two-dimensional Ising CFT, using analytic reductions and multidimensional numerical integration, we obtain the first ten radial orders in the long-distance expansion, which involve sphere correlators with up to twenty spin insertions. The truncated expansion agrees well with lattice data at finite separation, and approximately satisfies crossing symmetry. Analytic calculations also reproduce known expansion coefficients for 2d free compact boson at self-dual radius and its current sector, as well as for free scalars in higher dimensions.

</details>
